*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  INITIALIZE_USER_TREE
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM initialize_user_tree.

  node-node_key = 'Root'.
  CLEAR node-relatkey.
  CLEAR node-relatship.
  node-hidden = ' '.
  node-disabled = ' '.
  node-isfolder = abap_true.
  CLEAR node-n_image.
  CLEAR node-exp_image.
  node-expander = abap_true.
  SELECT SINGLE * FROM tstct
         WHERE sprsl = 'EN'
           AND tcode = syst-tcode.
  node-text = tstct-ttext.
  APPEND node TO node_table.

  IF sy-tcode = 'ZIM_CM' OR
     sy-tcode = 'ZIM_MGRCM'.
    PERFORM create_user_nodes_cm.
  ELSEIF sy-tcode = 'ZIM_PY'.
    PERFORM create_user_nodes_py.
  ELSEIF sy-tcode = 'ZWC_GAO'.
    PERFORM create_user_nodes_gao.
  ELSEIF sy-tcode = 'ZIM_RPTS'.  " Assigned to SAP Support
    PERFORM create_user_nodes_rpts.
*  IF sy-tcode = 'ZIM_CSA'.
*    PERFORM create_user_nodes_csa.
*  ELSEIF sy-tcode = 'ZIM_DEP' OR
*         sy-tcode = 'ZIM_MGRDEP'.
*    PERFORM create_user_nodes_dep.
*  ELSEIF sy-tcode = 'ZIM_MDU'.
*    PERFORM create_user_nodes_mdu.
  ELSE.
    PERFORM create_user_nodes.
  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.                    " INITIALIZE_USER_TREE

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  append_tree
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM append_tree        USING p_senior
                              p_code
                              p_desc
                              p_folder
                              p_image.

  node-node_key = p_code.
  node-relatkey = p_senior.
  node-relatship = cl_gui_simple_tree=>relat_last_child.
  node-hidden = ' '.
  node-disabled = ' '.
  node-isfolder = p_folder.
  node-n_image = p_image.
  CLEAR node-exp_image.
  node-expander = p_folder.
  node-text = p_desc.
  APPEND node TO node_table.

ENDFORM.                    " append_tree

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  add_nodes_user
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM add_nodes_user.

  CALL METHOD user_tree->add_nodes
    EXPORTING
      table_structure_name           = 'MTREESNODE'
      node_table                     = node_table
    EXCEPTIONS
      failed                         = 1
      error_in_node_table            = 2
      dp_error                       = 3
      table_structure_name_not_found = 4
      OTHERS                         = 5.
  IF sy-subrc <> 0.
    MESSAGE a000.
  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.                    " add_nodes_user

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  exclfunc_append
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      -->P_1275   text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM exclfunc_append  USING    value(funktion).

  exclfunc-funktion = funktion.
  exclfunc-nachricht = space.
  APPEND exclfunc.

ENDFORM.                    " exclfunc_append

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  read_tbl_menu
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM read_tbl_menu.

*
*READ TABLE tbl_menu WITH KEY node_12 = w_node_12.
  IF sy-subrc NE 0.
    MESSAGE e999 WITH 'Select a node to process'.
  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.                    " read_tbl_menu

                                icon_change.

      PERFORM append_tree USING 'TABLE_13'
                                'TABLE_13B'
                                'Document Categories'
                                ' '
                                icon_change.

      PERFORM append_tree USING 'TABLE_13'
                                'TABLE_13C'
                                'Meetings'
                                ' '
                                icon_change.

                                ' '

    ENDIF.

    IF sy-tcode = 'ZIM_MGR'    OR

       sy-tcode = 'ZIM_MGRCS'  OR
       sy-tcode = 'ZIM_MGRDEP' OR
       sy-tcode = 'ZIM_MGRSCU'.

      PERFORM append_tree USING 'Root'

                                'Letter Maintenance'

      PERFORM append_tree USING 'LETTER'

  ENDIF.

  IF g_user_group = 'V'.

  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.                    " create_user_nodes

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  free_rec_sel
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM free_rec_sel .

  IF NOT rec_sel_container IS INITIAL.
    " destroy tree containers (detroys contained tree control, too)
    CALL METHOD rec_sel_container->free
      EXCEPTIONS
        cntl_system_error = 1
        cntl_error        = 2.
    IF sy-subrc <> 0.
      MESSAGE a000.
    ENDIF.
    CALL METHOD cl_gui_cfw=>flush
      EXCEPTIONS
        cntl_system_error = 1
        cntl_error        = 2.
    IF sy-subrc <> 0.
      MESSAGE a000.
    ENDIF.
    CLEAR rec_sel_container.
    CLEAR rec_sel_alv.
  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.                    " free_rec_sel

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  exclude_functions
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      -->P_IBR_DET_FUNCTIONS  text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM exclude_functions   TABLES  p_exclude.

  DATA ls_exclude   TYPE ui_func.

  REFRESH p_exclude.
  CLEAR   p_exclude.

  ls_exclude = cl_gui_alv_grid=>mc_fc_excl_all.
  APPEND ls_exclude TO p_exclude.

ENDFORM.                    " exclude_functions

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  EXCLUDE_FUNCTIONS_IND
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      -->P_FRLOC_SEL_FUNCTIONS  text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM exclude_functions_ind   TABLES  p_exclude.

  DATA ls_exclude   TYPE ui_func.

  REFRESH p_exclude.
  CLEAR   p_exclude.

  ls_exclude = cl_gui_alv_grid=>mc_fc_graph.
  APPEND ls_exclude TO p_exclude.

  ls_exclude = cl_gui_alv_grid=>mc_fc_loc_append_row.
  APPEND ls_exclude TO p_exclude.

  ls_exclude = cl_gui_alv_grid=>mc_fc_loc_copy.
  APPEND ls_exclude TO p_exclude.

  ls_exclude = cl_gui_alv_grid=>mc_fc_loc_copy_row.
  APPEND ls_exclude TO p_exclude.

  ls_exclude = cl_gui_alv_grid=>mc_fc_loc_cut.
  APPEND ls_exclude TO p_exclude.

  ls_exclude = cl_gui_alv_grid=>mc_fc_loc_insert_row.
  APPEND ls_exclude TO p_exclude.

  ls_exclude = cl_gui_alv_grid=>mc_fc_loc_delete_row.
  APPEND ls_exclude TO p_exclude.

  ls_exclude = cl_gui_alv_grid=>mc_fc_loc_move_row.
  APPEND ls_exclude TO p_exclude.

  ls_exclude = cl_gui_alv_grid=>mc_fc_loc_paste.
  APPEND ls_exclude TO p_exclude.

  ls_exclude = cl_gui_alv_grid=>mc_fc_loc_paste_new_row.
  APPEND ls_exclude TO p_exclude.

  ls_exclude = cl_gui_alv_grid=>mc_fc_subtot.
  APPEND ls_exclude TO p_exclude.

  ls_exclude = cl_gui_alv_grid=>mc_mb_sum.
  APPEND ls_exclude TO p_exclude.

ENDFORM.                    " EXCLUDE_FUNCTIONS_IND

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  BUILD_FIELDCAT_INC_INJ
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      -->P_FRLOC_SEL_FIELDCAT  text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM build_fieldcat_inc_inj TABLES  p_fieldcat STRUCTURE lvc_s_fcat.

  DATA: l_col        TYPE i,
         l_text(20)   TYPE c.

  REFRESH p_fieldcat.
  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  l_col = 0.


  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'LOCATION_DESC'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Location of Injury'.
  rec_fieldcat-just          = 'L'.
  rec_fieldcat-outputlen     = 40.
  rec_fieldcat-lowercase     = abap_true.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'OTHER_LOCATION'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Other Location'.
  rec_fieldcat-just          = 'L'.
  rec_fieldcat-outputlen     = 40.
  rec_fieldcat-lowercase     = abap_true.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'NATURE_DESC'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Nature of Injury'.
  rec_fieldcat-just          = 'L'.
  rec_fieldcat-outputlen     = 40.
  rec_fieldcat-lowercase     = abap_true.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'OTHER_NATURE'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Other Nature'.
  rec_fieldcat-just          = 'L'.
  rec_fieldcat-outputlen     = 40.
  rec_fieldcat-lowercase     = abap_true.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'MAIN_INJURY'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Is Main Injury'.
  rec_fieldcat-checkbox      = abap_true.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

ENDFORM.                    " BUILD_FIELDCAT_INC_INJ

  rec_fieldcat-just          = 'L'.

  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  rec_fieldcat-just          = 'L'.

  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'PRD'.
  rec_fieldcat-just          = 'C'.
  rec_fieldcat-lowercase     = abap_true.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  BUILD_FIELDCAT_MED_PRAC
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      -->P_FRLOC_SEL_FIELDCAT  text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM build_fieldcat_med_prac TABLES  p_fieldcat STRUCTURE lvc_s_fcat.

  DATA: l_col        TYPE i,
         l_text(20)   TYPE c.

  REFRESH p_fieldcat.
  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  l_col = 0.


  IF w_2100_action NE 'DISP' AND
     w_2200_action NE 'DISP'.
    CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
    ADD 1 TO l_col.
    rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'INS_ICON'.
    rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
    rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Create'.
    rec_fieldcat-icon          = abap_true.
    rec_fieldcat-style         = cl_gui_alv_grid=>mc_style_button.
    rec_fieldcat-outputlen     = 9.
    APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

    CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
    ADD 1 TO l_col.
    rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'UPD_ICON'.
    rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
    rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Update'.
    rec_fieldcat-icon          = abap_true.
    rec_fieldcat-style         = cl_gui_alv_grid=>mc_style_button.
    rec_fieldcat-outputlen     = 9.
    APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.
  ENDIF.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'NTD'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'NTD'.
  rec_fieldcat-checkbox      = abap_true.
  IF w_2100_action NE 'DISP' AND
     w_2200_action NE 'DISP'.
    rec_fieldcat-edit        = abap_true.
  ENDIF.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'NTS'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'NTS'.
  rec_fieldcat-checkbox      = abap_true.
  IF w_2100_action NE 'DISP' AND
     w_2200_action NE 'DISP'.
    rec_fieldcat-edit        = abap_true.
  ENDIF.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-ref_table     = 'ZIM_IN_MED'.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'MED_PRAC'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Health Professional'.
  IF w_2100_action NE 'DISP' AND
     w_2200_action NE 'DISP'.
    rec_fieldcat-edit        = abap_true.
  ENDIF.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-ref_table     = 'ZIM_MED'.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'NAME'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Name'.
  rec_fieldcat-lowercase     = abap_true.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-ref_table     = 'ZIM_MED'.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'PHONE'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Phone No.'.
  rec_fieldcat-lowercase     = abap_true.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-ref_table     = 'ZIM_MED'.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'FAX'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Fax No.'.
  rec_fieldcat-lowercase     = abap_true.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-ref_table     = 'ZIM_MED_PROFT'.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'HEALTH_DESC'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Health Profession'.
  rec_fieldcat-lowercase     = abap_true.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-ref_table     = 'ZIM_MED'.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'EMAIL'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'E-Mail'.
  rec_fieldcat-lowercase     = abap_true.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

ENDFORM.                    " BUILD_FIELDCAT_MED_PRAC

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM build_fieldcat_init_con TABLES  p_fieldcat STRUCTURE lvc_s_fcat.

  DATA: l_col        TYPE i,
         l_text(20)   TYPE c.

  REFRESH p_fieldcat.
  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  l_col = 0.


  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-ref_table     = 'ZIM_IN_ACT'.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'CONT_ACT_COMP'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Action Performed'.
  rec_fieldcat-checkbox      = abap_true.
  rec_fieldcat-outputlen     = 16.
  IF w_8100_action NE 'DISP'.
    rec_fieldcat-edit          = abap_true.
  ENDIF.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-ref_table     = 'ZIM_ACTION'.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'CONT_ACT_DESC'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Action/Activity'.
  rec_fieldcat-lowercase     = abap_true.
  rec_fieldcat-outputlen     = 80.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'NOTES_ICON'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Notes'.
  rec_fieldcat-icon          = abap_true.
  rec_fieldcat-style         = cl_gui_alv_grid=>mc_style_button.
  rec_fieldcat-outputlen     = 9.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

*&      Form  BUILD_FIELDCAT_SERV_REQ
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      -->P_FRLOC_SEL_FIELDCAT  text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM build_fieldcat_serv_req TABLES  p_fieldcat STRUCTURE lvc_s_fcat.

  DATA: l_col        TYPE i,
         l_text(20)   TYPE c.

  REFRESH p_fieldcat.
  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  l_col = 0.

  IF w_request = 'E'.
    CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
    ADD 1 TO l_col.
    rec_fieldcat-ref_table     = 'ZIM_IN_PROV'.
    rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'DATE_REQ'.
    rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
    rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Date Req'.
    rec_fieldcat-just          = 'C'.
    APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

    CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
    ADD 1 TO l_col.
    rec_fieldcat-ref_table     = 'ZIM_IN_PROV'.
    rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'PROV_CODE'.
    rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
    rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Provider'.
    rec_fieldcat-hotspot       = abap_true.
    APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

    CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
    ADD 1 TO l_col.
    rec_fieldcat-ref_table     = 'ZIM_PROV'.
    rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'COMPANY'.
    rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
    rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Company'.
    rec_fieldcat-lowercase     = abap_true.
    APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

    CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
    ADD 1 TO l_col.
    rec_fieldcat-ref_table     = 'ZIM_PROV'.
    rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'CONSULTANT'.
    rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
    rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Consultant'.
    rec_fieldcat-lowercase     = abap_true.
    APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

    CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
    ADD 1 TO l_col.
    rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'ACTIVITY_DESC'.
    rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
    rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Activity'.
    rec_fieldcat-lowercase     = abap_true.
    APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

    CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
    ADD 1 TO l_col.
    rec_fieldcat-ref_table     = 'ZIM_PROV'.
    rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'PHONE'.
    rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
    rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Phone'.
    rec_fieldcat-lowercase     = abap_true.
    APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

    CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
    ADD 1 TO l_col.
    rec_fieldcat-ref_table     = 'ZIM_PROV'.
    rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'FAX'.
    rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
    rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Fax'.
    rec_fieldcat-lowercase     = abap_true.
    APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

    CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
    ADD 1 TO l_col.
    rec_fieldcat-ref_table     = 'ZIM_PROV'.
    rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'MOBILE'.
    rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
    rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Mobile'.
    rec_fieldcat-lowercase     = abap_true.
    APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

    CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
    ADD 1 TO l_col.
    rec_fieldcat-ref_table     = 'ZIM_PROV'.
    rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'EMAIL'.
    rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
    rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Email'.
    rec_fieldcat-lowercase     = abap_true.
    APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

    CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
    ADD 1 TO l_col.
    rec_fieldcat-ref_table     = 'ZIM_IN_PROV'.
    rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'REF_SENT'.
    rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
    rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Referral Sent'.
    rec_fieldcat-checkbox      = abap_true.
    IF w_3100_action NE 'DISP'.
      rec_fieldcat-edit          = abap_true.
    ENDIF.
    APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  ELSEIF w_request = 'I'.
    CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
    ADD 1 TO l_col.
    rec_fieldcat-ref_table     = 'ZIM_IN_IMEP'.
    rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'REF_MGMT'.
    rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
    rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Ref to Mgmt'.
    rec_fieldcat-checkbox      = abap_true.
    APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

    CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
    ADD 1 TO l_col.
    rec_fieldcat-ref_table     = 'ZIM_IN_IMEP'.
    rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'APP_MGMT'.
    rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
    rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'App by Mgmt'.
    rec_fieldcat-checkbox      = abap_true.
    IF sy-tcode = 'ZIM_MGR' AND
       w_3100_action NE 'DISP'.
      rec_fieldcat-edit          = abap_true.
    ENDIF.
    APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

    CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
    ADD 1 TO l_col.
    rec_fieldcat-ref_table     = 'ZIM_IN_IMEP'.
    rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'REPORT_REC'.
    rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
    rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Rpt Recvd'.
    rec_fieldcat-checkbox      = abap_true.
    IF w_3100_action NE 'DISP'.
      rec_fieldcat-edit          = abap_true.
    ENDIF.
    APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

    CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
    ADD 1 TO l_col.
    rec_fieldcat-ref_table     = 'ZIM_IN_IMEP'.
    rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'REPORT_SENT'.
    rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
    rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Rpt Sent'.
    rec_fieldcat-checkbox      = abap_true.
    IF w_3100_action NE 'DISP'.
      rec_fieldcat-edit          = abap_true.
    ENDIF.
    APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

    CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
    ADD 1 TO l_col.
    rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'IMEP_ICON'.
    rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
    rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Recommendation'.
    rec_fieldcat-icon          = abap_true.
    rec_fieldcat-style         = cl_gui_alv_grid=>mc_style_button.
    rec_fieldcat-outputlen     = 14.
    APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  ELSEIF w_request = 'R'.
    CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
    ADD 1 TO l_col.
    rec_fieldcat-ref_table     = 'ZIM_IN_ROSA'.
    rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'REQ_DATE'.
    rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
    rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Request Date'.
    rec_fieldcat-just          = 'C'.
    APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

    CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
    ADD 1 TO l_col.
    rec_fieldcat-ref_table     = 'ZIM_IN_ROSA'.
    rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'REF_DATE'.
    rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
    rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Referral Date'.
    IF w_3100_action NE 'DISP'.
      rec_fieldcat-edit          = abap_true.
    ENDIF.
    rec_fieldcat-just          = 'C'.
    APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

    CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
    ADD 1 TO l_col.
    rec_fieldcat-ref_table     = 'ZIM_IN_ROSA'.
    rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'REC_DATE'.
    rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
    rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Receipt Date'.
    IF w_3100_action NE 'DISP'.
      rec_fieldcat-edit          = abap_true.
    ENDIF.
    rec_fieldcat-just          = 'C'.
    APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

    CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
    ADD 1 TO l_col.
    rec_fieldcat-ref_table     = 'ZIM_IN_ROSA'.
    rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'STATUS'.
    rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
    rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Status'.
    IF w_3100_action NE 'DISP'.
      rec_fieldcat-edit          = abap_true.
    ENDIF.
    rec_fieldcat-just          = 'C'.
    APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

    CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
    ADD 1 TO l_col.
    rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'STATUS_DESC'.
    rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
    rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Status Desc'.
    rec_fieldcat-lowercase     = abap_true.
    APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

    CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
    ADD 1 TO l_col.
    rec_fieldcat-ref_table     = 'ZIM_IN_ROSA'.
    rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'REVIEW_DATE'.
    rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
    rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Review Date'.
    IF w_3100_action NE 'DISP'.
      rec_fieldcat-edit          = abap_true.
    ENDIF.
    rec_fieldcat-just          = 'C'.
    APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  ELSEIF w_request = 'P'.
    CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
    ADD 1 TO l_col.
    rec_fieldcat-ref_table     = 'ZIM_IN_PMO'.
    rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'REF_DATE'.
    rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
    rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Referral Date'.
    IF w_3100_action NE 'DISP'.
      rec_fieldcat-edit          = abap_true.
    ENDIF.
    rec_fieldcat-just          = 'C'.
    APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

    CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
    ADD 1 TO l_col.
    rec_fieldcat-ref_table     = 'ZIM_IN_PMO'.
    rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'REC_DATE'.
    rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
    rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Receipt Date'.
    IF w_3100_action NE 'DISP'.
      rec_fieldcat-edit          = abap_true.
    ENDIF.
    rec_fieldcat-just          = 'C'.
    APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

    CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
    ADD 1 TO l_col.
    rec_fieldcat-ref_table     = 'ZIM_IN_PMO'.
    rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'STATUS'.
    rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
    rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Status'.
    IF w_3100_action NE 'DISP'.
      rec_fieldcat-edit          = abap_true.
    ENDIF.
    rec_fieldcat-just          = 'C'.
    APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

    CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
    ADD 1 TO l_col.
    rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'STATUS_DESC'.
    rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
    rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Status Desc'.
    rec_fieldcat-lowercase     = abap_true.
    APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

    CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
    ADD 1 TO l_col.
    rec_fieldcat-ref_table     = 'ZIM_IN_PMO'.
    rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'REVIEW_DATE'.
    rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
    rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Review Date'.
    IF w_3100_action NE 'DISP'.
      rec_fieldcat-edit          = abap_true.
    ENDIF.
    rec_fieldcat-just          = 'C'.
    APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  ENDIF.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'DESC_ICON'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  IF w_request = 'E'.
    rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Email Text'.
  ELSE.
    rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Comments'.
  ENDIF.
  rec_fieldcat-icon          = abap_true.
  rec_fieldcat-style         = cl_gui_alv_grid=>mc_style_button.
  rec_fieldcat-outputlen     = 11.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  IF w_request NE 'P' AND
     w_3100_action NE 'DISP'.
    CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
    ADD 1 TO l_col.
    rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'EMAIL_ICON'.
    rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
    rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Resend Email'.
    rec_fieldcat-icon          = abap_true.
    rec_fieldcat-style         = cl_gui_alv_grid=>mc_style_button.
    rec_fieldcat-outputlen     = 12.
    APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.
  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.                    " BUILD_FIELDCAT_SERV_REQ


*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  BUILD_FIELDCAT_REHAB_REQ

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM build_fieldcat_rehab_req TABLES  p_fieldcat STRUCTURE lvc_s_fcat.

  DATA: l_col        TYPE i,
         l_text(20)   TYPE c.

  REFRESH p_fieldcat.
  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  l_col = 0.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-ref_table     = 'ZIM_IN_REHAB'.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'SEQ_NO'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Placement No.'.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-ref_table     = 'ZIM_IN_REHAB'.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'ORGEH'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Org Unit'.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'ORGEH_DESC'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Org Description'.
  rec_fieldcat-lowercase     = abap_true.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-ref_table     = 'ZIM_IN_REHAB'.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'BEGDA'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Start Date'.
  rec_fieldcat-just          = 'C'.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-ref_table     = 'ZIM_IN_REHAB'.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'ENDDA'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'End Date'.
  rec_fieldcat-just          = 'C'.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-ref_table     = 'ZIM_IN_REHAB'.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'REVIEW_DATE'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Review Date'.
  IF w_3100_action NE 'DISP'.
    rec_fieldcat-edit        = abap_true.
  ENDIF.
  rec_fieldcat-just          = 'C'.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-ref_table     = 'ZIM_IN_REHAB'.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'COMPLETE'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Complete'.
  IF w_3100_action NE 'DISP'.
    rec_fieldcat-edit        = abap_true.
  ENDIF.
  rec_fieldcat-checkbox      = abap_true.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'DESC_ICON'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Comments'.
  rec_fieldcat-icon          = abap_true.
  rec_fieldcat-style         = cl_gui_alv_grid=>mc_style_button.
  rec_fieldcat-outputlen     = 11.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  IF w_3100_action NE 'DISP'.
    CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
    ADD 1 TO l_col.
    rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'EMAIL_ICON'.
    rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
    rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Resend Email'.
    rec_fieldcat-icon          = abap_true.
    rec_fieldcat-style         = cl_gui_alv_grid=>mc_style_button.
    rec_fieldcat-outputlen     = 12.
    APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.
  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.                    " BUILD_FIELDCAT_REHAB_REQ


*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  BUILD_FIELDCAT_ERG_ASS
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      -->P_FRLOC_SEL_FIELDCAT  text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM build_fieldcat_erg_ass TABLES  p_fieldcat STRUCTURE lvc_s_fcat.

  DATA: l_col        TYPE i,
         l_text(20)   TYPE c.

  REFRESH p_fieldcat.
  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  l_col = 0.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-ref_table     = 'ZIM_IN_ERG'.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'INCIDENT'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Incident No.'.
  rec_fieldcat-just          = 'L'.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-ref_table     = 'ZIM_IN_ERG'.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'ERG_SENT_DATE'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Date Created'.
  rec_fieldcat-just          = 'C'.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-ref_table     = 'ZIM_IN_ERG'.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'ERG_REC_DATE'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Received Date'.
  rec_fieldcat-just          = 'C'.
  IF w_2100_action NE 'DISP' AND
     w_3100_action NE 'DISP'.
    rec_fieldcat-edit        = abap_true.
  ENDIF.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-ref_table     = 'ZIM_IN_ERG'.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'INSPECT_REF'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Referral Date'.
  rec_fieldcat-just          = 'C'.
  IF w_2100_action NE 'DISP' AND
     w_3100_action NE 'DISP'.
    rec_fieldcat-edit        = abap_true.
  ENDIF.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'DESC_ICON'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Comments'.
  rec_fieldcat-icon          = abap_true.
  rec_fieldcat-style         = cl_gui_alv_grid=>mc_style_button.
  rec_fieldcat-outputlen     = 11.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  IF w_2100_action NE 'DISP' AND
     w_3100_action NE 'DISP'.
    CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
    ADD 1 TO l_col.
    rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'EMAIL_ICON'.
    rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
    rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Resend Email'.
    rec_fieldcat-icon          = abap_true.
    rec_fieldcat-style         = cl_gui_alv_grid=>mc_style_button.
    rec_fieldcat-outputlen     = 12.
    APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

    CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
    ADD 1 TO l_col.
    rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'IMP_ICON1'.
    rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
    rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Attach Checklist'.
    rec_fieldcat-icon          = abap_true.
    rec_fieldcat-style         = cl_gui_alv_grid=>mc_style_button.
    rec_fieldcat-outputlen     = 18.
    APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.
  ENDIF.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'DISP_ICON1'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Display Checklist'.
  rec_fieldcat-icon          = abap_true.
  rec_fieldcat-style         = cl_gui_alv_grid=>mc_style_button.
  rec_fieldcat-outputlen     = 18.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.


ENDFORM.                    " BUILD_FIELDCAT_ERG_ASS

  rec_fieldcat-just          = 'C'.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-ref_table     = 'ZIM_IN_ERG'.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'INSPECT_REF'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Referral Date'.
  rec_fieldcat-just          = 'C'.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-ref_table     = 'ZIM_IN_ERG'.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'INSPECT_EMP'.
  rec_fieldcat-f4availabl    = abap_true.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Inspector'.
  rec_fieldcat-edit          = abap_true.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-ref_table     = 'PA0001'.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'ENAME'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Name'.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-ref_table     = 'ZIM_IN_ERG'.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'INSPECT_COMP'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Completion Date'.
  rec_fieldcat-edit          = abap_true.
  rec_fieldcat-just          = 'C'.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'DESC_ICON'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Comments'.
  rec_fieldcat-icon          = abap_true.
  rec_fieldcat-style         = cl_gui_alv_grid=>mc_style_button.
  rec_fieldcat-outputlen     = 11.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'IMP_ICON'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Document'.
  rec_fieldcat-icon          = abap_true.
  rec_fieldcat-style         = cl_gui_alv_grid=>mc_style_button.
  rec_fieldcat-outputlen     = 11.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

ENDFORM.                    " BUILD_FIELDCAT_ERG_ASS_2

  rec_fieldcat-lowercase     = abap_true.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

     w_3200_action NE 'DISP'.
    CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
    ADD 1 TO l_col.
    rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'COPY_ICON'.
    rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
    rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Copy'.
    rec_fieldcat-icon          = abap_true.
    rec_fieldcat-style         = cl_gui_alv_grid=>mc_style_button.
    rec_fieldcat-outputlen     = 11.
    APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.
  ENDIF.

  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.

  rec_fieldcat-just          = 'C'.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.

  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.

  rec_fieldcat-fix_column    = abap_true.

  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.

  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.

  rec_fieldcat-ref_table     = 'ZIM_IN_REM'.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'ACTION2'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Comments'.
  rec_fieldcat-lowercase     = abap_true.
  rec_fieldcat-edit          = abap_true.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

* STart of CR3197 DEVK9HRFUN
  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'IMA'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'IMA'.
*  rec_fieldcat-fix_column    = abap_true.
*  rec_fieldcat-f4availabl    = abap_true.
  rec_fieldcat-just          = 'C'.

  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.

*  rec_fieldcat-f4availabl    = abap_true.
  rec_fieldcat-just          = 'L'.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.
* End of CR3197 DEVK9HRFUN

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'ASS_TO_DESC'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.

  rec_fieldcat-just          = 'C'.

  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.

  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.

  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-ref_table     = 'ZIM_IN_CERT'.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'MC_DATE'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Cert Date'.
  rec_fieldcat-just          = 'C'.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'STATUS_DESC'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Status'.

  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.

  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'DATE_FROM'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Status From'.
  rec_fieldcat-just          = 'C'.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-ref_table     = 'ZIM_IN_CERT'.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'DATE_TO'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Status To'.
  rec_fieldcat-just          = 'C'.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-ref_table     = 'ZIM_IN_CERT'.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'CAP_HOURS1'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Hours/Day'.
  rec_fieldcat-just          = 'C'.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-ref_table     = 'ZIM_IN_CERT'.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'CAP_DAYS1'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Days/Week'.
  rec_fieldcat-just          = 'C'.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'CAT_DESC'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Category'.
  rec_fieldcat-lowercase     = abap_true.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'DIAGNOSIS'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Diagnosis'.
  rec_fieldcat-just          = 'L'.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'UPD_ICON'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  IF w_3100_action NE 'DISP'.
    rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Update'.
  ELSE.
    rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Display'.
  ENDIF.
  rec_fieldcat-icon          = abap_true.
  rec_fieldcat-style         = cl_gui_alv_grid=>mc_style_button.
  rec_fieldcat-outputlen     = 11.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  IF w_3100_action NE 'DISP'.
    CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
    ADD 1 TO l_col.
    rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'COPY_ICON'.
    rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
    rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Copy'.
    rec_fieldcat-icon          = abap_true.
    rec_fieldcat-style         = cl_gui_alv_grid=>mc_style_button.
    rec_fieldcat-outputlen     = 11.
    APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.
  ENDIF.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'DOC_ICON'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Display'.
  rec_fieldcat-icon          = abap_true.
  rec_fieldcat-style         = cl_gui_alv_grid=>mc_style_button.
  rec_fieldcat-outputlen     = 11.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  IF w_3400_action NE 'DISP'  AND
     zim_in_mast-hod IS INITIAL.
    IF sy-tcode NE 'ZWC_GAO'.
*      CHECK zim_in_cm-def_claim = abap_true.
      CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
      ADD 1 TO l_col.
      rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'GEN_ICON'.
      rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
      rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Upd P905'.
      rec_fieldcat-icon          = abap_true.
      rec_fieldcat-style         = cl_gui_alv_grid=>mc_style_button.
      rec_fieldcat-outputlen     = 11.
      APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.
    ENDIF.

    CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
    ADD 1 TO l_col.
    rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'EMAIL_ICON'.
    rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
    rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Send to EML'.
    rec_fieldcat-icon          = abap_true.
    rec_fieldcat-style         = cl_gui_alv_grid=>mc_style_button.
    rec_fieldcat-outputlen     = 11.
    APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

    IF sy-tcode = 'ZIM_CM'     OR
       sy-tcode = 'ZIM_MGRCM'  OR
       sy-tcode = 'ZWC_GAO'.
      CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
      ADD 1 TO l_col.
      rec_fieldcat-ref_table     = 'ZIM_IN_CERT'.
      rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'MC_SENT'.
      rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
      rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Sent Date'.
      rec_fieldcat-just          = 'C'.
      APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

    ENDIF.

  ENDIF.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-ref_table     = 'ZIM_IN_CERT'.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'INACTIVE'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Inactive'.
  rec_fieldcat-checkbox      = abap_true.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'CERT_CODE'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'MC'.
  rec_fieldcat-checkbox      = abap_true.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.


ENDFORM.                    " BUILD_FIELDCAT_MC_SEL

  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'STATUS_DESC'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Status'.
  rec_fieldcat-lowercase     = abap_true.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-ref_table     = 'ZIM_IN_CERT'.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'DATE_FROM'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Status From'.
  rec_fieldcat-just          = 'C'.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-ref_table     = 'ZIM_IN_CERT'.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'DATE_TO'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Status To'.
  rec_fieldcat-just          = 'C'.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-ref_table     = 'ZIM_IN_CERT'.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'CAP_HOURS1'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Hours/Day'.
  rec_fieldcat-just          = 'C'.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-ref_table     = 'ZIM_IN_CERT'.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'CAP_DAYS1'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Days/Week'.
  rec_fieldcat-just          = 'C'.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'CAT_DESC'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Category'.
  rec_fieldcat-lowercase     = abap_true.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'DIAGNOSIS'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Diagnosis'.
  rec_fieldcat-just          = 'L'.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'UPD_ICON'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  IF w_2200_action = 'DISP' OR
     w_3600_action = 'DISP'.
    rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Display'.
  ELSE.
    rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Update'.
  ENDIF.
  rec_fieldcat-icon          = abap_true.
  rec_fieldcat-style         = cl_gui_alv_grid=>mc_style_button.
  rec_fieldcat-outputlen     = 11.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  IF w_2200_action NE 'DISP' AND
     w_3600_action NE 'DISP'.
    CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
    ADD 1 TO l_col.
    rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'COPY_ICON'.
    rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
    rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Copy'.
    rec_fieldcat-icon          = abap_true.
    rec_fieldcat-style         = cl_gui_alv_grid=>mc_style_button.
    rec_fieldcat-outputlen     = 11.
    APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.
  ENDIF.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'DOC_ICON'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Display'.
  rec_fieldcat-icon          = abap_true.
  rec_fieldcat-style         = cl_gui_alv_grid=>mc_style_button.
  rec_fieldcat-outputlen     = 11.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-ref_table     = 'ZIM_IN_CERT'.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'INACTIVE'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Inactive'.
  rec_fieldcat-checkbox      = abap_true.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'CERT_CODE'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'MC'.
  rec_fieldcat-checkbox      = abap_true.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

    rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
    rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Upd P905'.
    rec_fieldcat-icon          = abap_true.
    rec_fieldcat-style         = cl_gui_alv_grid=>mc_style_button.
    rec_fieldcat-outputlen     = 11.
    APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.
  ENDIF.

  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-ref_table     = 'ZIM_IN_EPSS_EARN'.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'BEGDA'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Date From'.
  rec_fieldcat-just          = 'C'.
  rec_fieldcat-dd_outlen     = 10.
  rec_fieldcat-outputlen     = 10.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-ref_table     = 'ZIM_IN_EPSS_EARN'.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'ENDDA'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Date To'.
  rec_fieldcat-just          = 'C'.
  rec_fieldcat-dd_outlen     = 10.
  rec_fieldcat-outputlen     = 10.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-ref_table     = 'ZIM_IN_EPSS_EARN'.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'EARN_AMOUNT'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Weekly Amount'.
  rec_fieldcat-just          = 'R'.
  rec_fieldcat-dd_outlen     = 20.
  rec_fieldcat-outputlen     = 20.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  IF w_3500_action NE 'DISP'.
    CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
    ADD 1 TO l_col.
    rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'IMP_ICON'.
    rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
    rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Import'.
    rec_fieldcat-icon          = abap_true.
    rec_fieldcat-style         = cl_gui_alv_grid=>mc_style_button.
    rec_fieldcat-outputlen     = 11.
    APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.
  ENDIF.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'DOC_ICON'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Display'.
  rec_fieldcat-icon          = abap_true.
  rec_fieldcat-style         = cl_gui_alv_grid=>mc_style_button.
  rec_fieldcat-outputlen     = 11.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.

  rec_fieldcat-ref_table     = 'ZIM_IN_EPSS_EARN'.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'DESCRIPTION'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.

  rec_fieldcat-just          = 'L'.
  rec_fieldcat-dd_outlen     = 80.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.

  rec_fieldcat-just          = 'C'.

  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-ref_table     = 'ZIM_IN_EPSS_EARN'.

  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Last Modified By'.
  rec_fieldcat-just          = 'C'.
  rec_fieldcat-dd_outlen     = 18.

  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.

  rec_fieldcat-ref_table     = 'ZIM_IN_EPSS_EARN'.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'UPD_DATE'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Last Modified On'.
  rec_fieldcat-just          = 'C'.
  rec_fieldcat-dd_outlen     = 18.

  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.

  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.

  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.

  rec_fieldcat-ref_table     = 'ZIM_RTWP'.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'DATE_TO'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Date To'.
  rec_fieldcat-just          = 'C'.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-ref_table     = 'ZIM_RTWP'.

  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.

  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.

  rec_fieldcat-ref_table     = 'ZIM_RTWP'.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'SUP_AGREED'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Employer'.
  rec_fieldcat-checkbox      = abap_true.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-ref_table     = 'ZIM_RTWP'.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'NTD_AGREED'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.

  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Other'.
  rec_fieldcat-checkbox      = abap_true.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'UPD_ICON'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  IF w_3100_action = 'DISP' OR
     w_3300_action = 'DISP'.
    rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Display'.
  ELSE.
    rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Update'.
  ENDIF.
  rec_fieldcat-icon          = abap_true.
  rec_fieldcat-style         = cl_gui_alv_grid=>mc_style_button.
  rec_fieldcat-outputlen     = 11.

     w_3300_action = 'DISP'.
  ELSE.

    ADD 1 TO l_col.
    rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'COPY_ICON'.
    rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
    rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Copy'.
    rec_fieldcat-icon          = abap_true.
    rec_fieldcat-style         = cl_gui_alv_grid=>mc_style_button.
    rec_fieldcat-outputlen     = 11.
    APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

    CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
    ADD 1 TO l_col.
    rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'CONV_ICON'.
    rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
    rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Convert'.
    rec_fieldcat-icon          = abap_true.
    rec_fieldcat-style         = cl_gui_alv_grid=>mc_style_button.

    APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

    CLEAR rec_fieldcat.

    rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'EMAIL_ICON'.
    rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
    rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Send R@WP'.
    rec_fieldcat-icon          = abap_true.
    rec_fieldcat-style         = cl_gui_alv_grid=>mc_style_button.
    rec_fieldcat-outputlen     = 11.
    APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.
  ENDIF.

  DATA: l_col        TYPE i,

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-ref_table     = 'ZIM_RECOUP_DOC'.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'DATE_PROCESSED'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Date Processed'.
  rec_fieldcat-just          = 'C'.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-ref_table     = 'ZIM_RECOUP_DOC'.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'BEGDA'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Claim From'.
  rec_fieldcat-just          = 'C'.
  rec_fieldcat-hotspot       = abap_true.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-ref_table     = 'ZIM_RECOUP_DOC'.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'ENDDA'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Claim To'.
  rec_fieldcat-just          = 'C'.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

*  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
*  ADD 1 TO l_col.
*  rec_fieldcat-ref_table     = 'ZIM_RECOUP_DOC'.
*  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'DATE_REVERSED'.
*  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
*  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Date Reversed'.
*  rec_fieldcat-just          = 'C'.
*  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.
*
*  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
*  ADD 1 TO l_col.
*  rec_fieldcat-ref_table     = 'ZIM_RECOUP_DOC'.
*  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'RESUBMIT'.
*  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
*  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Re-Submit'.
*  rec_fieldcat-just          = 'C'.
*  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-ref_table     = 'ZIM_RECOUP_DOC'.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'RECEIPT_DATE'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.

  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'BELNR'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Document No.'.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-ref_table     = 'ZIM_RECOUP_DOC'.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'WC_AMT_RECOUP'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Recoup Amount'.
  rec_fieldcat-just          = 'R'.
  rec_fieldcat-do_sum        = abap_true.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'IMEP Date'.
  rec_fieldcat-just          = 'C'.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-ref_table     = 'ZIM_IN_IMEP'.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'REF_SIMA'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Referred to SIMA'.
  rec_fieldcat-checkbox      = abap_true.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'SIMA_STAT'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'SIMA Status'.
  rec_fieldcat-just          = 'L'.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

*  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.

  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'REC_WORK_DESC'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Recommended Work Status'.
  rec_fieldcat-just          = 'L'.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'UPD_ICON'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  IF w_3300_action NE 'DISP'.
    rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Update'.
  ELSE.
    rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Display'.
  ENDIF.

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  BUILD_FIELDCAT_FTC_SEL
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      -->P_FRLOC_SEL_FIELDCAT  text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM build_fieldcat_ftc_sel TABLES  p_fieldcat STRUCTURE lvc_s_fcat.

  DATA: l_col        TYPE i,
         l_text(20)   TYPE c.

  REFRESH p_fieldcat.
  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  l_col = 0.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-ref_table     = 'ZIM_IN_MAST'.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'FTCO_DATE'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'FTCO Date'.
  rec_fieldcat-just          = 'C'.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  IF zim_in_cm-unsworn_claim IS INITIAL.
    CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
    ADD 1 TO l_col.
    rec_fieldcat-ref_table     = 'ZIM_IN_FTC'.
    rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'MD_RPT_COMP'.
    rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
    rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Report Complete'.
    rec_fieldcat-checkbox      = abap_true.
    APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

    CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
    ADD 1 TO l_col.
    rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'TL_STAT'.
    rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
    rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'TL Status'.
    rec_fieldcat-just          = 'L'.
    rec_fieldcat-lowercase     = abap_true.
    APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

    CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
    ADD 1 TO l_col.
    rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'APP_COM_DATE'.
    rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
    rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Signed Submission Date'.
    rec_fieldcat-just          = 'C'.
    APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.
  ELSE.
    CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
    ADD 1 TO l_col.
    rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'ADMIN_LTR_RCVD'.
    rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
    rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Waiver Form Received'.
    rec_fieldcat-checkbox      = abap_true.
    APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.
  ENDIF.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'CASE_STAT'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Case Status'.
  rec_fieldcat-just          = 'L'.
  rec_fieldcat-lowercase     = abap_true.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'UPD_ICON'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  IF w_3300_action NE 'DISP'.
    rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Update'.
  ELSE.
    rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Display'.
  ENDIF.
  rec_fieldcat-icon          = abap_true.
  rec_fieldcat-style         = cl_gui_alv_grid=>mc_style_button.
  rec_fieldcat-outputlen     = 11.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

ENDFORM.                    " BUILD_FIELDCAT_FTC_SEL

    rec_fieldcat-outputlen     = 11.
    APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.
  ENDIF.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'NOTES_ICON'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Return Notes'.
  rec_fieldcat-icon          = abap_true.
  rec_fieldcat-style         = cl_gui_alv_grid=>mc_style_button.
  rec_fieldcat-outputlen     = 12.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  IF w_3600_action NE 'DISP'.

    ADD 1 TO l_col.
    rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'EMAIL_ICON'.
    rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
    rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Send Request'.
    rec_fieldcat-icon          = abap_true.
    rec_fieldcat-style         = cl_gui_alv_grid=>mc_style_button.
    rec_fieldcat-outputlen     = 12.
    APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.
  ENDIF.

  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Approved Date'.
  rec_fieldcat-just          = 'C'.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-ref_table     = 'ZIM_IN_PRE_INV'.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'INV_APP'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Invoice Approved'.
  rec_fieldcat-checkbox      = abap_true.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-ref_table     = 'ZIM_IN_PRE_INV'.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'PROC_DATE'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Processed Date'.
  rec_fieldcat-just          = 'C'.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-ref_table     = 'ZIM_IN_PRE_INV'.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'BELNR'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Document No.'.
  rec_fieldcat-just          = 'C'.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'UPD_ICON'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  IF w_3600_action NE 'DISP'.
    rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Update'.
  ELSE.
    rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Display'.
  ENDIF.
  rec_fieldcat-icon          = abap_true.
  rec_fieldcat-style         = cl_gui_alv_grid=>mc_style_button.
  rec_fieldcat-outputlen     = 11.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  IF w_3600_action NE 'DISP' AND
     sy-tcode       = 'ZIM_SCU'.
    CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
    ADD 1 TO l_col.
    rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'REV_ICON'.
    rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
    rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Set to Review by TL/Mgr'.
    rec_fieldcat-icon          = abap_true.
    rec_fieldcat-style         = cl_gui_alv_grid=>mc_style_button.
    rec_fieldcat-outputlen     = 11.
    APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.
  ENDIF.

  IF w_3600_action NE 'DISP' AND
     sy-tcode       = 'ZIM_MGRSCU'.
    CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
    ADD 1 TO l_col.
    rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'APP_ICON'.
    rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
    rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'TL Approve Invoice'.
    rec_fieldcat-icon          = abap_true.
    rec_fieldcat-style         = cl_gui_alv_grid=>mc_style_button.
    rec_fieldcat-outputlen     = 11.
    APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

    CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
    ADD 1 TO l_col.
    rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'MGR_ICON'.
    rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
    rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Mgr Approve Invoice'.
    rec_fieldcat-icon          = abap_true.
    rec_fieldcat-style         = cl_gui_alv_grid=>mc_style_button.
    rec_fieldcat-outputlen     = 11.
    APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

    CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
    ADD 1 TO l_col.
    rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'RET_ICON'.
    rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
    rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Return for Re-work'.
    rec_fieldcat-icon          = abap_true.
    rec_fieldcat-style         = cl_gui_alv_grid=>mc_style_button.
    rec_fieldcat-outputlen     = 11.
    APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.
  ENDIF.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'NOTES_ICON'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Return Notes'.
  rec_fieldcat-icon          = abap_true.
  rec_fieldcat-style         = cl_gui_alv_grid=>mc_style_button.
  rec_fieldcat-outputlen     = 12.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  IF w_3600_action NE 'DISP'.
    CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
    ADD 1 TO l_col.
    rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'PROC_ICON'.
    rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
    rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Process Invoice'.
    rec_fieldcat-icon          = abap_true.
    rec_fieldcat-style         = cl_gui_alv_grid=>mc_style_button.
    rec_fieldcat-outputlen     = 12.
    APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.
  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.                    " BUILD_FIELDCAT_INV_SEL_PRE88


*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  BUILD_FIELDCAT_SEC_CLM_PRE88

FORM build_fieldcat_sec_clm_pre88 TABLES  p_fieldcat
                                  STRUCTURE lvc_s_fcat.

  DATA : l_col        TYPE i,
         l_text(20)   TYPE c.

  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Incident Date'.

  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Claim Number'.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.

  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Claims Status'.
  rec_fieldcat-just          = 'L'.
  rec_fieldcat-lowercase     = abap_true.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'ASS_STATUS_DESC'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Assessment Status'.
  rec_fieldcat-just          = 'L'.
  rec_fieldcat-lowercase     = abap_true.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.

  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Current Status'.
  rec_fieldcat-just          = 'L'.
  rec_fieldcat-lowercase     = abap_true.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'MAIN_INJURY'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Main Injury'.
  rec_fieldcat-just          = 'L'.
  rec_fieldcat-lowercase     = abap_true.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'UPD_ICON'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  IF w_3600_action NE 'DISP'.
    rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Update'.
  ELSE.
    rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Display'.
  ENDIF.
  rec_fieldcat-icon          = abap_true.
  rec_fieldcat-style         = cl_gui_alv_grid=>mc_style_button.
  rec_fieldcat-outputlen     = 11.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Date To'.

  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Status'.
  rec_fieldcat-lowercase     = abap_true.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.


  IF w_3400_action NE 'DISP'.
    IF sy-tcode = 'ZIM_MGRCM'.
      CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
      ADD 1 TO l_col.
      rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'DEL_ICON'.
      rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
      rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Delete'.
      rec_fieldcat-icon          = abap_true.
      rec_fieldcat-style         = cl_gui_alv_grid=>mc_style_button.
      rec_fieldcat-outputlen     = 11.
      APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.
    ENDIF.

    CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
    ADD 1 TO l_col.
    rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'IMP_ICON'.
    rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
    rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Import'.
    rec_fieldcat-icon          = abap_true.
    rec_fieldcat-style         = cl_gui_alv_grid=>mc_style_button.
    rec_fieldcat-outputlen     = 11.
    APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.
  ENDIF.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'DOC_ICON'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Display'.
  rec_fieldcat-icon          = abap_true.
  rec_fieldcat-style         = cl_gui_alv_grid=>mc_style_button.
  rec_fieldcat-outputlen     = 11.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  IF w_3400_action NE 'DISP'.
    IF w_claim_type = 'W'.
      CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
      ADD 1 TO l_col.
      rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'EMAIL_ICON'.
      rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
      rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Send Doc'.
      rec_fieldcat-icon          = abap_true.
      rec_fieldcat-style         = cl_gui_alv_grid=>mc_style_button.
      rec_fieldcat-outputlen     = 11.
      APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-ref_table     = 'ZIM_IN_CLAIM'.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'CLAIM_DESC'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Description'.
  rec_fieldcat-lowercase     = abap_true.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'REASON_DESC'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Reason'.
  rec_fieldcat-lowercase     = abap_true.
  rec_fieldcat-outputlen     = 40.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-ref_table     = 'ZIM_IN_CLAIM_LOG'.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'DATE_UPDATED'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Last Modified On'.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-ref_table     = 'ZIM_IN_CLAIM_LOG'.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'USER_PROCESSED'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Last Modified By'.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

ENDFORM.                    " BUILD_FIELDCAT_CLM_SEL

      rec_fieldcat-icon          = abap_true.
      rec_fieldcat-style         = cl_gui_alv_grid=>mc_style_button.
      rec_fieldcat-outputlen     = 11.
      APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-ref_table     = 'ZIM_IN_REQ_REV'.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'DESCRIPTION'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Description'.
  rec_fieldcat-lowercase     = abap_true.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-ref_table     = 'ZIM_IN_REQ_REV'.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'OTHER_REVIEW_DESC'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Other Review - Description'.
  rec_fieldcat-lowercase     = abap_true.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-ref_table     = 'ZIM_IN_REQ_REV'.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'DATE_UPDATED'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Last Modified On'.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-ref_table     = 'ZIM_IN_REQ_REV'.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'USER_PROCESSED'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Last Modified By'.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

ENDFORM.                    " BUILD_FIELDCAT_REQ_SEL

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  BUILD_FIELDCAT_FIX_SEL
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      -->P_FRLOC_SEL_FIELDCAT  text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM build_fieldcat_fix_sel TABLES  p_fieldcat STRUCTURE lvc_s_fcat.

  DATA: l_col        TYPE i,
         l_text(20)   TYPE c.

  REFRESH p_fieldcat.
  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  l_col = 0.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-ref_table     = 'ZIM_IN_REC_AMT'.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'BEGDA'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Date From'.
  rec_fieldcat-just          = 'C'.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-ref_table     = 'ZIM_IN_REC_AMT'.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'ENDDA'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Date To'.
  rec_fieldcat-just          = 'C'.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  IF w_amount_type NE 'E'.
    CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
    ADD 1 TO l_col.
    rec_fieldcat-ref_table     = 'ZIM_IN_REC_AMT'.
    rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'AMOUNT'.
    rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
    rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Fixed Amount'.
    rec_fieldcat-just          = 'R'.
    APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.
  ELSE.
    CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
    ADD 1 TO l_col.
    rec_fieldcat-ref_table     = 'ZIM_IN_REC_AMT'.
    rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'EXT_EARNINGS'.
    rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
    rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'External Earnings'.
    rec_fieldcat-just          = 'R'.
    APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.
  ENDIF.

  IF w_3400_action NE 'DISP'.
    IF sy-tcode = 'ZIM_MGRCM'.
      CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
      ADD 1 TO l_col.
      rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'DEL_ICON'.
      rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
      rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Delete'.
      rec_fieldcat-icon          = abap_true.
      rec_fieldcat-style         = cl_gui_alv_grid=>mc_style_button.
      rec_fieldcat-outputlen     = 11.
      APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.
    ENDIF.
    IF sy-tcode = 'ZIM_CM'.
      CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
      ADD 1 TO l_col.
      rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'DEL_ICON'.
      rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
      rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Delete'.
      rec_fieldcat-icon          = abap_true.
      rec_fieldcat-style         = cl_gui_alv_grid=>mc_style_button.
      rec_fieldcat-outputlen     = 11.
      APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.
    ENDIF.

    CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
    ADD 1 TO l_col.
    rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'IMP_ICON'.
    rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
    rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Import'.
    rec_fieldcat-icon          = abap_true.
    rec_fieldcat-style         = cl_gui_alv_grid=>mc_style_button.
    rec_fieldcat-outputlen     = 11.
    APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.
  ENDIF.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'DOC_ICON'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Display'.
  rec_fieldcat-icon          = abap_true.
  rec_fieldcat-style         = cl_gui_alv_grid=>mc_style_button.
  rec_fieldcat-outputlen     = 11.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'DESC_ICON'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Comments'.
  rec_fieldcat-icon          = abap_true.
  rec_fieldcat-style         = cl_gui_alv_grid=>mc_style_button.
  rec_fieldcat-outputlen     = 11.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.


ENDFORM.                    " BUILD_FIELDCAT_FIX_SEL
