  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'DEL_ICON'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Delete'.
  rec_fieldcat-icon          = abap_true.
  rec_fieldcat-style         = cl_gui_alv_grid=>mc_style_button.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

ENDFORM.                    " BUILD_FIELDCAT_ECP_HIST


*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  BUILD_FIELDCAT_S49_SEL
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      -->P_FRLOC_SEL_FIELDCAT  text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM build_fieldcat_s49_sel TABLES  p_fieldcat STRUCTURE lvc_s_fcat.

  DATA: l_col        TYPE i,
         l_text(20)   TYPE c.

  REFRESH p_fieldcat.
  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  l_col = 0.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'INCIDENT'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Incident No.'.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'QCTYP_DESC'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Quota Type'.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'DATE_FROM'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Date From'.
  rec_fieldcat-just          = 'C'.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'DATE_TO'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Date To'.
  rec_fieldcat-just          = 'C'.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
* rec_fieldcat-ref_table     = 'PA0416'.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'NUMBR_2'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Hrs/Days'.
  rec_fieldcat-just          = 'R'.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  IF w_3800_action NE 'DISP'.
    CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
    ADD 1 TO l_col.
    rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'IMP_ICON'.
    rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
    rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Import'.
    rec_fieldcat-icon          = abap_true.
    rec_fieldcat-style         = cl_gui_alv_grid=>mc_style_button.
    rec_fieldcat-outputlen     = 11.
    APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.
  ENDIF.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'DOC_ICON'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Display'.
  rec_fieldcat-icon          = abap_true.
  rec_fieldcat-style         = cl_gui_alv_grid=>mc_style_button.
  rec_fieldcat-outputlen     = 11.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.


ENDFORM.                    " BUILD_FIELDCAT_S49_SEL


*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  BUILD_FIELDCAT_WC_LVE
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      -->P_FRLOC_SEL_FIELDCAT  text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM build_fieldcat_wc_lve TABLES  p_fieldcat STRUCTURE lvc_s_fcat.

  DATA: l_col        TYPE i,
         l_text(20)   TYPE c.

  REFRESH p_fieldcat.
  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  l_col = 0.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-ref_table     = 'PA0573'.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'BEGDA'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Date From'.
  rec_fieldcat-just          = 'C'.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-ref_table     = 'PA0573'.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'ENDDA'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Date To'.
  rec_fieldcat-just          = 'C'.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-ref_table     = 'PA0573'.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'BEGUZ'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Time From'.
  rec_fieldcat-just          = 'C'.
  rec_fieldcat-edit_mask     = '__:__'.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-ref_table     = 'PA0573'.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'ENDUZ'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Time To'.
  rec_fieldcat-just          = 'C'.
  rec_fieldcat-edit_mask     = '__:__'.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-ref_table     = 'PA0573'.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'SUBTY'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Lve Code'.
  rec_fieldcat-just          = 'C'.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'LVE_DESC'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Description'.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-ref_table     = 'PA0573'.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'VTKEN'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Prev Day'.
  rec_fieldcat-checkbox      = abap_true.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.

  CLEAR rec_fieldcat.
  ADD 1 TO l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-ref_table     = 'PA0573'.
  rec_fieldcat-fieldname     = 'STDAZ'.
  rec_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col.
  rec_fieldcat-coltext       = 'Duration'.
  rec_fieldcat-just          = 'R'.
  APPEND rec_fieldcat TO p_fieldcat.


ENDFORM.                    " BUILD_FIELDCAT_WC_LVE


*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  GET_INCIDENT
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM get_incident .

  DATA: l_nearmiss             TYPE pa9001-nearmiss,
         l_inc_status           TYPE zim_in_mast-inc_status.


  REFRESH gt_outtab3.
  IF w_pernr IS NOT INITIAL.
    SELECT * FROM zim_incident
           INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE gt_outtab3
           WHERE pernr          = w_pernr.
  ENDIF.

  SORT gt_outtab3 BY nearmiss.
  CLEAR : l_nearmiss, w_dd07v.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab3 INTO gr_outtab3.
    IF gr_outtab3-nearmiss NE l_nearmiss.
      w_domvalue_l = gr_outtab3-nearmiss.
      CALL FUNCTION 'DD_DOMVALUE_TEXT_GET'
        EXPORTING
          domname  = 'ZHR_INC_NEARMISS'
          value    = w_domvalue_l
          langu    = 'E'
        IMPORTING
          dd07v_wa = w_dd07v
          rc       = w_rc
        EXCEPTIONS
          OTHERS   = 1.
      IF  w_rc NE 0.
        CLEAR w_dd07v.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
    gr_outtab3-nearmiss_desc = w_dd07v-ddtext.
    l_nearmiss = gr_outtab3-nearmiss.

    SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_in_mast
           WHERE pernr    = gr_outtab3-pernr
             AND incident = gr_outtab3-identnr.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      gr_outtab3-inc_status = zim_in_mast-inc_status.
    ENDIF.
    MODIFY gt_outtab3 FROM gr_outtab3.
  ENDLOOP.

  SORT gt_outtab3 BY inc_status.
  CLEAR : l_inc_status, w_dd07v.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab3 INTO gr_outtab3.
    IF gr_outtab3-inc_status NE l_inc_status.
      w_domvalue_l = gr_outtab3-inc_status.
      CALL FUNCTION 'DD_DOMVALUE_TEXT_GET'
        EXPORTING
          domname  = 'ZIM_INCIDENT_STATUS'
          value    = w_domvalue_l
          langu    = 'E'
        IMPORTING
          dd07v_wa = w_dd07v
          rc       = w_rc
        EXCEPTIONS
          OTHERS   = 1.
      IF  w_rc NE 0.
        CLEAR w_dd07v.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
    gr_outtab3-im_desc = w_dd07v-ddtext.
    l_inc_status = gr_outtab3-inc_status.

    SELECT SINGLE * FROM pa9082
           WHERE pernr = gr_outtab3-pernr
             AND casno = gr_outtab3-identnr.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      SELECT SINGLE * FROM pa9076
             WHERE pernr = pa9082-pernr
               AND objps = pa9082-objps.
      IF sy-subrc = 0.
        gr_outtab3-wclmn  = pa9076-wclmn.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.

    MODIFY gt_outtab3 FROM gr_outtab3.
  ENDLOOP.

  SORT gt_outtab3 BY pernr
                     dateincident DESCENDING
                     timeincident DESCENDING.


ENDFORM.                    " GET_INCIDENT

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  GET_NEARMISS
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM get_nearmiss .

  DATA: l_nearmiss             TYPE pa9001-nearmiss,
         l_inc_status           TYPE zim_in_mast-inc_status.


  REFRESH gt_outtab13.
  IF w_pernr IS NOT INITIAL.
    IF w_history = abap_true.
      SELECT * FROM pa9001
             INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE gt_outtab13
             WHERE pernr          = w_pernr.
    ELSE.
      SELECT * FROM pa9001
             INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE gt_outtab13
             WHERE pernr          = w_pernr
               AND nearmiss       = '2'.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

  LOOP AT gt_outtab13 INTO gr_outtab13.
    SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_incident
           WHERE pernr    = gr_outtab13-pernr
             AND incident = gr_outtab13-identnr.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      DELETE gt_outtab13.
    ENDIF.
  ENDLOOP.

  CLEAR : l_nearmiss, w_dd07v.
  SORT gt_outtab13 BY nearmiss.
  CLEAR : l_nearmiss, w_dd07v.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab13 INTO gr_outtab13.
    IF gr_outtab13-nearmiss NE l_nearmiss.
      w_domvalue_l = gr_outtab13-nearmiss.
      CALL FUNCTION 'DD_DOMVALUE_TEXT_GET'
        EXPORTING
          domname  = 'ZHR_INC_NEARMISS'
          value    = w_domvalue_l
          langu    = 'E'
        IMPORTING
          dd07v_wa = w_dd07v
          rc       = w_rc
        EXCEPTIONS
          OTHERS   = 1.
      IF  w_rc NE 0.
        CLEAR w_dd07v.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
    gr_outtab13-nearmiss_desc = w_dd07v-ddtext.
    l_nearmiss = gr_outtab13-nearmiss.

    SELECT SINGLE * FROM pa9082
           WHERE pernr = gr_outtab13-pernr
             AND casno = gr_outtab13-identnr.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      SELECT SINGLE * FROM pa9076
             WHERE pernr = pa9082-pernr
               AND objps = pa9082-objps.
      IF sy-subrc = 0.
        gr_outtab13-wclmn  = pa9076-wclmn.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.

    MODIFY gt_outtab13 FROM gr_outtab13.
  ENDLOOP.


  SORT gt_outtab13 BY pernr
                     dateincident DESCENDING
                     timeincident DESCENDING.


ENDFORM.                    " GET_NEARMISS


*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  GET_INIT_ASS
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM get_init_ass .

  DATA: l_inc_type             TYPE zim_in_mast-inc_type,
        l_inc_status           TYPE zim_in_mast-inc_status,
        l_orgeh                TYPE pa0001-orgeh,
        l_orgeh_desc           TYPE hrp1000-stext,
        l_inc_inj              TYPE zim_inc_inj,
        l_locationt            TYPE zp902_locationt,
        l_pa9203               TYPE pa9203,
        l_injury               TYPE string.                 "DEVK9HRGNT



  PERFORM get_employee USING    sy-uname
                       CHANGING w_user_pernr
                                w_user_name.

  REFRESH gt_outtab4.
  SELECT * FROM zim_in_mast
         INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE gt_outtab4
         WHERE inc_status  = 'I'
            OR ass_status  = 'P'.

  SORT gt_outtab4 BY inc_type.
  CLEAR : l_inc_type, w_dd07v.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab4 INTO gr_outtab4.
    IF gr_outtab4-inc_type NE l_inc_type.
      w_domvalue_l = gr_outtab4-inc_type.
      CALL FUNCTION 'DD_DOMVALUE_TEXT_GET'
        EXPORTING
          domname  = 'ZIM_INCIDENT_TYPE'
          value    = w_domvalue_l
          langu    = 'E'
        IMPORTING
          dd07v_wa = w_dd07v
          rc       = w_rc
        EXCEPTIONS
          OTHERS   = 1.
      IF  w_rc NE 0.
        CLEAR w_dd07v.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
    gr_outtab4-inc_type_desc = w_dd07v-ddtext.
    l_inc_type = gr_outtab4-inc_type.

    SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_incident
           WHERE pernr    = gr_outtab4-pernr
             AND incident = gr_outtab4-incident.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      gr_outtab4-dateincident = zim_incident-dateincident.
      gr_outtab4-timeincident = zim_incident-timeincident.
      IF zim_incident-bp_number IS INITIAL.
        SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_inc_inj
               INTO l_inc_inj
               WHERE pernr       = gr_outtab4-pernr
                 AND identnr     = gr_outtab4-incident
                 AND main_injury = 'X'.
        IF sy-subrc = 0.
          gr_outtab4-main_loc = l_inc_inj-location_code.
          gr_outtab4-main_nat = l_inc_inj-nature_code.
        ELSE.
          IF zim_incident-main_loc IS NOT INITIAL.
            CONCATENATE 'LOC_' zim_incident-main_loc
                        INTO w_fieldname.
            LOOP AT nametab
                 WHERE fieldname = w_fieldname.
              gr_outtab4-main_injury  = nametab-fieldtext+3(50).
            ENDLOOP.
          ENDIF.
        ENDIF.
      ELSE.
        PERFORM check_get_bp_injury  USING gr_outtab4-pernr
                                           gr_outtab4-incident
                                    CHANGING l_injury.
        gr_outtab4-main_injury  = l_injury.
      ENDIF.
      MODIFY gt_outtab4 FROM gr_outtab4.

    ENDIF.

    SELECT SINGLE * FROM pa0002
           WHERE pernr    = gr_outtab4-pernr
             AND begda   LE sy-datum
             AND endda   GE sy-datum.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      gr_outtab4-nachn = pa0002-nachn.
      gr_outtab4-vorna = pa0002-vorna.
      PERFORM get_rank USING pa0002-titel
                             pa0002-namzu
                       CHANGING gr_outtab4-rank.
    ENDIF.

    PERFORM get_emp_orgeh USING    gr_outtab4-pernr
                          CHANGING gr_outtab4-orgeh.

    SELECT SINGLE * FROM pa0573
           WHERE pernr = gr_outtab4-pernr
             AND begda LE sy-datum
             AND endda GE sy-datum
             AND ( subty = '5000'
              OR   subty = '5010'
              OR   subty = '5100'
              OR   subty = '5110'
              OR   subty = '5502'
              OR   subty = '5503'
              OR   subty = '5504'
              OR   subty = '5505'
              OR   subty = '5506' ).
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      gr_outtab4-suspended = 'Yes'.
    ENDIF.

    SELECT SINGLE * FROM pa9203
           INTO l_pa9203
           WHERE pernr          = gr_outtab4-pernr
             AND z_duty_status NE 'PID'
             AND begda         LE sy-datum
             AND endda         GE sy-datum.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      gr_outtab4-prd       = 'Yes'.
    ENDIF.


    MODIFY gt_outtab4 FROM gr_outtab4.
  ENDLOOP.

* Only allow managers or the assigned IMA to review cases
* for restricted org units.
  IF sy-tcode NE 'ZIM_MGR'.
    LOOP AT gt_outtab4 INTO gr_outtab4.
      IF gr_outtab4-ima NE w_user_pernr.
        IF gr_outtab4-restricted = abap_true.
          DELETE gt_outtab4.
        ELSE.
          SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_org
                 WHERE orgeh = gr_outtab4-orgeh.
          IF sy-subrc = 0.
            DELETE gt_outtab4.
          ENDIF.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.
    ENDLOOP.
  ENDIF.

  SORT gt_outtab4 BY inc_status.
  CLEAR : l_inc_status, w_dd07v.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab4 INTO gr_outtab4.
    IF gr_outtab4-inc_status NE l_inc_status.
      w_domvalue_l = gr_outtab4-inc_status.
      CALL FUNCTION 'DD_DOMVALUE_TEXT_GET'
        EXPORTING
          domname  = 'ZIM_INCIDENT_STATUS'
          value    = w_domvalue_l
          langu    = 'E'
        IMPORTING
          dd07v_wa = w_dd07v
          rc       = w_rc
        EXCEPTIONS
          OTHERS   = 1.
      IF  w_rc NE 0.
        CLEAR w_dd07v.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
    gr_outtab4-im_desc = w_dd07v-ddtext.
    l_inc_status = gr_outtab4-inc_status.

    MODIFY gt_outtab4 FROM gr_outtab4.
  ENDLOOP.

  SORT gt_outtab4 BY orgeh.
  CLEAR l_orgeh.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab4 INTO gr_outtab4.
    IF gr_outtab4-orgeh NE l_orgeh.
      l_orgeh = gr_outtab4-orgeh.
      SELECT SINGLE * FROM zhorgstruc
             WHERE unit_code = gr_outtab4-orgeh
               AND vbegda   <= sy-datum
               AND vendda   >= sy-datum.
      IF sy-subrc NE 0.
        CLEAR zhorgstruc.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
    IF  zhorgstruc-lvl3_code = 0.
      gr_outtab4-lvl3_orgeh = gr_outtab4-orgeh.
    ELSE.
      gr_outtab4-lvl3_orgeh = zhorgstruc-lvl3_code.
    ENDIF.
    IF  zhorgstruc-lvl4_code = 0.
      gr_outtab4-lvl4_orgeh = gr_outtab4-orgeh.
    ELSE.
      gr_outtab4-lvl4_orgeh = zhorgstruc-lvl4_code.
    ENDIF.
    MODIFY gt_outtab4 FROM gr_outtab4.
  ENDLOOP.

  SORT gt_outtab4 BY orgeh.
  CLEAR : l_orgeh, l_orgeh_desc.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab4 INTO gr_outtab4.
    IF gr_outtab4-orgeh NE l_orgeh.
      l_orgeh = gr_outtab4-orgeh.
      PERFORM get_objtxt  USING    l_orgeh
                                   'O'
                          CHANGING l_orgeh_desc
                                   w_short.
    ENDIF.
    gr_outtab4-orgeh_stext = l_orgeh_desc.
    MODIFY gt_outtab4 FROM gr_outtab4.
  ENDLOOP.

  SORT gt_outtab4 BY lvl3_orgeh.
  CLEAR : l_orgeh, l_orgeh_desc.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab4 INTO gr_outtab4.
    IF gr_outtab4-lvl3_orgeh NE l_orgeh.
      l_orgeh = gr_outtab4-lvl3_orgeh.
      PERFORM get_objtxt  USING    l_orgeh
                                   'O'
                          CHANGING l_orgeh_desc
                                   w_short.
    ENDIF.
    gr_outtab4-lvl3_stext = l_orgeh_desc.
    MODIFY gt_outtab4 FROM gr_outtab4.
  ENDLOOP.

  SORT gt_outtab4 BY lvl4_orgeh.
  CLEAR : l_orgeh, l_orgeh_desc.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab4 INTO gr_outtab4.
    IF gr_outtab4-lvl4_orgeh NE l_orgeh.
      l_orgeh = gr_outtab4-lvl4_orgeh.
      PERFORM get_objtxt  USING    l_orgeh
                                   'O'
                          CHANGING l_orgeh_desc
                                   w_short.
    ENDIF.
    gr_outtab4-lvl4_stext = l_orgeh_desc.
    MODIFY gt_outtab4 FROM gr_outtab4.
  ENDLOOP.

  SORT gt_outtab4 BY ima.
  CLEAR : pa0001.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab4 INTO gr_outtab4.
    IF gr_outtab4-ima IS NOT INITIAL.
      IF gr_outtab4-ima NE pa0001-pernr.
        SELECT SINGLE * FROM  pa0001
               WHERE  pernr  EQ gr_outtab4-ima
                 AND    endda  GE sy-datum
                 AND    begda  LE sy-datum.
        IF  sy-subrc NE 0.
          CLEAR pa0001.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.
      gr_outtab4-ima_name = pa0001-ename.
      MODIFY gt_outtab4 FROM gr_outtab4.
    ENDIF.
  ENDLOOP.

  SORT gt_outtab4 BY main_loc.
  CLEAR : l_locationt.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab4 INTO gr_outtab4.
    IF gr_outtab4-main_loc IS NOT INITIAL.
      IF gr_outtab4-main_loc NE l_locationt-location_code.
        SELECT SINGLE * FROM  zp902_locationt
               INTO l_locationt
               WHERE  sprsl = 'E'
                 AND  location_code = gr_outtab4-main_loc.
        IF  sy-subrc NE 0.
          CLEAR l_locationt.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.
      gr_outtab4-main_injury  = l_locationt-location_desc.
      MODIFY gt_outtab4 FROM gr_outtab4.
    ENDIF.
  ENDLOOP.

  SORT gt_outtab4 BY lvl3_orgeh
                     lvl4_orgeh
                     dateincident
                     timeincident.


ENDFORM.                    " GET_INIT_ASS

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  GET_INIT_ASS_WC
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM get_init_ass_wc .

  DATA: l_inc_type             TYPE zim_in_mast-inc_type,
         l_inc_status           TYPE zim_in_mast-inc_status,
         l_orgeh                TYPE pa0001-orgeh,
         l_orgeh_desc           TYPE hrp1000-stext.


  PERFORM get_employee USING    sy-uname
                       CHANGING w_user_pernr
                                w_user_name.

  REFRESH gt_outtab4.
  SELECT * FROM zim_in_mast
         INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE gt_outtab4
         WHERE hod         = ' '.

  SORT gt_outtab4 BY pernr incident.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab4 INTO gr_outtab4.
    SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_in_cm
           WHERE pernr    = gr_outtab4-pernr
             AND incident = gr_outtab4-incident.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      IF zim_in_cm-wc_advisor IS NOT INITIAL OR
         zim_in_cm-wc_status   = 'C'.
        DELETE gt_outtab4.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
  ENDLOOP.

  PERFORM get_init_ass_cm .

ENDFORM.                    " GET_INIT_ASS_WC

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  GET_INIT_ASS_IP
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM get_init_ass_ip .

  DATA: l_inc_type             TYPE zim_in_mast-inc_type,
         l_inc_status           TYPE zim_in_mast-inc_status,
         l_orgeh                TYPE pa0001-orgeh,
         l_orgeh_desc           TYPE hrp1000-stext,
         lt_220                 TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF pa0220,
         ls_220                 LIKE LINE OF           lt_220.


  PERFORM get_employee USING    sy-uname
                       CHANGING w_user_pernr
                                w_user_name.

  REFRESH gt_outtab4.
  SELECT * FROM zim_in_mast
         INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE gt_outtab4
         WHERE hod         = ' '.

  SORT gt_outtab4 BY pernr incident.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab4 INTO gr_outtab4.
    SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_in_cm
           WHERE pernr    = gr_outtab4-pernr
             AND incident = gr_outtab4-incident.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      IF zim_in_cm-ip_advisor IS NOT INITIAL OR
         zim_in_cm-wc_status   = 'C'         OR
         zim_in_cm-ip_status   = 'C'.
        DELETE gt_outtab4.
      ELSE.
        gr_outtab4-ip_start_date  = zim_in_cm-ip_start_date.
        gr_outtab4-topup_end_date = zim_in_cm-topup_end_date.
        IF gr_outtab4-topup_end_date IS NOT INITIAL.
          gr_outtab4-ip_to_commence = gr_outtab4-topup_end_date -
                                      sy-datum + 1.
          IF gr_outtab4-ip_to_commence LT 0.
            gr_outtab4-ip_to_commence = 0.
          ENDIF.
        ENDIF.
        MODIFY gt_outtab4 FROM gr_outtab4.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
  ENDLOOP.


* do not display incidents for admin, security and police covered by
* the police super scheme and anyone else who is not covered by the
* death and disability scheme.

  IF sy-dynnr = '2002'.
    LOOP AT gt_outtab4 INTO gr_outtab4.
      SELECT * FROM pa0220 INTO TABLE lt_220
         WHERE pernr = gr_outtab4-pernr.

      IF sy-subrc = 0.
        SORT lt_220 DESCENDING BY begda.
        READ TABLE lt_220 INTO ls_220 INDEX 1.
        IF ls_220-fcode NOT BETWEEN '9DDA' AND '9DDH'.
          DELETE gt_outtab4.
          CONTINUE.
        ENDIF.
      ELSE.
        DELETE gt_outtab4.
        CONTINUE.
      ENDIF.
    ENDLOOP.
  ENDIF.

  SORT gt_outtab4 BY inc_type.
  CLEAR : l_inc_type, w_dd07v.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab4 INTO gr_outtab4.
    IF gr_outtab4-inc_type NE l_inc_type.
      w_domvalue_l = gr_outtab4-inc_type.
      CALL FUNCTION 'DD_DOMVALUE_TEXT_GET'
        EXPORTING
          domname  = 'ZIM_INCIDENT_TYPE'
          value    = w_domvalue_l
          langu    = 'E'
        IMPORTING
          dd07v_wa = w_dd07v
          rc       = w_rc
        EXCEPTIONS
          OTHERS   = 1.
      IF  w_rc NE 0.
        CLEAR w_dd07v.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
    gr_outtab4-inc_type_desc = w_dd07v-ddtext.
    l_inc_type = gr_outtab4-inc_type.

    SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_incident
           WHERE pernr    = gr_outtab4-pernr
             AND incident = gr_outtab4-incident.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      gr_outtab4-dateincident = zim_incident-dateincident.
      gr_outtab4-timeincident = zim_incident-timeincident.
    ENDIF.

    SELECT SINGLE * FROM pa0002
           WHERE pernr    = gr_outtab4-pernr
             AND begda   LE sy-datum
             AND endda   GE sy-datum.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      gr_outtab4-nachn = pa0002-nachn.
      gr_outtab4-vorna = pa0002-vorna.
    ENDIF.

    PERFORM get_emp_orgeh USING    gr_outtab4-pernr
                          CHANGING gr_outtab4-orgeh.
    IF pa0001-persk GE 'XA' AND
       pa0001-persk LE 'XF'.
      MODIFY gt_outtab4 FROM gr_outtab4.
    ELSE.
      DELETE gt_outtab4.
    ENDIF.
  ENDLOOP.

  SORT gt_outtab4 BY inc_status.
  CLEAR : l_inc_status, w_dd07v.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab4 INTO gr_outtab4.
    IF gr_outtab4-inc_status NE l_inc_status.
      w_domvalue_l = gr_outtab4-inc_status.
      CALL FUNCTION 'DD_DOMVALUE_TEXT_GET'
        EXPORTING
          domname  = 'ZIM_INCIDENT_STATUS'
          value    = w_domvalue_l
          langu    = 'E'
        IMPORTING
          dd07v_wa = w_dd07v
          rc       = w_rc
        EXCEPTIONS
          OTHERS   = 1.
      IF  w_rc NE 0.
        CLEAR w_dd07v.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
    gr_outtab4-im_desc = w_dd07v-ddtext.
    l_inc_status = gr_outtab4-inc_status.

    MODIFY gt_outtab4 FROM gr_outtab4.
  ENDLOOP.

  SORT gt_outtab4 BY orgeh.
  CLEAR l_orgeh.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab4 INTO gr_outtab4.
    IF gr_outtab4-orgeh NE l_orgeh.
      l_orgeh = gr_outtab4-orgeh.
      SELECT SINGLE * FROM zhorgstruc
             WHERE unit_code = gr_outtab4-orgeh
               AND vbegda   <= sy-datum
               AND vendda   >= sy-datum.
      IF sy-subrc NE 0.
        CLEAR zhorgstruc.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
    IF  zhorgstruc-lvl3_code = 0.
      gr_outtab4-lvl3_orgeh = gr_outtab4-orgeh.
    ELSE.
      gr_outtab4-lvl3_orgeh = zhorgstruc-lvl3_code.
    ENDIF.
    IF  zhorgstruc-lvl4_code = 0.
      gr_outtab4-lvl4_orgeh = gr_outtab4-orgeh.
    ELSE.
      gr_outtab4-lvl4_orgeh = zhorgstruc-lvl4_code.
    ENDIF.
    MODIFY gt_outtab4 FROM gr_outtab4.
  ENDLOOP.

  SORT gt_outtab4 BY lvl3_orgeh.
  CLEAR : l_orgeh, l_orgeh_desc.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab4 INTO gr_outtab4.
    IF gr_outtab4-lvl3_orgeh NE l_orgeh.
      l_orgeh = gr_outtab4-lvl3_orgeh.
      PERFORM get_objtxt  USING    l_orgeh
                                   'O'
                          CHANGING l_orgeh_desc
                                   w_short.
    ENDIF.
    gr_outtab4-lvl3_stext = l_orgeh_desc.
    MODIFY gt_outtab4 FROM gr_outtab4.
  ENDLOOP.

  SORT gt_outtab4 BY lvl4_orgeh.
  CLEAR : l_orgeh, l_orgeh_desc.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab4 INTO gr_outtab4.
    IF gr_outtab4-lvl4_orgeh NE l_orgeh.
      l_orgeh = gr_outtab4-lvl4_orgeh.
      PERFORM get_objtxt  USING    l_orgeh
                                   'O'
                          CHANGING l_orgeh_desc
                                   w_short.
    ENDIF.
    gr_outtab4-lvl4_stext = l_orgeh_desc.
    MODIFY gt_outtab4 FROM gr_outtab4.
  ENDLOOP.


  SORT gt_outtab4 BY lvl3_orgeh
                     lvl4_orgeh
                     dateincident
                     timeincident.


ENDFORM.                    " GET_INIT_ASS_IP

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  GET_INIT_ASS_CM
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM get_init_ass_cm .

  DATA: l_inc_type             TYPE zim_in_mast-inc_type,
         l_inc_status           TYPE zim_in_mast-inc_status,
         l_orgeh                TYPE pa0001-orgeh,
         l_orgeh_desc           TYPE hrp1000-stext.


  SORT gt_outtab4 BY inc_type.
  CLEAR : l_inc_type, w_dd07v.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab4 INTO gr_outtab4.
    IF gr_outtab4-inc_type NE l_inc_type.
      w_domvalue_l = gr_outtab4-inc_type.
      CALL FUNCTION 'DD_DOMVALUE_TEXT_GET'
        EXPORTING
          domname  = 'ZIM_INCIDENT_TYPE'
          value    = w_domvalue_l
          langu    = 'E'
        IMPORTING
          dd07v_wa = w_dd07v
          rc       = w_rc
        EXCEPTIONS
          OTHERS   = 1.
      IF  w_rc NE 0.
        CLEAR w_dd07v.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
    gr_outtab4-inc_type_desc = w_dd07v-ddtext.
    l_inc_type = gr_outtab4-inc_type.

    SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_incident
           WHERE pernr    = gr_outtab4-pernr
             AND incident = gr_outtab4-incident.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      gr_outtab4-dateincident = zim_incident-dateincident.
      gr_outtab4-timeincident = zim_incident-timeincident.
    ENDIF.

    SELECT SINGLE * FROM pa0002
           WHERE pernr    = gr_outtab4-pernr
             AND begda   LE sy-datum
             AND endda   GE sy-datum.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      gr_outtab4-nachn = pa0002-nachn.
      gr_outtab4-vorna = pa0002-vorna.
    ENDIF.

    PERFORM get_emp_orgeh USING    gr_outtab4-pernr
                          CHANGING gr_outtab4-orgeh.

    MODIFY gt_outtab4 FROM gr_outtab4.
  ENDLOOP.

  SORT gt_outtab4 BY inc_status.
  CLEAR : l_inc_status, w_dd07v.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab4 INTO gr_outtab4.
    IF gr_outtab4-inc_status NE l_inc_status.
      w_domvalue_l = gr_outtab4-inc_status.
      CALL FUNCTION 'DD_DOMVALUE_TEXT_GET'
        EXPORTING
          domname  = 'ZIM_INCIDENT_STATUS'
          value    = w_domvalue_l
          langu    = 'E'
        IMPORTING
          dd07v_wa = w_dd07v
          rc       = w_rc
        EXCEPTIONS
          OTHERS   = 1.
      IF  w_rc NE 0.
        CLEAR w_dd07v.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
    gr_outtab4-im_desc = w_dd07v-ddtext.
    l_inc_status = gr_outtab4-inc_status.

    MODIFY gt_outtab4 FROM gr_outtab4.
  ENDLOOP.

  SORT gt_outtab4 BY orgeh.
  CLEAR l_orgeh.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab4 INTO gr_outtab4.
    IF gr_outtab4-orgeh NE l_orgeh.
      l_orgeh = gr_outtab4-orgeh.
      SELECT SINGLE * FROM zhorgstruc
             WHERE unit_code = gr_outtab4-orgeh
               AND vbegda   <= sy-datum
               AND vendda   >= sy-datum.
      IF sy-subrc NE 0.
        CLEAR zhorgstruc.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
    IF  zhorgstruc-lvl3_code = 0.
      gr_outtab4-lvl3_orgeh = gr_outtab4-orgeh.
    ELSE.
      gr_outtab4-lvl3_orgeh = zhorgstruc-lvl3_code.
    ENDIF.
    IF  zhorgstruc-lvl4_code = 0.
      gr_outtab4-lvl4_orgeh = gr_outtab4-orgeh.
    ELSE.
      gr_outtab4-lvl4_orgeh = zhorgstruc-lvl4_code.
    ENDIF.
    MODIFY gt_outtab4 FROM gr_outtab4.
  ENDLOOP.

  SORT gt_outtab4 BY lvl3_orgeh.
  CLEAR : l_orgeh, l_orgeh_desc.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab4 INTO gr_outtab4.
    IF gr_outtab4-lvl3_orgeh NE l_orgeh.
      l_orgeh = gr_outtab4-lvl3_orgeh.
      PERFORM get_objtxt  USING    l_orgeh
                                   'O'
                          CHANGING l_orgeh_desc
                                   w_short.
    ENDIF.
    gr_outtab4-lvl3_stext = l_orgeh_desc.
    MODIFY gt_outtab4 FROM gr_outtab4.
  ENDLOOP.

  SORT gt_outtab4 BY lvl4_orgeh.
  CLEAR : l_orgeh, l_orgeh_desc.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab4 INTO gr_outtab4.
    IF gr_outtab4-lvl4_orgeh NE l_orgeh.
      l_orgeh = gr_outtab4-lvl4_orgeh.
      PERFORM get_objtxt  USING    l_orgeh
                                   'O'
                          CHANGING l_orgeh_desc
                                   w_short.
    ENDIF.
    gr_outtab4-lvl4_stext = l_orgeh_desc.
    MODIFY gt_outtab4 FROM gr_outtab4.
  ENDLOOP.

  LOOP AT gt_outtab4 INTO gr_outtab4.
    SELECT SINGLE * FROM  pa0001
           WHERE  pernr  EQ gr_outtab4-pernr
             AND    endda  GE gr_outtab4-dateincident
             AND    begda  LE gr_outtab4-dateincident.
    IF  sy-subrc NE 0.
      CLEAR pa0001.
    ENDIF.
    gr_outtab4-kostl = pa0001-kostl.
    MODIFY gt_outtab4 FROM gr_outtab4.
  ENDLOOP.

  SORT gt_outtab4 BY kostl.
  CLEAR : cskt.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab4 INTO gr_outtab4.
    IF gr_outtab4-kostl NE cskt-kostl.
      SELECT SINGLE * FROM cskt
             WHERE spras = 'E'
               AND kokrs = '1100'
               AND kostl = gr_outtab4-kostl
               AND datbi GE sy-datum.
      IF sy-subrc NE 0.
        CLEAR cskt.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
    gr_outtab4-ltext = cskt-ltext.
    MODIFY gt_outtab4 FROM gr_outtab4.
  ENDLOOP.


  SORT gt_outtab4 BY lvl3_orgeh
                     lvl4_orgeh
                     dateincident
                     timeincident.


ENDFORM.                    " GET_INIT_ASS_CM

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  GET_INIT_ASS_PRE88
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM get_init_ass_pre88 .

  DATA : l_inc_type             TYPE zim_in_mast-inc_type,
         l_inc_status           TYPE zim_in_mast-inc_status,
         l_orgeh                TYPE pa0001-orgeh,
         l_orgeh_desc           TYPE hrp1000-stext,
         tb_in_pre88            TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF zim_in_pre88,
         l_in_pre88             TYPE zim_in_pre88.


  PERFORM get_employee USING    sy-uname
                       CHANGING w_user_pernr
                                w_user_name.

  REFRESH gt_outtab4.

  IF zim_screen-ass_status IS INITIAL.
    SELECT * FROM zim_in_mast
           INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE gt_outtab4
           WHERE hod         = 'X'.

    SORT gt_outtab4 BY pernr incident.
    LOOP AT gt_outtab4 INTO gr_outtab4.
      SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_in_pre88
             WHERE pernr    = gr_outtab4-pernr
               AND incident = gr_outtab4-incident.
      IF sy-subrc = 0.
        IF zim_in_pre88-ass_status   = 'I' OR  " Incident Only
           zim_in_pre88-ass_status   = 'T'.    " Triage Complete
          DELETE gt_outtab4.
        ELSE.
          gr_outtab4-scu_ass_status = zim_in_pre88-ass_status.
          MODIFY gt_outtab4 FROM gr_outtab4.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.
    ENDLOOP.
  ELSE.
    SELECT * FROM zim_in_pre88
         INTO TABLE tb_in_pre88
         WHERE ass_status = zim_screen-ass_status.
    LOOP AT tb_in_pre88 INTO l_in_pre88.
      SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_in_mast
             WHERE pernr    = l_in_pre88-pernr
               AND incident = l_in_pre88-incident.
      IF sy-subrc = 0.
        CLEAR gr_outtab4.
        MOVE-CORRESPONDING zim_in_mast TO gr_outtab4.
        gr_outtab4-scu_ass_status = l_in_pre88-ass_status.
        APPEND gr_outtab4 TO gt_outtab4.
      ENDIF.
    ENDLOOP.
  ENDIF.

  PERFORM get_init_ass_pre88_f .

ENDFORM.                    " GET_INIT_ASS_PRE88

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  GET_INIT_ASS_PRE88_F
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM get_init_ass_pre88_f .

  DATA: l_inc_type             TYPE zim_in_mast-inc_type,
        l_inc_status           TYPE zim_in_mast-inc_status,
        l_orgeh                TYPE pa0001-orgeh,
        l_orgeh_desc           TYPE hrp1000-stext,
        l_inc_inj              TYPE zim_inc_inj,
        l_locationt            TYPE zp902_locationt,
        l_cert_status          TYPE zim_mc_status,
        l_cert_type(1)         TYPE c,
        l_t1                   TYPE c,
        l_t2                   TYPE c,
        l_t3                   TYPE c,
        l_t4                   TYPE c,
        l_desc(60)             TYPE c.


  SORT gt_outtab4 BY inc_type.
  CLEAR : l_inc_type, w_dd07v.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab4 INTO gr_outtab4.
    IF gr_outtab4-inc_type NE l_inc_type.
      w_domvalue_l = gr_outtab4-inc_type.
      CALL FUNCTION 'DD_DOMVALUE_TEXT_GET'
        EXPORTING
          domname  = 'ZIM_INCIDENT_TYPE'
          value    = w_domvalue_l
          langu    = 'E'
        IMPORTING
          dd07v_wa = w_dd07v
          rc       = w_rc
        EXCEPTIONS
          OTHERS   = 1.
      IF  w_rc NE 0.
        CLEAR w_dd07v.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
    gr_outtab4-inc_type_desc = w_dd07v-ddtext.
    l_inc_type = gr_outtab4-inc_type.

    SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_incident
           WHERE pernr    = gr_outtab4-pernr
             AND incident = gr_outtab4-incident.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      gr_outtab4-dateincident = zim_incident-dateincident.
      gr_outtab4-timeincident = zim_incident-timeincident.
    ENDIF.

    IF gr_outtab4-dateincident LT '20201231'.
      DELETE gt_outtab4.
      CONTINUE.
    ENDIF.

    SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_inc_inj
           INTO l_inc_inj
           WHERE pernr       = gr_outtab4-pernr
             AND identnr     = gr_outtab4-incident
             AND main_injury = 'X'.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      gr_outtab4-main_loc = l_inc_inj-location_code.
      gr_outtab4-main_nat = l_inc_inj-nature_code.
    ELSE.
      CONCATENATE 'LOC_' zim_incident-main_loc
                  INTO w_fieldname.
      LOOP AT nametab
           WHERE fieldname = w_fieldname.
        gr_outtab4-main_injury  = nametab-fieldtext+3(50).
      ENDLOOP.
    ENDIF.

    SELECT SINGLE * FROM pa0002
           WHERE pernr    = gr_outtab4-pernr
             AND begda   LE sy-datum
             AND endda   GE sy-datum.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      gr_outtab4-nachn = pa0002-nachn.
      gr_outtab4-vorna = pa0002-vorna.
      PERFORM get_rank USING pa0002-titel
                             pa0002-namzu
                       CHANGING gr_outtab4-rank.
    ENDIF.

    PERFORM get_emp_orgeh USING    gr_outtab4-pernr
                          CHANGING gr_outtab4-orgeh.

    MODIFY gt_outtab4 FROM gr_outtab4.
  ENDLOOP.


  SORT gt_outtab4 BY scu_ass_status.
  CLEAR : l_inc_status, w_dd07v.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab4 INTO gr_outtab4.
    IF gr_outtab4-scu_ass_status NE l_inc_status.
      w_domvalue_l = gr_outtab4-scu_ass_status.
      CALL FUNCTION 'DD_DOMVALUE_TEXT_GET'
        EXPORTING
          domname  = 'ZIM_SCU_ASS_STATUS'
          value    = w_domvalue_l
          langu    = 'E'
        IMPORTING
          dd07v_wa = w_dd07v
          rc       = w_rc
        EXCEPTIONS
          OTHERS   = 1.
      IF  w_rc NE 0.
        CLEAR w_dd07v.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
    gr_outtab4-scu_ass_status_desc = w_dd07v-ddtext.
    l_inc_status = gr_outtab4-scu_ass_status.

    MODIFY gt_outtab4 FROM gr_outtab4.
  ENDLOOP.

  SORT gt_outtab4 BY orgeh.
  CLEAR l_orgeh.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab4 INTO gr_outtab4.
    IF gr_outtab4-orgeh NE l_orgeh.
      l_orgeh = gr_outtab4-orgeh.
      SELECT SINGLE * FROM zhorgstruc
             WHERE unit_code = gr_outtab4-orgeh
               AND vbegda   <= sy-datum
               AND vendda   >= sy-datum.
      IF sy-subrc NE 0.
        CLEAR zhorgstruc.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
    IF  zhorgstruc-lvl3_code = 0.
      gr_outtab4-lvl3_orgeh = gr_outtab4-orgeh.
    ELSE.
      gr_outtab4-lvl3_orgeh = zhorgstruc-lvl3_code.
    ENDIF.
    IF  zhorgstruc-lvl4_code = 0.
      gr_outtab4-lvl4_orgeh = gr_outtab4-orgeh.
    ELSE.
      gr_outtab4-lvl4_orgeh = zhorgstruc-lvl4_code.
    ENDIF.
    MODIFY gt_outtab4 FROM gr_outtab4.
  ENDLOOP.

  SORT gt_outtab4 BY orgeh.
  CLEAR : l_orgeh, l_orgeh_desc.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab4 INTO gr_outtab4.
    IF gr_outtab4-orgeh NE l_orgeh.
      l_orgeh = gr_outtab4-orgeh.
      PERFORM get_objtxt  USING    l_orgeh
                                   'O'
                          CHANGING l_orgeh_desc
                                   w_short.
    ENDIF.
    gr_outtab4-orgeh_stext = l_orgeh_desc.
    MODIFY gt_outtab4 FROM gr_outtab4.
  ENDLOOP.

  SORT gt_outtab4 BY lvl3_orgeh.
  CLEAR : l_orgeh, l_orgeh_desc.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab4 INTO gr_outtab4.
    IF gr_outtab4-lvl3_orgeh NE l_orgeh.
      l_orgeh = gr_outtab4-lvl3_orgeh.
      PERFORM get_objtxt  USING    l_orgeh
                                   'O'
                          CHANGING l_orgeh_desc
                                   w_short.
    ENDIF.
    gr_outtab4-lvl3_stext = l_orgeh_desc.
    MODIFY gt_outtab4 FROM gr_outtab4.
  ENDLOOP.

  SORT gt_outtab4 BY lvl4_orgeh.
  CLEAR : l_orgeh, l_orgeh_desc.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab4 INTO gr_outtab4.
    IF gr_outtab4-lvl4_orgeh NE l_orgeh.
      l_orgeh = gr_outtab4-lvl4_orgeh.
      PERFORM get_objtxt  USING    l_orgeh
                                   'O'
                          CHANGING l_orgeh_desc
                                   w_short.
    ENDIF.
    gr_outtab4-lvl4_stext = l_orgeh_desc.
    MODIFY gt_outtab4 FROM gr_outtab4.
  ENDLOOP.

  LOOP AT gt_outtab4 INTO gr_outtab4.
    PERFORM get_current_cert USING    gr_outtab4-pernr
                                      gr_outtab4-incident
                             CHANGING gr_outtab4-cert_status
                                      gr_outtab4-cert_type
                                      l_t1
                                      l_t2
                                      l_t3
                                      l_t4.

    MODIFY gt_outtab4 FROM gr_outtab4.
  ENDLOOP.

  SORT gt_outtab4 BY cert_type cert_status.
  CLEAR : l_cert_status, l_desc.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab4 INTO gr_outtab4.

    IF gr_outtab4-cert_status IS NOT INITIAL.
      IF gr_outtab4-cert_status NE l_cert_status OR
         gr_outtab4-cert_type   NE l_cert_type.

        l_cert_status = gr_outtab4-cert_status.
        l_cert_type   = gr_outtab4-cert_type.
        IF l_cert_type = 'M'.
          PERFORM get_domain_desc  USING 'ZIM_MC_STATUS'
                                         l_cert_status
                                CHANGING l_desc.
        ELSE.
          PERFORM get_domain_desc  USING 'ZIM_CAP_STATUS'
                                         l_cert_status
                                CHANGING l_desc.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.
      gr_outtab4-cert_status_desc = l_desc.

      MODIFY gt_outtab4 FROM gr_outtab4.
    ENDIF.
  ENDLOOP.

  SORT gt_outtab4 BY main_loc.
  CLEAR : l_locationt.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab4 INTO gr_outtab4.
    IF gr_outtab4-main_loc IS NOT INITIAL.
      IF gr_outtab4-main_loc NE l_locationt-location_code.
        SELECT SINGLE * FROM  zp902_locationt
               INTO l_locationt
               WHERE  sprsl = 'E'
                 AND  location_code = gr_outtab4-main_loc.
        IF  sy-subrc NE 0.
          CLEAR l_locationt.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.
      gr_outtab4-main_injury  = l_locationt-location_desc.
      MODIFY gt_outtab4 FROM gr_outtab4.
    ENDIF.
  ENDLOOP.


  SORT gt_outtab4 BY scu_ass_status
                     lvl3_orgeh
                     lvl4_orgeh
                     dateincident
                     timeincident.


ENDFORM.                    " GET_INIT_ASS_PRE88_F

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  GET_INIT_CON
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM get_init_con USING p_type.

  DATA: l_inc_type             TYPE zim_in_mast-inc_type,
         l_inc_status           TYPE zim_in_mast-inc_status,
         l_orgeh                TYPE pa0001-orgeh,
         l_orgeh_desc           TYPE hrp1000-stext.


  PERFORM get_employee USING    sy-uname
                       CHANGING w_user_pernr
                                w_user_name.

  REFRESH gt_outtab5.
  SELECT * FROM zim_action
         INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE gt_outtab5
         WHERE contact_type = p_type.

  SORT gt_outtab5 BY cont_act_code.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab5 INTO gr_outtab5.
    SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_in_act
           WHERE pernr    = zim_in_mast-pernr
             AND incident = zim_in_mast-incident
             AND contact_type = gr_outtab5-contact_type
             AND cont_act_code = gr_outtab5-cont_act_code.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      gr_outtab5-cont_act_comp = zim_in_act-cont_act_comp.
      gr_outtab5-cont_act_date = zim_in_act-cont_act_date.
    ENDIF.

    CONCATENATE zim_in_mast-pernr zim_in_mast-incident
                gr_outtab5-contact_type gr_outtab5-cont_act_code
                INTO w_text_key.
    PERFORM check_text      USING    w_text_key
                            CHANGING w_exists.
    IF w_exists = abap_true.
      gr_outtab5-notes_icon = icon_text_act.
    ELSE.
      gr_outtab5-notes_icon = icon_text_ina.
    ENDIF.
    MODIFY gt_outtab5 FROM gr_outtab5.
  ENDLOOP.


ENDFORM.                    " GET_INIT_CON

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  GET_CASE_ACT
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM get_case_act .

  PERFORM get_employee USING    sy-uname
                       CHANGING w_user_pernr
                                w_user_name.

  REFRESH gt_outtab7.
  IF  w_ima IS INITIAL AND
     w_incident_status = 'Z'.
    SELECT * FROM zim_in_mast
            INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE gt_outtab7
            WHERE inc_status = 'O' OR
                  inc_status = 'T'.
  ELSEIF w_ima IS NOT INITIAL AND
     w_incident_status = 'Z'.
    SELECT * FROM zim_in_mast
           INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE gt_outtab7
          WHERE ( inc_status = 'O' OR
                inc_status = 'T')  AND
                ima        =  w_ima.
  ELSEIF w_ima IS NOT INITIAL AND
     w_incident_status NE 'A'.
    SELECT * FROM zim_in_mast
           INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE gt_outtab7
           WHERE inc_status = w_incident_status
             AND ima        = w_ima.
  ELSEIF w_ima IS NOT INITIAL AND
     w_incident_status = 'A'.
    SELECT * FROM zim_in_mast
           INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE gt_outtab7
           WHERE ima        = w_ima.
  ELSEIF w_ima IS INITIAL AND
     w_incident_status NE 'A'.
    SELECT * FROM zim_in_mast
           INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE gt_outtab7
           WHERE inc_status = w_incident_status.
  ELSEIF w_ima IS INITIAL AND
     w_incident_status = 'A'.
    SELECT * FROM zim_in_mast
           INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE gt_outtab7.
  ENDIF.


  PERFORM get_case_add.

  IF w_ima IS INITIAL.
    CLEAR w_ima_name.
  ENDIF.

  SORT gt_outtab7 BY nachn.

ENDFORM.                    " GET_CASE_ACT

*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  get_case_claim
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM get_case_claim .

  DATA : l_pa9076             TYPE pa9076,
         l_pa9082             TYPE pa9082,
         tb_pa9076            TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF pa9076.


  PERFORM get_employee USING    sy-uname
                       CHANGING w_user_pernr
                                w_user_name.

  REFRESH gt_outtab7.

  IF zim_screen-wclmn IS NOT INITIAL.
    SELECT * FROM pa9076
           INTO TABLE tb_pa9076
           WHERE wclmn = zim_screen-wclmn.
    LOOP AT tb_pa9076 INTO l_pa9076.
      CLEAR gr_outtab7.
      SELECT SINGLE * FROM pa9082
             INTO l_pa9082
             WHERE pernr = l_pa9076-pernr
               AND objps = l_pa9076-objps.
      IF sy-subrc = 0.
        gr_outtab7-pernr    = l_pa9082-pernr.
        gr_outtab7-incident = l_pa9082-casno.
        SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_in_mast
               INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF gr_outtab7
               WHERE pernr    = l_pa9082-pernr
                 AND incident = l_pa9082-casno.
        APPEND gr_outtab7 TO gt_outtab7.
      ENDIF.
    ENDLOOP.
  ELSE.
    SELECT * FROM zim_in_mast
           INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE gt_outtab7
           WHERE incident = zim_screen-incident.
  ENDIF.


  PERFORM get_case_add.

  SORT gt_outtab7 BY nachn.

ENDFORM.                    " GET_CASE_CLAIM

*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  GET_CASE_ORGEH
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM get_case_orgeh .


  PERFORM get_employee USING    sy-uname
                       CHANGING w_user_pernr
                                w_user_name.

  REFRESH gt_outtab7.
  SELECT * FROM zim_in_mast
         INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE gt_outtab7
         WHERE inc_status NE 'C'.

  PERFORM get_case_add.

  SORT gt_outtab7 BY nachn.

ENDFORM.                    " GET_CASE_ORGEH

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  GET_CASE_PRE88
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM get_case_pre88 .

  DATA : tb_in_pre88         TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF zim_in_pre88,
         l_in_pre88          TYPE zim_in_pre88,
         l_in_mast           TYPE zim_in_mast.

  PERFORM get_employee USING    sy-uname
                       CHANGING w_user_pernr
                                w_user_name.

  IF w_pre88_status NE 'F' AND
     w_pre88_status NE 'D' AND
     w_pre88_status NE 'Z'.
    SELECT * FROM zim_in_pre88
            INTO TABLE tb_in_pre88
            WHERE ass_status  = 'T'
              AND closed_date = '00000000'.
  ELSE.
    SELECT * FROM zim_in_pre88
            INTO TABLE tb_in_pre88
            WHERE ass_status  = 'T'.
  ENDIF.

  REFRESH gt_outtab7.
  LOOP AT tb_in_pre88 INTO l_in_pre88.
    IF  ( w_ima IS INITIAL AND
          w_pre88_status IS INITIAL ) OR
        ( w_ima = l_in_pre88-scu_advisor AND
          w_pre88_status IS INITIAL ) OR
        ( w_ima IS INITIAL AND
          w_pre88_status = l_in_pre88-current_status ) OR
        ( w_ima = l_in_pre88-scu_advisor AND
          w_pre88_status = l_in_pre88-current_status ).

      CLEAR gr_outtab7.
      SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_in_mast
             INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF gr_outtab7
            WHERE pernr    = l_in_pre88-pernr
              AND incident = l_in_pre88-incident.
      IF sy-subrc = 0.
        gr_outtab7-scu_advisor        = l_in_pre88-scu_advisor.
        gr_outtab7-scu_current_status = l_in_pre88-current_status.
        gr_outtab7-scu_current_action = l_in_pre88-current_action.
        gr_outtab7-scu_created_date   = l_in_pre88-created_date.
        gr_outtab7-scu_status_date    = l_in_pre88-status_date.
        gr_outtab7-scu_ass_status     = l_in_pre88-ass_status.
        APPEND gr_outtab7 TO gt_outtab7.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
  ENDLOOP.

  PERFORM get_case_add_pre88.

  IF w_ima IS INITIAL.
    CLEAR w_ima_name.
  ENDIF.

  SORT gt_outtab7 BY nachn.

ENDFORM.                    " GET_CASE_PRE88

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  GET_CASE_ADD
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM get_case_add .

  DATA: l_inc_type             TYPE zim_in_mast-inc_type,
        l_inc_status           TYPE zim_in_mast-inc_status,
        l_orgeh                TYPE pa0001-orgeh,
        l_orgeh_desc           TYPE hrp1000-stext,
        l_domvalue_l           TYPE dd07l-domvalue_l,
        l_dd07v                TYPE dd07v,
        l_cert_status          TYPE zim_mc_status,
        l_cert_type(1)         TYPE c,
        l_pa9203               TYPE pa9203,
        l_desc(30)             TYPE c,
        l_inc_inj              TYPE zim_inc_inj,
        l_locationt            TYPE zp902_locationt,
        l_access(1)            TYPE c,
        l_injury               TYPE string.

  DATA :  tb_orgeh_access   TYPE zp902_orgeh_odata_t,
          l_orgeh_access    TYPE zp902_orgeh_odata,
          tb_orgeh_range    TYPE zorgeh_range_t,
          tb_orgeh_range2   TYPE zorgeh_range_t,
          l_orgeh_range     TYPE zorgeh_range.



  IF g_user_group = 'V'.
* Retrieve the org units the person has access to
    CALL METHOD zcl_p902_class=>get_org_access_list
      IMPORTING
        tb_org_list = tb_orgeh_access.

    LOOP AT tb_orgeh_access INTO l_orgeh_access.
      CLEAR l_orgeh_range.
      l_orgeh_range-low    = l_orgeh_access-orgeh.
      l_orgeh_range-sign   = 'I'.
      l_orgeh_range-option = 'EQ'.
      APPEND l_orgeh_range TO tb_orgeh_range2.
    ENDLOOP.

    IF zim_screen-orgeh IS NOT INITIAL.
      CALL METHOD zcl_p902_class=>org_unit_range
        EXPORTING
          i_orgeh       = zim_screen-orgeh
          i_sub         = zim_screen-subordinate
        IMPORTING
          e_orgeh_range = tb_orgeh_range.
* Cater for selecting a high level org unit that has been assigned
* to the user without lower level org units included.
      LOOP AT tb_orgeh_range INTO l_orgeh_range.
        IF l_orgeh_range-low NOT IN tb_orgeh_range2.
          DELETE tb_orgeh_range.
        ENDIF.
      ENDLOOP.
    ELSE.
      tb_orgeh_range[] = tb_orgeh_range2[].
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.


  DATA: date_1 LIKE sy-datum,
               date_2 LIKE sy-datum.
  DATA days TYPE i.

  LOOP AT gt_outtab7 INTO gr_outtab7.

    PERFORM get_emp_orgeh USING    gr_outtab7-pernr
                          CHANGING gr_outtab7-orgeh.

    IF g_user_group = 'V'.
      IF gr_outtab7-orgeh NOT IN tb_orgeh_range.
        DELETE gt_outtab7.
        CONTINUE.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.

    CLEAR zim_incident. "DEVK9HR9U0       CR2414
    SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_incident
           WHERE pernr    = gr_outtab7-pernr
             AND incident = gr_outtab7-incident.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      IF g_user_group = 'V' AND
         zim_incident-higher_email IS NOT INITIAL.
* Delete the incident if the email was sent to a higher person
* and the user only has display access.
        DELETE gt_outtab7.
        CONTINUE.
      ENDIF.

      gr_outtab7-dateincident = zim_incident-dateincident.
      gr_outtab7-timeincident = zim_incident-timeincident.

      IF zim_incident-bp_number IS INITIAL.
        SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_inc_inj
               INTO l_inc_inj
               WHERE pernr       = gr_outtab7-pernr
                 AND identnr     = gr_outtab7-incident
                 AND main_injury = 'X'.
        IF sy-subrc = 0.
          gr_outtab7-main_loc = l_inc_inj-location_code.
          gr_outtab7-main_nat = l_inc_inj-nature_code.
        ELSE.
          CONCATENATE 'LOC_' zim_incident-main_loc
                      INTO w_fieldname.
          LOOP AT nametab
               WHERE fieldname = w_fieldname.
            gr_outtab7-main_injury  = nametab-fieldtext+3(50).
          ENDLOOP.
        ENDIF.
      ELSE.
        PERFORM check_get_bp_injury  USING gr_outtab7-pernr
                                           gr_outtab7-incident
                                    CHANGING l_injury.
        gr_outtab7-main_injury  = l_injury.
      ENDIF.

    ENDIF.

    SELECT SINGLE * FROM pa0002
           WHERE pernr    = gr_outtab7-pernr
             AND begda   LE sy-datum
             AND endda   GE sy-datum.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      gr_outtab7-nachn = pa0002-nachn.
      gr_outtab7-vorna = pa0002-vorna.
      PERFORM get_rank USING pa0002-titel
                             pa0002-namzu
                       CHANGING gr_outtab7-rank.

    ENDIF.

* Claim duration column to be displayed
* on the Overview screen.

*    SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_in_mast
*       WHERE pernr    = gr_outtab7-pernr
*        AND incident = gr_outtab7-incident.
*
*    IF sy-subrc = 0.
    IF NOT zim_incident-datereported IS INITIAL.
      date_1 = sy-datum.
      date_2 = zim_incident-datereported.
      days = date_1 - date_2.
      gr_outtab7-claim_dur = days / 30.
    ENDIF.
*   ENDIF.

    SELECT SINGLE * FROM pa9082
           WHERE pernr = gr_outtab7-pernr
             AND casno = gr_outtab7-incident.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      IF gr_outtab7-dateincident IS INITIAL.
        gr_outtab7-dateincident = pa9082-idate.
      ENDIF.
      SELECT SINGLE * FROM pa9076
             WHERE pernr = pa9082-pernr
               AND objps = pa9082-objps.
      IF sy-subrc = 0.
        gr_outtab7-wclmn  = pa9076-wclmn.
        gr_outtab7-stats  = pa9076-stats.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.

    SELECT SINGLE * FROM pa0573
           WHERE pernr = gr_outtab7-pernr
             AND begda LE sy-datum
             AND endda GE sy-datum
             AND ( subty = '5000'
              OR   subty = '5010'
              OR   subty = '5100'
              OR   subty = '5110'
              OR   subty = '5502'
              OR   subty = '5503'
              OR   subty = '5504'
              OR   subty = '5505'
              OR   subty = '5506' ).
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      gr_outtab7-suspended = 'Yes'.
    ENDIF.

    SELECT SINGLE * FROM pa9203
           INTO l_pa9203
           WHERE pernr          = gr_outtab7-pernr
             AND z_duty_status NE 'PID'
             AND begda         LE sy-datum
             AND endda         GE sy-datum.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      gr_outtab7-prd       = 'Yes'.
    ENDIF.

    MODIFY gt_outtab7 FROM gr_outtab7.
  ENDLOOP.

  SORT gt_outtab7 BY stats.
  CLEAR : t990a.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab7 INTO gr_outtab7.
    IF gr_outtab7-stats NE t990a-stat.
      SELECT SINGLE * FROM t990a
             WHERE langu = 'E'
               AND stat  = gr_outtab7-stats.
      IF sy-subrc NE 0.
        CLEAR t990a.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
    gr_outtab7-stats_desc = t990a-stext.
    MODIFY gt_outtab7 FROM gr_outtab7.
  ENDLOOP.


* Only allow managers or the assigned IMA to review cases
* for restricted org units.
  IF sy-tcode NE 'ZIM_MGR' AND
     sy-tcode NE 'ZIM_MDU' AND
     sy-tcode NE 'ZIM_CM' AND
     sy-tcode NE 'ZIM_MGRCM'.
    LOOP AT gt_outtab7 INTO gr_outtab7.
      IF gr_outtab7-ima NE w_user_pernr.
        IF gr_outtab7-restricted = abap_true.
          DELETE gt_outtab7.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.
    ENDLOOP.
  ENDIF.

  SORT gt_outtab7 BY inc_status.
  CLEAR : l_inc_status, w_dd07v.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab7 INTO gr_outtab7.
    IF gr_outtab7-inc_status NE l_inc_status.
      w_domvalue_l = gr_outtab7-inc_status.
      CALL FUNCTION 'DD_DOMVALUE_TEXT_GET'
        EXPORTING
          domname  = 'ZIM_INCIDENT_STATUS'
          value    = w_domvalue_l
          langu    = 'E'
        IMPORTING
          dd07v_wa = w_dd07v
          rc       = w_rc
        EXCEPTIONS
          OTHERS   = 1.
      IF  w_rc NE 0.
        CLEAR w_dd07v.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
    gr_outtab7-im_desc = w_dd07v-ddtext.
    l_inc_status = gr_outtab7-inc_status.

    MODIFY gt_outtab7 FROM gr_outtab7.
  ENDLOOP.


  SORT gt_outtab7 BY orgeh.
  CLEAR : l_orgeh, zhorgstruc.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab7 INTO gr_outtab7.
    IF gr_outtab7-orgeh NE l_orgeh.
      l_orgeh = gr_outtab7-orgeh.
      SELECT SINGLE * FROM zhorgstruc
             WHERE unit_code = gr_outtab7-orgeh
               AND vbegda   <= sy-datum
               AND vendda   >= sy-datum.
      IF sy-subrc NE 0.
        CLEAR zhorgstruc.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
    IF  zhorgstruc-lvl4_code = 0.
      gr_outtab7-lvl4_orgeh = gr_outtab7-orgeh.
    ELSE.
      gr_outtab7-lvl4_orgeh = zhorgstruc-lvl4_code.
    ENDIF.
    MODIFY gt_outtab7 FROM gr_outtab7.
  ENDLOOP.

  SORT gt_outtab7 BY lvl4_orgeh.
  CLEAR : l_orgeh, l_orgeh_desc.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab7 INTO gr_outtab7.
    IF gr_outtab7-lvl4_orgeh NE l_orgeh.
      l_orgeh = gr_outtab7-lvl4_orgeh.
      PERFORM get_objtxt  USING    l_orgeh
                                   'O'
                          CHANGING l_orgeh_desc
                                   w_short.
    ENDIF.
    gr_outtab7-lvl4_stext = l_orgeh_desc.
    MODIFY gt_outtab7 FROM gr_outtab7.
  ENDLOOP.


  SORT gt_outtab7 BY ima.
  CLEAR : pa0001.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab7 INTO gr_outtab7.
    IF gr_outtab7-ima IS NOT INITIAL.
      IF gr_outtab7-ima NE pa0001-pernr.
        SELECT SINGLE * FROM  pa0001
               WHERE  pernr  EQ gr_outtab7-ima
                 AND    endda  GE sy-datum
                 AND    begda  LE sy-datum.
        IF  sy-subrc NE 0.
          CLEAR pa0001.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.
      gr_outtab7-ima_name = pa0001-ename.
      MODIFY gt_outtab7 FROM gr_outtab7.
    ENDIF.
  ENDLOOP.

*  SORT gt_outtab7 BY ftc.
*  CLEAR : pa0001.
*  LOOP AT gt_outtab7 INTO gr_outtab7.
*    IF gr_outtab7-ftc IS NOT INITIAL.
*      IF gr_outtab7-ftc NE pa0001-pernr.
*        SELECT SINGLE * FROM  pa0001
*               WHERE  pernr  EQ gr_outtab7-ftc
*                 AND    endda  GE sy-datum
*                 AND    begda  LE sy-datum.
*        IF  sy-subrc NE 0.
*          CLEAR pa0001.
*        ENDIF.
*      ENDIF.
*      gr_outtab7-ftc_name = pa0001-ename.
*      MODIFY gt_outtab7 FROM gr_outtab7.
*    ENDIF.
*  ENDLOOP.

  LOOP AT gt_outtab7 INTO gr_outtab7.
    PERFORM get_current_cert USING    gr_outtab7-pernr
                                      gr_outtab7-incident
                             CHANGING gr_outtab7-cert_status
                                      gr_outtab7-cert_type
                                      gr_outtab7-cap_hours
                                      gr_outtab7-cap_days
                                      gr_outtab7-cap_hours2
                                      gr_outtab7-cap_days2.

    CLEAR gr_outtab7-row_colour.
    CASE gr_outtab7-cert_status.
      WHEN 'P'.
        gr_outtab7-row_colour = 'C100'.   " Blue
      WHEN 'U' OR 'N'.
        gr_outtab7-row_colour = 'C700'.   " Orange
      WHEN 'SD' OR 'C'.
        gr_outtab7-row_colour = 'C500'.   " Green
    ENDCASE.

    IF gr_outtab7-row_colour = 'C700' AND
       gr_outtab7-prim_injury = 'S'.
      gr_outtab7-row_colour = 'C600'.   " Red
    ENDIF.

    MODIFY gt_outtab7 FROM gr_outtab7.
  ENDLOOP.



  SORT gt_outtab7 BY cert_type cert_status.
  CLEAR : l_cert_status, l_desc.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab7 INTO gr_outtab7.

    IF gr_outtab7-cert_status IS NOT INITIAL.
      IF gr_outtab7-cert_status NE l_cert_status OR
         gr_outtab7-cert_type   NE l_cert_type.

        l_domvalue_l  = gr_outtab7-cert_status.
        l_cert_status = gr_outtab7-cert_status.
        l_cert_type   = gr_outtab7-cert_type.
        IF l_cert_type = 'M'.
          PERFORM get_domain_desc  USING 'ZIM_MC_STATUS'
                                         l_cert_status
                                CHANGING l_desc.
        ELSE.
          PERFORM get_domain_desc  USING 'ZIM_CAP_STATUS'
                                         l_cert_status
                                CHANGING l_desc.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.
      gr_outtab7-cert_status_desc = l_desc.

      MODIFY gt_outtab7 FROM gr_outtab7.
    ENDIF.
  ENDLOOP.

  SORT gt_outtab7 BY insurer ins_first_cont.
  CLEAR : zim_ins_cont.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab7 INTO gr_outtab7.
    IF gr_outtab7-ins_first_cont IS NOT INITIAL.
      IF gr_outtab7-insurer        NE zim_ins_cont-insurer OR
         gr_outtab7-ins_first_cont NE zim_ins_cont-contact.
        SELECT SINGLE * FROM  zim_ins_cont
               WHERE  insurer = gr_outtab7-insurer
                 AND  contact = gr_outtab7-ins_first_cont.
        IF  sy-subrc NE 0.
          CLEAR pa0001.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.
      gr_outtab7-ins_name = zim_ins_cont-name.
      MODIFY gt_outtab7 FROM gr_outtab7.
    ENDIF.
  ENDLOOP.

  SORT gt_outtab7 BY main_loc.
  CLEAR : l_locationt.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab7 INTO gr_outtab7.
    IF gr_outtab7-main_loc IS NOT INITIAL.
      IF gr_outtab7-main_loc NE l_locationt-location_code.
        SELECT SINGLE * FROM  zp902_locationt
               INTO l_locationt
               WHERE  sprsl = 'E'
                 AND  location_code = gr_outtab7-main_loc.
        IF  sy-subrc NE 0.
          CLEAR l_locationt.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.
      gr_outtab7-main_injury  = l_locationt-location_desc.
      MODIFY gt_outtab7 FROM gr_outtab7.
    ENDIF.
  ENDLOOP.


  LOOP AT gt_outtab7 INTO gr_outtab7.
    SELECT COUNT( * ) FROM zim_in_mast
           INTO gr_outtab7-inc_count
           WHERE pernr = gr_outtab7-pernr.
    MODIFY gt_outtab7 FROM gr_outtab7.
  ENDLOOP.


  SORT gt_outtab7 BY lvl4_stext
                     nachn
                     incident.


ENDFORM.                    " GET_CASE_ADD

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  GET_CASE_ADD_PRE88
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM get_case_add_pre88 .

  DATA: l_inc_type             TYPE zim_in_mast-inc_type,
        l_inc_status           TYPE zim_in_mast-inc_status,
        l_current_action       TYPE zim_scu_current_action,
        l_claim_class          TYPE zim_scu_claim_class,
        l_orgeh                TYPE pa0001-orgeh,
        l_orgeh_desc           TYPE hrp1000-stext,
        l_domvalue_l           TYPE dd07l-domvalue_l,
        l_dd07v                TYPE dd07v,
        l_cert_status          TYPE zim_mc_status,
        l_cert_type(1)         TYPE c,
        l_desc(60)             TYPE c,
        l_inc_inj              TYPE zim_inc_inj,
        l_locationt            TYPE zp902_locationt,
        l_access(1)            TYPE c.

  DATA :  tb_orgeh_access   TYPE zp902_orgeh_odata_t,
          l_orgeh_access    TYPE zp902_orgeh_odata,
          tb_orgeh_range    TYPE zorgeh_range_t,
          tb_orgeh_range2   TYPE zorgeh_range_t,
          l_orgeh_range     TYPE zorgeh_range.



  DATA: date_1 LIKE sy-datum,
               date_2 LIKE sy-datum.
  DATA days TYPE i.

  LOOP AT gt_outtab7 INTO gr_outtab7.

    PERFORM get_emp_orgeh USING    gr_outtab7-pernr
                          CHANGING gr_outtab7-orgeh.

    CLEAR zim_incident. "DEVK9HR9U0       CR2414
    SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_incident
           WHERE pernr    = gr_outtab7-pernr
             AND incident = gr_outtab7-incident.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      gr_outtab7-dateincident = zim_incident-dateincident.
      gr_outtab7-timeincident = zim_incident-timeincident.
    ENDIF.

    SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_inc_inj
           INTO l_inc_inj
           WHERE pernr       = gr_outtab7-pernr
             AND identnr     = gr_outtab7-incident
             AND main_injury = 'X'.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      gr_outtab7-main_loc = l_inc_inj-location_code.
      gr_outtab7-main_nat = l_inc_inj-nature_code.
    ELSE.
      CONCATENATE 'LOC_' zim_incident-main_loc
                  INTO w_fieldname.
      LOOP AT nametab
           WHERE fieldname = w_fieldname.
        gr_outtab7-main_injury  = nametab-fieldtext+3(50).
      ENDLOOP.
    ENDIF.

    SELECT SINGLE * FROM pa0002
           WHERE pernr    = gr_outtab7-pernr
             AND begda   LE sy-datum
             AND endda   GE sy-datum.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      gr_outtab7-nachn = pa0002-nachn.
      gr_outtab7-vorna = pa0002-vorna.
      PERFORM get_rank USING pa0002-titel
                             pa0002-namzu
                       CHANGING gr_outtab7-rank.

    ENDIF.

    SELECT SINGLE * FROM pa9082
           WHERE pernr = gr_outtab7-pernr
             AND casno = gr_outtab7-incident.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      IF gr_outtab7-dateincident IS INITIAL.
        gr_outtab7-dateincident = pa9082-idate.
      ENDIF.
      SELECT SINGLE * FROM pa9076
             WHERE pernr = pa9082-pernr
               AND objps = pa9082-objps.
      IF sy-subrc = 0.
        gr_outtab7-wclmn  = pa9076-wclmn.
        gr_outtab7-stats  = pa9076-stats.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.

    MODIFY gt_outtab7 FROM gr_outtab7.
  ENDLOOP.

  SORT gt_outtab7 BY stats.
  CLEAR : t990a.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab7 INTO gr_outtab7.
    IF gr_outtab7-stats NE t990a-stat.
      SELECT SINGLE * FROM t990a
             WHERE langu = 'E'
               AND stat  = gr_outtab7-stats.
      IF sy-subrc NE 0.
        CLEAR t990a.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
    gr_outtab7-stats_desc = t990a-stext.
    MODIFY gt_outtab7 FROM gr_outtab7.
  ENDLOOP.

  SORT gt_outtab7 BY scu_current_status.
  CLEAR : l_inc_status, l_desc.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab7 INTO gr_outtab7.
    IF gr_outtab7-scu_current_status NE l_inc_status.
      l_inc_status = gr_outtab7-scu_current_status.
      PERFORM get_domain_desc  USING 'ZIM_SCU_CURRENT_STATUS'
                                     l_inc_status
                            CHANGING l_desc.
    ENDIF.
    gr_outtab7-scu_current_status_desc = l_desc.
    MODIFY gt_outtab7 FROM gr_outtab7.
  ENDLOOP.


  SORT gt_outtab7 BY scu_ass_status.
  CLEAR : l_inc_status, l_desc.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab7 INTO gr_outtab7.
    IF gr_outtab7-scu_ass_status NE l_inc_status.
      l_inc_status = gr_outtab7-scu_ass_status.
      PERFORM get_domain_desc  USING 'ZIM_SCU_ASS_STATUS'
                                     l_inc_status
                            CHANGING l_desc.
    ENDIF.
    gr_outtab7-scu_ass_status_desc = l_desc.
    MODIFY gt_outtab7 FROM gr_outtab7.
  ENDLOOP.

  SORT gt_outtab7 BY scu_current_action.
  CLEAR : l_current_action, l_desc.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab7 INTO gr_outtab7.
    IF gr_outtab7-scu_current_action NE l_current_action.
      l_current_action = gr_outtab7-scu_current_action.
      PERFORM get_domain_desc  USING 'ZIM_SCU_CURRENT_ACTION'
                                     l_current_action
                            CHANGING l_desc.
    ENDIF.
    gr_outtab7-scu_current_action_desc = l_desc.
    MODIFY gt_outtab7 FROM gr_outtab7.
  ENDLOOP.

  SORT gt_outtab7 BY scu_claim_class.
  CLEAR : l_claim_class, l_desc.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab7 INTO gr_outtab7.
    IF gr_outtab7-scu_claim_class NE l_claim_class.
      l_claim_class = gr_outtab7-scu_claim_class.
      PERFORM get_domain_desc  USING 'ZIM_SCU_CLAIM_CLASS'
                                     l_claim_class
                            CHANGING l_desc.
    ENDIF.
    gr_outtab7-scu_claim_class_desc = l_desc.
    MODIFY gt_outtab7 FROM gr_outtab7.
  ENDLOOP.


  SORT gt_outtab7 BY orgeh.
  CLEAR l_orgeh.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab7 INTO gr_outtab7.
    IF gr_outtab7-orgeh NE l_orgeh.
      l_orgeh = gr_outtab7-orgeh.
      SELECT SINGLE * FROM zhorgstruc
             WHERE unit_code = gr_outtab7-orgeh
               AND vbegda   <= sy-datum
               AND vendda   >= sy-datum.
      IF sy-subrc NE 0.
        CLEAR zhorgstruc.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
    IF  zhorgstruc-lvl4_code = 0.
      gr_outtab7-lvl4_orgeh = gr_outtab7-orgeh.
    ELSE.
      gr_outtab7-lvl4_orgeh = zhorgstruc-lvl4_code.
    ENDIF.
    MODIFY gt_outtab7 FROM gr_outtab7.
  ENDLOOP.

  SORT gt_outtab7 BY lvl4_orgeh.
  CLEAR : l_orgeh, l_orgeh_desc.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab7 INTO gr_outtab7.
    IF gr_outtab7-lvl4_orgeh NE l_orgeh.
      l_orgeh = gr_outtab7-lvl4_orgeh.
      PERFORM get_objtxt  USING    l_orgeh
                                   'O'
                          CHANGING l_orgeh_desc
                                   w_short.
    ENDIF.
    gr_outtab7-lvl4_stext = l_orgeh_desc.
    MODIFY gt_outtab7 FROM gr_outtab7.
  ENDLOOP.


  SORT gt_outtab7 BY scu_advisor.
  CLEAR : pa0001.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab7 INTO gr_outtab7.
    IF gr_outtab7-scu_advisor IS NOT INITIAL.
      IF gr_outtab7-scu_advisor NE pa0001-pernr.
        SELECT SINGLE * FROM  pa0001
               WHERE  pernr  EQ gr_outtab7-scu_advisor
                 AND    endda  GE sy-datum
                 AND    begda  LE sy-datum.
        IF  sy-subrc NE 0.
          CLEAR pa0001.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.
      gr_outtab7-scu_name = pa0001-ename.
      MODIFY gt_outtab7 FROM gr_outtab7.
    ENDIF.
  ENDLOOP.

  LOOP AT gt_outtab7 INTO gr_outtab7.
    PERFORM get_current_cert USING    gr_outtab7-pernr
                                      gr_outtab7-incident
                             CHANGING gr_outtab7-cert_status
                                      gr_outtab7-cert_type
                                      gr_outtab7-cap_hours
                                      gr_outtab7-cap_days
                                      gr_outtab7-cap_hours2
                                      gr_outtab7-cap_days2.

    CLEAR gr_outtab7-row_colour.
    CASE gr_outtab7-cert_status.
      WHEN 'P'.
        gr_outtab7-row_colour = 'C100'.   " Blue
      WHEN 'U' OR 'N'.
        gr_outtab7-row_colour = 'C700'.   " Orange
      WHEN 'SD' OR 'C'.
        gr_outtab7-row_colour = 'C500'.   " Green
    ENDCASE.

    IF gr_outtab7-row_colour = 'C700' AND
       gr_outtab7-prim_injury = 'S'.
      gr_outtab7-row_colour = 'C600'.   " Red
    ENDIF.

    MODIFY gt_outtab7 FROM gr_outtab7.
  ENDLOOP.



  SORT gt_outtab7 BY cert_type cert_status.
  CLEAR : l_cert_status, l_desc.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab7 INTO gr_outtab7.

    IF gr_outtab7-cert_status IS NOT INITIAL.
      IF gr_outtab7-cert_status NE l_cert_status OR
         gr_outtab7-cert_type   NE l_cert_type.

        l_domvalue_l  = gr_outtab7-cert_status.
        l_cert_status = gr_outtab7-cert_status.
        l_cert_type   = gr_outtab7-cert_type.
        IF l_cert_type = 'M'.
          PERFORM get_domain_desc  USING 'ZIM_MC_STATUS'
                                         l_cert_status
                                CHANGING l_desc.
        ELSE.
          PERFORM get_domain_desc  USING 'ZIM_CAP_STATUS'
                                         l_cert_status
                                CHANGING l_desc.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.
      gr_outtab7-cert_status_desc = l_desc.

      MODIFY gt_outtab7 FROM gr_outtab7.
    ENDIF.
  ENDLOOP.

  SORT gt_outtab7 BY main_loc.
  CLEAR : l_locationt.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab7 INTO gr_outtab7.
    IF gr_outtab7-main_loc IS NOT INITIAL.
      IF gr_outtab7-main_loc NE l_locationt-location_code.
        SELECT SINGLE * FROM  zp902_locationt
               INTO l_locationt
               WHERE  sprsl = 'E'
                 AND  location_code = gr_outtab7-main_loc.
        IF  sy-subrc NE 0.
          CLEAR l_locationt.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.
      gr_outtab7-main_injury  = l_locationt-location_desc.
      MODIFY gt_outtab7 FROM gr_outtab7.
    ENDIF.
  ENDLOOP.

  SORT gt_outtab7 BY lvl4_stext
                     nachn
                     incident.


ENDFORM.                    " GET_CASE_ADD_PRE88
