FORM get_case_add_pre_md .

  DATA: l_inc_type             TYPE zim_in_mast-inc_type,
        l_inc_status           TYPE zim_in_mast-inc_status,
        l_action               TYPE zim_scu_md_action,
        l_current_action       TYPE zim_scu_current_action,
        l_orgeh                TYPE pa0001-orgeh,
        l_orgeh_desc           TYPE hrp1000-stext,
        l_domvalue_l           TYPE dd07l-domvalue_l,
        l_dd07v                TYPE dd07v,
        l_cert_status          TYPE zim_mc_status,
        l_cert_type(1)         TYPE c,
        l_desc(60)             TYPE c,
        l_inc_inj              TYPE zim_inc_inj,
        l_locationt            TYPE zp902_locationt,
        l_access(1)            TYPE c.

  DATA :  tb_orgeh_access   TYPE zp902_orgeh_odata_t,
          l_orgeh_access    TYPE zp902_orgeh_odata,
          tb_orgeh_range    TYPE zorgeh_range_t,
          tb_orgeh_range2   TYPE zorgeh_range_t,
          l_orgeh_range     TYPE zorgeh_range.



  DATA: date_1 LIKE sy-datum,
               date_2 LIKE sy-datum.
  DATA days TYPE i.

  LOOP AT gt_outtab7 INTO gr_outtab7.

    PERFORM get_emp_orgeh USING    gr_outtab7-pernr
                          CHANGING gr_outtab7-orgeh.


    SELECT SINGLE * FROM pa0002
           WHERE pernr    = gr_outtab7-pernr
             AND begda   LE sy-datum
             AND endda   GE sy-datum.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      gr_outtab7-nachn = pa0002-nachn.
      gr_outtab7-vorna = pa0002-vorna.
      PERFORM get_rank USING pa0002-titel
                             pa0002-namzu
                       CHANGING gr_outtab7-rank.

    ENDIF.

    MODIFY gt_outtab7 FROM gr_outtab7.
  ENDLOOP.

  SORT gt_outtab7 BY md_status.
  CLEAR : l_inc_status, l_desc.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab7 INTO gr_outtab7.
    IF gr_outtab7-md_status NE l_inc_status.
      l_inc_status = gr_outtab7-md_status.
      PERFORM get_domain_desc  USING 'ZIM_SCU_MD_STATUS'
                                     l_inc_status
                            CHANGING l_desc.
    ENDIF.
    gr_outtab7-md_status_desc = l_desc.
    MODIFY gt_outtab7 FROM gr_outtab7.
  ENDLOOP.


  SORT gt_outtab7 BY md_action.
  CLEAR : l_action, l_desc.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab7 INTO gr_outtab7.
    IF gr_outtab7-md_action NE l_action.
      l_action = gr_outtab7-md_action.
      PERFORM get_domain_desc  USING 'ZIM_SCU_MD_ACTION'
                                     l_action
                            CHANGING l_desc.
    ENDIF.
    gr_outtab7-md_action_desc = l_desc.
    MODIFY gt_outtab7 FROM gr_outtab7.
  ENDLOOP.


  SORT gt_outtab7 BY orgeh.
  CLEAR l_orgeh.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab7 INTO gr_outtab7.
    IF gr_outtab7-orgeh NE l_orgeh.
      l_orgeh = gr_outtab7-orgeh.
      SELECT SINGLE * FROM zhorgstruc
             WHERE unit_code = gr_outtab7-orgeh
               AND vbegda   <= sy-datum
               AND vendda   >= sy-datum.
      IF sy-subrc NE 0.
        CLEAR zhorgstruc.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
    IF  zhorgstruc-lvl4_code = 0.
      gr_outtab7-lvl4_orgeh = gr_outtab7-orgeh.
    ELSE.
      gr_outtab7-lvl4_orgeh = zhorgstruc-lvl4_code.
    ENDIF.
    MODIFY gt_outtab7 FROM gr_outtab7.
  ENDLOOP.

  SORT gt_outtab7 BY lvl4_orgeh.
  CLEAR : l_orgeh, l_orgeh_desc.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab7 INTO gr_outtab7.
    IF gr_outtab7-lvl4_orgeh NE l_orgeh.
      l_orgeh = gr_outtab7-lvl4_orgeh.
      PERFORM get_objtxt  USING    l_orgeh
                                   'O'
                          CHANGING l_orgeh_desc
                                   w_short.
    ENDIF.
    gr_outtab7-lvl4_stext = l_orgeh_desc.
    MODIFY gt_outtab7 FROM gr_outtab7.
  ENDLOOP.


  SORT gt_outtab7 BY md_officer.
  CLEAR : pa0001.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab7 INTO gr_outtab7.
    IF gr_outtab7-md_officer IS NOT INITIAL.
      IF gr_outtab7-md_officer NE pa0001-pernr.
        SELECT SINGLE * FROM  pa0001
               WHERE  pernr  EQ gr_outtab7-md_officer
                 AND    endda  GE sy-datum
                 AND    begda  LE sy-datum.
        IF  sy-subrc NE 0.
          CLEAR pa0001.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.
      gr_outtab7-md_name = pa0001-ename.
      MODIFY gt_outtab7 FROM gr_outtab7.
    ENDIF.
  ENDLOOP.


  SORT gt_outtab7 BY lvl4_stext
                     nachn
                     md_seq_no.


ENDFORM.                    " GET_CASE_ADD_PRE_MD

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  GET_CASE_ADD_CM_1
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM get_case_add_cm_1 .

  DATA: l_inc_type             TYPE zim_in_mast-inc_type,
        l_inc_status           TYPE zim_in_mast-inc_status,
        l_wc_status            TYPE zim_in_cm-wc_status,
        l_ip_type              TYPE zim_in_cm-ip_type,
        l_orgeh                TYPE pa0001-orgeh,
        l_orgeh_desc           TYPE hrp1000-stext,
        l_desc(30)             TYPE c,
        ls_in_prim             TYPE zim_in_prim,
        l_cell_colour          TYPE lvc_s_scol.

  LOOP AT gt_outtab7 INTO gr_outtab7.
    SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_incident
           WHERE pernr    = gr_outtab7-pernr
             AND incident = gr_outtab7-incident.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      gr_outtab7-dateincident = zim_incident-dateincident.
      gr_outtab7-timeincident = zim_incident-timeincident.
*      CONCATENATE 'LOC_' zim_incident-main_loc
*                  INTO w_fieldname.
*      LOOP AT nametab
*           WHERE fieldname = w_fieldname.
*        gr_outtab7-main_injury  = nametab-fieldtext+3(50).
*      ENDLOOP.
    ENDIF.

    SELECT SINGLE * FROM pa9082
           WHERE pernr = gr_outtab7-pernr
             AND casno = gr_outtab7-incident.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      IF gr_outtab7-dateincident IS INITIAL.
        gr_outtab7-dateincident = pa9082-idate.
      ENDIF.
      SELECT SINGLE * FROM pa9076
             WHERE pernr = pa9082-pernr
               AND objps = pa9082-objps.
      IF sy-subrc = 0.
        gr_outtab7-wclmn  = pa9076-wclmn.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.

    PERFORM get_emp_orgeh
      USING    gr_outtab7-pernr
      CHANGING gr_outtab7-orgeh.

    SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_in_cm
           WHERE pernr    = gr_outtab7-pernr
             AND incident = gr_outtab7-incident.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      gr_outtab7-wc_advisor  = zim_in_cm-wc_advisor.
      gr_outtab7-wc_status   = zim_in_cm-wc_status.
      gr_outtab7-ip_status   = zim_in_cm-ip_status.
      gr_outtab7-ip_advisor  = zim_in_cm-ip_advisor.
      gr_outtab7-ip_wclmn    = zim_in_cm-ip_wclmn.
      gr_outtab7-ip_type     = zim_in_cm-ip_type.
      gr_outtab7-si_wclmn    = zim_in_cm-si_wclmn.
      gr_outtab7-def_claim   = zim_in_cm-def_claim.
      gr_outtab7-cannot_work = zim_in_cm-cannot_work.

      IF gr_outtab7-cannot_work IS NOT INITIAL.
        CLEAR l_cell_colour.
        l_cell_colour-fname = 'CANNOT_WORK'.
        l_cell_colour-color-col = '6'.
*   l_cell_colour-COLOR-INT = '1'.  "1 = Intensified on, 0 = Intensified off
*   l_cell_colour-COLOR-INV = '0'.  "1 = text colour, 0 = background colour
        APPEND l_cell_colour TO gr_outtab7-cell_colour.
      ENDIF.

    ELSE.
*   Set the "unsworn claim" indicator if the Person is Unsworn.
      zcl_hr_utilities=>is_person_sworn_unsworn(
        EXPORTING
          i_pernr   = gr_outtab7-pernr
        IMPORTING
          e_unsworn = zim_in_cm-unsworn_claim ).
    ENDIF.

*<<< AZORBA EPSS BRD 03
* Implementing the EPSS logic into OLIMS.
* Set w_epss flag which will drive the logic to EPSS
* EPSS when disablement date is GE 01.10.2024 or incident has been transitioned to EPSS flag is true
    SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_in_mast
           WHERE pernr    = gr_outtab7-pernr
             AND incident = gr_outtab7-incident.
    IF zim_in_mast-hod = abap_false. " EPSS not applicable for Pre88 Police Employees
      PERFORM determine_if_epss USING gr_outtab7-pernr gr_outtab7-incident.

      IF zim_in_cm-unsworn_claim = abap_false. " Police Officers
        gr_outtab7-epss = w_epss.
        CLEAR w_epss.
        gr_outtab7-transition_to_epss = zim_in_cm-transition_to_epss.
      ENDIF. " IF zim_in_cm-unsworn_claim = abap_true.
    ENDIF. " IF zim_in_mast-hod = abap_false. " EPSS not applicable for Pre88 Police Employees
*>>> AZORBA EPSS BRD 03

    SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_address
           WHERE pernr    = gr_outtab7-pernr.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      gr_outtab7-no_corro   = zim_address-no_corro.
      IF zim_address-no_corro IS NOT INITIAL.
        CLEAR l_cell_colour.
        l_cell_colour-fname = 'NO_CORRO'.
        l_cell_colour-color-col = '6'.
*   l_cell_colour-COLOR-INT = '1'.  "1 = Intensified on, 0 = Intensified off
*   l_cell_colour-COLOR-INV = '0'.  "1 = text colour, 0 = background colour
        APPEND l_cell_colour TO gr_outtab7-cell_colour.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.

*    SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_in_claim
*           WHERE pernr    = gr_outtab7-pernr
*             AND incident = gr_outtab7-incident
*             and claim_type = 'I'
*             and begda le sy-datum
*             and endda ge sy-datum.
*    IF sy-subrc = 0.
*      gr_outtab7-ip_status  = zim_in_claim-stat.
*    ENDIF.

    MODIFY gt_outtab7 FROM gr_outtab7.
  ENDLOOP.

* CR 1167 ER 01/04/2014
* Reflect org unit/LAC
  SORT gt_outtab7 BY orgeh.
  CLEAR l_orgeh.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab7 INTO gr_outtab7.
    IF gr_outtab7-orgeh NE l_orgeh.
      l_orgeh = gr_outtab7-orgeh.
      SELECT SINGLE * FROM zhorgstruc
             WHERE unit_code = gr_outtab7-orgeh
               AND vbegda   <= sy-datum
               AND vendda   >= sy-datum.
      IF sy-subrc NE 0.
        CLEAR zhorgstruc.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
    IF  zhorgstruc-lvl3_code = 0.
      gr_outtab7-lvl3_orgeh = gr_outtab7-orgeh.
    ELSE.
      gr_outtab7-lvl3_orgeh = zhorgstruc-lvl3_code.
    ENDIF.
    IF  zhorgstruc-lvl4_code = 0.
      gr_outtab7-lvl4_orgeh = gr_outtab7-orgeh.
    ELSE.
      gr_outtab7-lvl4_orgeh = zhorgstruc-lvl4_code.
    ENDIF.
    MODIFY gt_outtab7 FROM gr_outtab7.
  ENDLOOP.

  SORT gt_outtab7 BY lvl3_orgeh.
  CLEAR : l_orgeh, l_orgeh_desc.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab7 INTO gr_outtab7.
    IF gr_outtab7-lvl3_orgeh NE l_orgeh.
      l_orgeh = gr_outtab7-lvl3_orgeh.
      PERFORM get_objtxt  USING    l_orgeh
                                   'O'
                          CHANGING l_orgeh_desc
                                   w_short.
    ENDIF.
    gr_outtab7-lvl3_stext = l_orgeh_desc.
    MODIFY gt_outtab7 FROM gr_outtab7.
  ENDLOOP.

  SORT gt_outtab7 BY lvl4_orgeh.
  CLEAR : l_orgeh, l_orgeh_desc.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab7 INTO gr_outtab7.
    IF gr_outtab7-lvl4_orgeh NE l_orgeh.
      l_orgeh = gr_outtab7-lvl4_orgeh.
      PERFORM get_objtxt  USING    l_orgeh
                                   'O'
                          CHANGING l_orgeh_desc
                                   w_short.
    ENDIF.
    gr_outtab7-lvl4_stext = l_orgeh_desc.
    MODIFY gt_outtab7 FROM gr_outtab7.
  ENDLOOP.

* end of CR 1167

  SORT gt_outtab7 BY wc_status.
  CLEAR : l_wc_status, l_desc.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab7 INTO gr_outtab7.
    IF gr_outtab7-wc_status NE l_wc_status.
      l_wc_status = gr_outtab7-wc_status.
      PERFORM get_domain_desc  USING 'ZIM_WC_STATUS'
                                     l_wc_status
                            CHANGING l_desc.
    ENDIF.
    gr_outtab7-wc_status_desc = l_desc.
    MODIFY gt_outtab7 FROM gr_outtab7.
  ENDLOOP.

  SORT gt_outtab7 BY ip_status.
  CLEAR : l_wc_status, l_desc.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab7 INTO gr_outtab7.
    IF gr_outtab7-ip_status NE l_wc_status.
      l_wc_status = gr_outtab7-ip_status.
      PERFORM get_domain_desc  USING 'ZIM_IP_CLAIM_STATUS'
                                     l_wc_status
                            CHANGING l_desc.
    ENDIF.
    gr_outtab7-ip_status_desc = l_desc.
    MODIFY gt_outtab7 FROM gr_outtab7.
  ENDLOOP.

  SORT gt_outtab7 BY ip_type.
  CLEAR : l_ip_type, l_desc.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab7 INTO gr_outtab7.
    IF gr_outtab7-ip_type NE l_ip_type.
      l_ip_type = gr_outtab7-ip_type.
      PERFORM get_domain_desc  USING 'ZIM_IP_TYPE'
                                     l_ip_type
                            CHANGING l_desc.
    ENDIF.
    gr_outtab7-ip_type_desc = l_desc.
    MODIFY gt_outtab7 FROM gr_outtab7.
  ENDLOOP.

  SORT gt_outtab7 BY wc_advisor.
  CLEAR : pa0001.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab7 INTO gr_outtab7.
    IF gr_outtab7-wc_advisor IS NOT INITIAL.
      IF gr_outtab7-wc_advisor NE pa0001-pernr.
        SELECT SINGLE * FROM  pa0001
               WHERE  pernr  EQ gr_outtab7-wc_advisor
                 AND    endda  GE sy-datum
                 AND    begda  LE sy-datum.
        IF  sy-subrc NE 0.
          CLEAR pa0001.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.
      gr_outtab7-wc_name = pa0001-ename.
      MODIFY gt_outtab7 FROM gr_outtab7.
    ENDIF.
  ENDLOOP.

  SORT gt_outtab7 BY ip_advisor.
  CLEAR : pa0001.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab7 INTO gr_outtab7.
    IF gr_outtab7-ip_advisor IS NOT INITIAL.
      IF gr_outtab7-ip_advisor NE pa0001-pernr.
        SELECT SINGLE * FROM  pa0001
               WHERE  pernr  EQ gr_outtab7-ip_advisor
                 AND    endda  GE sy-datum
                 AND    begda  LE sy-datum.
        IF  sy-subrc NE 0.
          CLEAR pa0001.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.
      gr_outtab7-ip_name = pa0001-ename.
      MODIFY gt_outtab7 FROM gr_outtab7.
    ENDIF.
  ENDLOOP.

  SORT gt_outtab7 BY incident.


* Get Primary Claim details.
*tvermeulen
  LOOP AT gt_outtab7 INTO gr_outtab7.
    gr_outtab7-prim_claim = ''.
    gr_outtab7-def_claim = ''.

    SELECT SINGLE *
      FROM zim_in_prim
      INTO ls_in_prim
      WHERE pernr = gr_outtab7-pernr
        AND incident = gr_outtab7-incident
        AND inactive NE 'X'
        AND begda <= sy-datum
        AND endda >= sy-datum.
    IF sy-subrc EQ 0.
      gr_outtab7-def_claim = ls_in_prim-prim_claim.
      gr_outtab7-prim_claim = ls_in_prim-prim_claim.
    ENDIF.
    MODIFY gt_outtab7 FROM gr_outtab7.
  ENDLOOP.



ENDFORM.                    " GET_CASE_ADD_CM_1

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  GET_CASE_ADD_CM
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM get_case_add_cm .

  DATA: l_inc_type             TYPE zim_in_mast-inc_type,
         l_status               TYPE zim_in_cm-wc_status,
         l_ip_type              TYPE zim_in_cm-ip_type,
         l_orgeh                TYPE pa0001-orgeh,
         l_orgeh_desc           TYPE hrp1000-stext,
         l_short                TYPE hrp1000-short,
         lt_220                 TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF pa0220,
         ls_220                 LIKE LINE OF           lt_220.

  LOOP AT gt_outtab7a INTO gr_outtab7a.
    SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_incident
           WHERE pernr    = gr_outtab7a-pernr
             AND incident = gr_outtab7a-incident.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      gr_outtab7a-dateincident = zim_incident-dateincident.
      gr_outtab7a-timeincident = zim_incident-timeincident.
    ENDIF.

    SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_in_mast
           WHERE pernr    = gr_outtab7a-pernr
             AND incident = gr_outtab7a-incident.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      IF zim_in_mast-hod = abap_true.
        DELETE gt_outtab7a.
        CONTINUE.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
    "CR1629 - Start of change by Ajay Jangid
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
    "Incidents created incorrectly for Contractors and Students
    "is appearing in the Workers Compensation Overview screen.
    "Following logic is to hide such records:
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
    "Read Infotype 0001 on Incident Date
    CLEAR pa0001.
    SELECT SINGLE * FROM pa0001
      WHERE pernr = gr_outtab7a-pernr
      AND   begda <= gr_outtab7a-dateincident
      AND   endda >= gr_outtab7a-dateincident.
    IF  sy-subrc = 0
    AND pa0001-persg = c_persg_g.           "G = Non Employee
      DELETE gt_outtab7a.
      CONTINUE.
    ENDIF.
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
    "CR1629 - End of change by Ajay Jangid
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*

* do not display incidents for admin, security and police covered by
* the police super scheme and anyone else who is not covered by the
* death and disability scheme.

    IF sy-dynnr = '3002'.
      SELECT SINGLE * FROM pa0220
         INTO ls_220
         WHERE pernr = gr_outtab7a-pernr
           AND fcode BETWEEN '9DDA' AND '9DDH'.

      IF sy-subrc NE 0.
*        SORT lt_220 DESCENDING BY begda.
*        READ TABLE lt_220 INTO ls_220 INDEX 1.
*        IF ls_220-fcode NOT BETWEEN '9DDA' AND '9DDH'.
*          DELETE gt_outtab7a.
*          CONTINUE.
*        ENDIF.
*      ELSE.
        DELETE gt_outtab7a.
        CONTINUE.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.

    SELECT SINGLE * FROM pa0002
           WHERE pernr    = gr_outtab7a-pernr
             AND begda   LE sy-datum
             AND endda   GE sy-datum.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      gr_outtab7a-nachn = pa0002-nachn.
      gr_outtab7a-vorna = pa0002-vorna.
    ENDIF.

    SELECT SINGLE * FROM pa9082
           WHERE pernr = gr_outtab7a-pernr
             AND casno = gr_outtab7a-incident.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      IF gr_outtab7a-dateincident IS INITIAL.
        gr_outtab7a-dateincident = pa9082-idate.
      ENDIF.
      SELECT SINGLE * FROM pa9076
             WHERE pernr = pa9082-pernr
               AND objps = pa9082-objps.
      IF sy-subrc = 0.
        gr_outtab7a-wclmn  = pa9076-wclmn.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.


* CR1155 - Reflect org unit and EML Claim Status in Workers Comp
* Overview
*    SELECT SINGLE * FROM pa0001
*      WHERE pernr = gr_outtab7a-pernr
*      AND begda LE sy-datum
*      AND endda GE sy-datum.
*
*    IF sy-subrc = 0.
*      PERFORM get_objtxt
*        USING pa0001-orgeh 'O'
*        CHANGING gr_outtab7a-org_txt w_short.
*    ELSE.
*      CLEAR: pa0001, gr_outtab7a-org_txt.
*    ENDIF.
    PERFORM get_emp_orgeh
      USING    gr_outtab7a-pernr
      CHANGING gr_outtab7a-orgeh.

    SELECT * FROM zim_in_claim
      INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE tbl_zim_in_claim
      WHERE pernr      = gr_outtab7a-pernr
      AND   incident   = gr_outtab7a-incident
      AND   claim_type = w_claim_type.

    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      SORT tbl_zim_in_claim BY begda DESCENDING.
      READ TABLE tbl_zim_in_claim INDEX 1.
      IF sy-subrc = 0.
        CLEAR : t990a.
        SELECT SINGLE * FROM t990a
          WHERE langu = 'E'
          AND   stat  = tbl_zim_in_claim-stat.
        IF sy-subrc = 0.
          gr_outtab7a-cl_stat_txt = t990a-stext.
        ELSE.
          CLEAR t990a.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.
    ELSE.
      CLEAR: zim_in_claim, gr_outtab7a-cl_stat_txt.
    ENDIF.

    MODIFY gt_outtab7a FROM gr_outtab7a.
  ENDLOOP.


  SORT gt_outtab7a BY orgeh.
  CLEAR l_orgeh.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab7a INTO gr_outtab7a.
    IF gr_outtab7a-orgeh NE l_orgeh.
      l_orgeh = gr_outtab7a-orgeh.
      SELECT SINGLE * FROM zhorgstruc
             WHERE unit_code = gr_outtab7a-orgeh
               AND vbegda   <= sy-datum
               AND vendda   >= sy-datum.
      IF sy-subrc NE 0.
        CLEAR zhorgstruc.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
    IF  zhorgstruc-lvl3_code = 0.
      gr_outtab7a-lvl3_orgeh = gr_outtab7a-orgeh.
    ELSE.
      gr_outtab7a-lvl3_orgeh = zhorgstruc-lvl3_code.
    ENDIF.
    IF  zhorgstruc-lvl4_code = 0.
      gr_outtab7a-lvl4_orgeh = gr_outtab7a-orgeh.
    ELSE.
      gr_outtab7a-lvl4_orgeh = zhorgstruc-lvl4_code.
    ENDIF.
    MODIFY gt_outtab7a FROM gr_outtab7a.
  ENDLOOP.

  SORT gt_outtab7a BY lvl3_orgeh.
  CLEAR : l_orgeh, l_orgeh_desc.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab7a INTO gr_outtab7a.
    IF gr_outtab7a-lvl3_orgeh NE l_orgeh.
      l_orgeh = gr_outtab7a-lvl3_orgeh.
      PERFORM get_objtxt  USING    l_orgeh
                                   'O'
                          CHANGING l_orgeh_desc
                                   w_short.
    ENDIF.
    gr_outtab7a-lvl3_stext = l_orgeh_desc.
    MODIFY gt_outtab7a FROM gr_outtab7a.
  ENDLOOP.

  SORT gt_outtab7a BY lvl4_orgeh.
  CLEAR : l_orgeh, l_orgeh_desc.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab7a INTO gr_outtab7a.
    IF gr_outtab7a-lvl4_orgeh NE l_orgeh.
      l_orgeh = gr_outtab7a-lvl4_orgeh.
      PERFORM get_objtxt  USING    l_orgeh
                                   'O'
                          CHANGING l_orgeh_desc
                                   w_short.
    ENDIF.
    gr_outtab7a-lvl4_stext = l_orgeh_desc.
    MODIFY gt_outtab7a FROM gr_outtab7a.
  ENDLOOP.
* End of CR1155

  SORT gt_outtab7a BY wc_status.
  CLEAR : l_status, w_dd07v.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab7a INTO gr_outtab7a.
    IF gr_outtab7a-wc_status NE l_status.
      w_domvalue_l = gr_outtab7a-wc_status.
      CALL FUNCTION 'DD_DOMVALUE_TEXT_GET'
        EXPORTING
          domname  = 'ZIM_WC_STATUS'
          value    = w_domvalue_l
          langu    = 'E'
        IMPORTING
          dd07v_wa = w_dd07v
          rc       = w_rc
        EXCEPTIONS
          OTHERS   = 1.
      IF  w_rc NE 0.
        CLEAR w_dd07v.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
    gr_outtab7a-wc_status_desc = w_dd07v-ddtext.
    l_status = gr_outtab7a-wc_status.

    MODIFY gt_outtab7a FROM gr_outtab7a.
  ENDLOOP.

  SORT gt_outtab7a BY ip_status.
  CLEAR : l_status, w_dd07v.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab7a INTO gr_outtab7a.
    IF gr_outtab7a-ip_status NE l_status.
      w_domvalue_l = gr_outtab7a-ip_status.
      CALL FUNCTION 'DD_DOMVALUE_TEXT_GET'
        EXPORTING
          domname  = 'ZIM_IP_CLAIM_STATUS'
          value    = w_domvalue_l
          langu    = 'E'
        IMPORTING
          dd07v_wa = w_dd07v
          rc       = w_rc
        EXCEPTIONS
          OTHERS   = 1.
      IF  w_rc NE 0.
        CLEAR w_dd07v.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
    gr_outtab7a-ip_status_desc = w_dd07v-ddtext.
    l_status = gr_outtab7a-ip_status.

    MODIFY gt_outtab7a FROM gr_outtab7a.
  ENDLOOP.

  SORT gt_outtab7a BY ip_type.
  CLEAR : l_ip_type, w_dd07v.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab7a INTO gr_outtab7a.
    IF gr_outtab7a-ip_type NE l_ip_type.
      w_domvalue_l = gr_outtab7a-ip_type.
      CALL FUNCTION 'DD_DOMVALUE_TEXT_GET'
        EXPORTING
          domname  = 'ZIM_IP_TYPE'
          value    = w_domvalue_l
          langu    = 'E'
        IMPORTING
          dd07v_wa = w_dd07v
          rc       = w_rc
        EXCEPTIONS
          OTHERS   = 1.
      IF  w_rc NE 0.
        CLEAR w_dd07v.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
    gr_outtab7a-ip_type_desc = w_dd07v-ddtext.
    l_ip_type = gr_outtab7a-ip_type.

    MODIFY gt_outtab7a FROM gr_outtab7a.
  ENDLOOP.


  SORT gt_outtab7a BY wc_advisor.
  CLEAR : pa0001.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab7a INTO gr_outtab7a.
    IF gr_outtab7a-wc_advisor IS NOT INITIAL.
      IF gr_outtab7a-wc_advisor NE pa0001-pernr.
        SELECT SINGLE * FROM  pa0001
               WHERE  pernr  EQ gr_outtab7a-wc_advisor
                 AND    endda  GE sy-datum
                 AND    begda  LE sy-datum.
        IF  sy-subrc NE 0.
          CLEAR pa0001.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.
      gr_outtab7a-wc_name = pa0001-ename.
      MODIFY gt_outtab7a FROM gr_outtab7a.
    ENDIF.
  ENDLOOP.

  SORT gt_outtab7a BY ip_advisor.
  CLEAR : pa0001.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab7a INTO gr_outtab7a.
    IF gr_outtab7a-ip_advisor IS NOT INITIAL.
      IF gr_outtab7a-ip_advisor NE pa0001-pernr.
        SELECT SINGLE * FROM  pa0001
               WHERE  pernr  EQ gr_outtab7a-ip_advisor
                 AND    endda  GE sy-datum
                 AND    begda  LE sy-datum.
        IF  sy-subrc NE 0.
          CLEAR pa0001.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.
      gr_outtab7a-ip_name = pa0001-ename.
      MODIFY gt_outtab7a FROM gr_outtab7a.
    ENDIF.
  ENDLOOP.

ENDFORM.                    " GET_CASE_ADD_CM

FORM get_case_act_by_emp .


  REFRESH gt_outtab7.
  SELECT * FROM zim_in_mast
         INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE gt_outtab7
         WHERE pernr = w_pernr.

  PERFORM get_case_add.

ENDFORM.                    " GET_CASE_ACT_BY_EMP

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  GET_CASE_ACT_BY_EMP_PRE88
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM get_case_act_by_emp_pre88 .


  REFRESH gt_outtab7.
  SELECT * FROM zim_in_mast
         INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE gt_outtab7
         WHERE pernr = w_pernr.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab7 INTO gr_outtab7.
    SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_in_pre88
           WHERE pernr    = gr_outtab7-pernr
             AND incident = gr_outtab7-incident.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      gr_outtab7-scu_advisor = zim_in_pre88-scu_advisor.
      gr_outtab7-scu_current_status = zim_in_pre88-current_status.
      gr_outtab7-scu_current_action = zim_in_pre88-current_action.
      gr_outtab7-scu_status_date    = zim_in_pre88-status_date.
      gr_outtab7-scu_created_date   = zim_in_pre88-created_date.
      gr_outtab7-scu_ass_status     = zim_in_pre88-ass_status.
      gr_outtab7-scu_claim_class    = zim_in_pre88-claim_class.
      MODIFY gt_outtab7 FROM gr_outtab7.
    ENDIF.
  ENDLOOP.

  PERFORM get_case_add_pre88.

ENDFORM.                    " GET_CASE_ACT_BY_EMP_PRE88

  PERFORM get_case_add_cm_1.


ENDFORM.                    " GET_CASE_ACT_BY_EMP_CM

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  GET_CASE_ACT_BY_EMP_GAO
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM get_case_act_by_emp_gao .


  REFRESH gt_outtab7.
  SELECT * FROM zim_in_mast
         INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE gt_outtab7
         WHERE pernr = w_pernr.

  DATA: l_inc_type             TYPE zim_in_mast-inc_type,
         l_inc_status           TYPE zim_in_mast-inc_status,
         l_wc_status            TYPE zim_in_cm-wc_status,
         l_orgeh                TYPE pa0001-orgeh,
         l_orgeh_desc           TYPE hrp1000-stext,
         l_desc(30)             TYPE c,
         ls_in_prim             TYPE zim_in_prim.


  LOOP AT gt_outtab7 INTO gr_outtab7.
    SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_incident
           WHERE pernr    = gr_outtab7-pernr
             AND incident = gr_outtab7-incident.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      gr_outtab7-dateincident = zim_incident-dateincident.
      gr_outtab7-timeincident = zim_incident-timeincident.
      CONCATENATE 'LOC_' zim_incident-main_loc
                  INTO w_fieldname.
      LOOP AT nametab
           WHERE fieldname = w_fieldname.
        gr_outtab7-main_injury  = nametab-fieldtext+3(50).
      ENDLOOP.
    ENDIF.

    SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_in_cm
           WHERE pernr    = gr_outtab7-pernr
             AND incident = gr_outtab7-incident.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      gr_outtab7-wc_status = zim_in_cm-wc_status.
      gr_outtab7-def_claim = zim_in_cm-def_claim.
    ENDIF.

    SELECT SINGLE * FROM pa0002
           WHERE pernr    = gr_outtab7-pernr
             AND begda   LE sy-datum
             AND endda   GE sy-datum.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      gr_outtab7-nachn = pa0002-nachn.
      gr_outtab7-vorna = pa0002-vorna.
    ENDIF.

    PERFORM get_emp_orgeh USING    gr_outtab7-pernr
                          CHANGING gr_outtab7-orgeh.

    SELECT SINGLE * FROM pa9082
           WHERE pernr = gr_outtab7-pernr
             AND casno = gr_outtab7-incident.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      IF gr_outtab7-dateincident IS INITIAL.
        gr_outtab7-dateincident = pa9082-idate.
      ENDIF.
      SELECT SINGLE * FROM pa9076
             WHERE pernr = pa9082-pernr
               AND objps = pa9082-objps.
      IF sy-subrc = 0.
        gr_outtab7-wclmn  = pa9076-wclmn.
        gr_outtab7-stats  = pa9076-stats.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.

    MODIFY gt_outtab7 FROM gr_outtab7.
  ENDLOOP.

  SORT gt_outtab7 BY stats.
  CLEAR : t990a.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab7 INTO gr_outtab7.
    IF gr_outtab7-stats NE t990a-stat.
      SELECT SINGLE * FROM t990a
             WHERE langu = 'E'
               AND stat  = gr_outtab7-stats.
      IF sy-subrc NE 0.
        CLEAR t990a.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
    gr_outtab7-stats_desc = t990a-stext.
    MODIFY gt_outtab7 FROM gr_outtab7.
  ENDLOOP.

  SORT gt_outtab7 BY wc_status.
  CLEAR : l_wc_status, l_desc.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab7 INTO gr_outtab7.
    IF gr_outtab7-wc_status NE l_wc_status.
      l_wc_status = gr_outtab7-wc_status.
      PERFORM get_domain_desc  USING 'ZIM_WC_STATUS'
                                     l_wc_status
                            CHANGING l_desc.
    ENDIF.
    gr_outtab7-wc_status_desc = l_desc.
    MODIFY gt_outtab7 FROM gr_outtab7.
  ENDLOOP.


  SORT gt_outtab7 BY orgeh.
  CLEAR l_orgeh.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab7 INTO gr_outtab7.
    IF gr_outtab7-orgeh NE l_orgeh.
      l_orgeh = gr_outtab7-orgeh.
      SELECT SINGLE * FROM zhorgstruc
             WHERE unit_code = gr_outtab7-orgeh
               AND vbegda   <= sy-datum
               AND vendda   >= sy-datum.
      IF sy-subrc NE 0.
        CLEAR zhorgstruc.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
    IF  zhorgstruc-lvl4_code = 0.
      gr_outtab7-lvl4_orgeh = gr_outtab7-orgeh.
    ELSE.
      gr_outtab7-lvl4_orgeh = zhorgstruc-lvl4_code.
    ENDIF.
    MODIFY gt_outtab7 FROM gr_outtab7.
  ENDLOOP.

  SORT gt_outtab7 BY lvl4_orgeh.
  CLEAR : l_orgeh, l_orgeh_desc.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab7 INTO gr_outtab7.
    IF gr_outtab7-lvl4_orgeh NE l_orgeh.
      l_orgeh = gr_outtab7-lvl4_orgeh.
      PERFORM get_objtxt  USING    l_orgeh
                                   'O'
                          CHANGING l_orgeh_desc
                                   w_short.
    ENDIF.
    gr_outtab7-lvl4_stext = l_orgeh_desc.
    MODIFY gt_outtab7 FROM gr_outtab7.
  ENDLOOP.

  SORT gt_outtab7 BY lvl4_stext
                     nachn
                     incident.

* Get Primary Claim details.
*tvermeulen



  LOOP AT gt_outtab7 INTO gr_outtab7.
    gr_outtab7-prim_claim = ''.
    gr_outtab7-def_claim = ''.

    SELECT SINGLE *
      FROM zim_in_prim
      INTO ls_in_prim
      WHERE pernr = gr_outtab7-pernr
        AND incident = gr_outtab7-incident
        AND inactive NE 'X'
        AND begda <= sy-datum
        AND endda >= sy-datum.
    IF sy-subrc EQ 0.
      gr_outtab7-def_claim = ls_in_prim-prim_claim.
      gr_outtab7-prim_claim = ls_in_prim-prim_claim.
    ENDIF.
    MODIFY gt_outtab7 FROM gr_outtab7.
  ENDLOOP.




ENDFORM.                    " GET_CASE_ACT_BY_EMP_GAO

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  GET_FTC_CASES
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM get_ftc_cases .


  REFRESH gt_outtab7.

  IF zim_screen-ftc_status = 'F' OR
     zim_screen-ftc_status = ' '.
    SELECT * FROM zim_in_mast
           INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE gt_outtab7
           WHERE inc_status = 'F'.

    LOOP AT gt_outtab7 INTO gr_outtab7.
      gr_outtab7-status_desc = 'FTC Process'.
      MODIFY gt_outtab7 FROM gr_outtab7.
    ENDLOOP.
  ENDIF.

  IF zim_screen-ftc_status = 'I' OR
     zim_screen-ftc_status = ' '.
    SELECT * FROM zim_in_imep
           WHERE report_rec = ' '
             AND ftco_date  = '00000000'.
      SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_in_mast
             WHERE pernr = zim_in_imep-pernr
               AND incident = zim_in_imep-incident.
      IF zim_in_mast-inc_status NE 'C'.
        CLEAR gr_outtab7.
        MOVE-CORRESPONDING zim_in_mast TO gr_outtab7.
        gr_outtab7-seq_no      = zim_in_imep-seq_no.
        gr_outtab7-status_desc = 'IMEP Review'.
        APPEND gr_outtab7 TO gt_outtab7.
      ENDIF.
    ENDSELECT.
  ENDIF.

  IF w_ftco IS NOT INITIAL.
    LOOP AT gt_outtab7 INTO gr_outtab7.
      IF gr_outtab7-ftc NE w_ftco.
        DELETE gt_outtab7.
      ENDIF.
    ENDLOOP.
  ENDIF.

  LOOP AT gt_outtab7 INTO gr_outtab7.
    SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_in_ftc
           WHERE pernr = gr_outtab7-pernr
             AND incident = gr_outtab7-incident.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      CLEAR zim_in_ftc.
    ENDIF.
    gr_outtab7-md_rpt_comp = zim_in_ftc-md_rpt_comp.
    MODIFY gt_outtab7 FROM gr_outtab7.
  ENDLOOP.

  PERFORM get_case_add.

  SORT gt_outtab7 BY ftco_date DESCENDING.


ENDFORM.                    " GET_FTC_CASES

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  GET_FTC_CASES2
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM get_ftc_cases2.


  REFRESH gt_outtab7.

  SELECT * FROM zim_in_mast
         INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE gt_outtab7
         WHERE inc_status = 'F'.

  LOOP AT gt_outtab7 INTO gr_outtab7.
    SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_in_ftc
           WHERE pernr    = gr_outtab7-pernr
             AND incident = gr_outtab7-incident.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      DELETE gt_outtab7.
    ELSE.
      IF zim_in_ftc-case_status NE 'R'.
        DELETE gt_outtab7.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
  ENDLOOP.

  PERFORM get_case_add.


ENDFORM.                    " GET_FTC_CASES2

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  GET_MDU_CASES
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM get_mdu_cases USING p_from.

  DATA : tb_md_pre           TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF zim_md_pre,
         l_md_pre            TYPE zim_md_pre,
         l_in_mast           TYPE zim_in_mast.

  PERFORM get_employee USING    sy-uname
                       CHANGING w_user_pernr
                                w_user_name.

  IF p_from = '1' AND
   ( zim_screen-premd_screen_stat = 'N' OR
     zim_screen-premd_screen_stat = 'C' OR
     zim_screen-premd_screen_stat = 'W' OR
     zim_screen-premd_screen_stat = 'E' ).
    SELECT * FROM zim_md_pre
            INTO TABLE tb_md_pre.
  ELSEIF p_from = '1'.
    SELECT * FROM zim_md_pre
            INTO TABLE tb_md_pre
            WHERE closed_date = '00000000'.
  ELSE.
    SELECT * FROM zim_md_pre
            INTO TABLE tb_md_pre
            WHERE pernr = w_pernr.
  ENDIF.

  REFRESH gt_outtab7.
  LOOP AT tb_md_pre INTO l_md_pre.
    IF  ( w_ima IS INITIAL AND
          zim_screen-premd_screen_stat IS INITIAL ) OR
        ( w_ima = l_md_pre-md_officer AND
          zim_screen-premd_screen_stat IS INITIAL ) OR
        ( ( zim_screen-premd_screen_stat = l_md_pre-status OR
            ( zim_screen-premd_screen_stat = 'M' AND
              ( l_md_pre-current_action = '01' OR
                l_md_pre-current_action = '20' OR
                l_md_pre-current_action = '30' ) ) OR
            ( zim_screen-premd_screen_stat = 'D' AND
              ( l_md_pre-current_action = '02' OR
                l_md_pre-current_action = '21' OR
                l_md_pre-current_action = '31' ) ) OR
            ( zim_screen-premd_screen_stat = 'O' AND
              l_md_pre-current_action IS NOT INITIAL AND
              l_md_pre-current_action NE '01'        AND
              l_md_pre-current_action NE '02'        AND
              l_md_pre-current_action NE '20'        AND
              l_md_pre-current_action NE '21'        AND
              l_md_pre-current_action NE '30'        AND
              l_md_pre-current_action NE '31'        ) ) AND
        ( w_ima IS INITIAL OR
          w_ima = l_md_pre-md_officer ) ) OR
          p_from = '2'.

      CLEAR gr_outtab7.
      gr_outtab7-pernr              = l_md_pre-pernr.
      gr_outtab7-md_officer         = l_md_pre-md_officer.
      gr_outtab7-md_status          = l_md_pre-status.
      gr_outtab7-md_action          = l_md_pre-current_action.
      gr_outtab7-md_seq_no          = l_md_pre-seq_no.
      gr_outtab7-md_created_date    = l_md_pre-created_date.
      gr_outtab7-md_closed_date     = l_md_pre-closed_date.
      APPEND gr_outtab7 TO gt_outtab7.
    ENDIF.
  ENDLOOP.


  PERFORM get_case_add_pre_md.

  IF w_ima IS INITIAL.
    CLEAR w_ima_name.
  ENDIF.

  SORT gt_outtab7 BY nachn.


ENDFORM.                    " GET_MDU_CASES

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  GET_WC_CASE
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM get_wc_case .

  PERFORM get_employee USING    sy-uname
                       CHANGING w_user_pernr
                                w_user_name.

  REFRESH gt_outtab7a.
  IF w_wc IS NOT INITIAL AND
     w_wc_status = 'N'.
    SELECT * FROM zim_in_cm
           INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE gt_outtab7a
           WHERE wc_status  = ' '
             AND wc_advisor = w_wc.
  ELSEIF w_wc IS NOT INITIAL AND
     w_wc_status NE 'A'.
    IF w_wc_status NE 'O'.
      SELECT * FROM zim_in_cm
             INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE gt_outtab7a
             WHERE wc_status  = w_wc_status
               AND wc_advisor = w_wc.
    ELSE.
      SELECT * FROM zim_in_cm
             INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE gt_outtab7a
             WHERE ( wc_status  = w_wc_status
                OR   wc_status  = 'R' )
               AND wc_advisor = w_wc.
    ENDIF.
  ELSEIF w_wc IS NOT INITIAL AND
     w_wc_status = 'A'.
    SELECT * FROM zim_in_cm
           INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE gt_outtab7a
           WHERE wc_advisor = w_wc.
  ELSEIF w_wc IS INITIAL AND
     w_wc_status = 'N'.
    SELECT * FROM zim_in_cm
           INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE gt_outtab7a
           WHERE wc_status = ' '.
  ELSEIF w_wc IS INITIAL AND
     w_wc_status NE 'A'.
    IF w_wc_status NE 'O'.
      SELECT * FROM zim_in_cm
             INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE gt_outtab7a
             WHERE wc_status = w_wc_status.
    ELSE.
      SELECT * FROM zim_in_cm
            INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE gt_outtab7a
            WHERE wc_status = w_wc_status
               OR wc_status = 'R'.
    ENDIF.
  ELSEIF w_wc IS INITIAL AND
      w_wc_status = 'A'.
    SELECT * FROM zim_in_cm
           INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE gt_outtab7a.
  ENDIF.

  PERFORM get_case_add_cm.

  IF w_wc IS INITIAL.
    CLEAR w_wc_name_old.
  ENDIF.

  SORT gt_outtab7a BY nachn.

ENDFORM.                    " GET_WC_CASE

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  GET_SERVICE_REQUEST
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM get_service_request.

  DATA: l_inc_type             TYPE zim_in_mast-inc_type,
         l_inc_status           TYPE zim_in_mast-inc_status,
         l_orgeh                TYPE pa0001-orgeh,
         l_orgeh_desc           TYPE hrp1000-stext.


  REFRESH gt_outtab8.
  IF w_request = 'E'.
    SELECT * FROM zim_in_prov
           INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE gt_outtab8
           WHERE pernr    = zim_in_mast-pernr
             AND incident = zim_in_mast-incident.
  ELSEIF w_request = 'R'.
    SELECT * FROM zim_in_rosa
           INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE gt_outtab8
           WHERE pernr    = zim_in_mast-pernr
             AND incident = zim_in_mast-incident.
  ELSEIF w_request = 'P'.
    SELECT * FROM zim_in_pmo
           INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE gt_outtab8
           WHERE pernr    = zim_in_mast-pernr
             AND incident = zim_in_mast-incident.
  ENDIF.

  SORT gt_outtab8 BY seq_no.
  IF w_request = 'E'.
    LOOP AT gt_outtab8 INTO gr_outtab8.
      SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_prov
             WHERE prov_code = gr_outtab8-prov_code.
      IF sy-subrc = 0.
        gr_outtab8-company    = zim_prov-company.
        gr_outtab8-consultant = zim_prov-consultant.
        gr_outtab8-service    = zim_prov-service.
        gr_outtab8-phone      = zim_prov-phone.
        gr_outtab8-fax        = zim_prov-fax.
        gr_outtab8-mobile     = zim_prov-mobile.
        gr_outtab8-email      = zim_prov-email.
      ENDIF.
      w_domvalue_l = gr_outtab8-prov_activity.
      w_domname = 'ZIM_PROV_ACTIVITY'.
      CALL FUNCTION 'DD_DOMVALUE_TEXT_GET'
        EXPORTING
          domname  = w_domname
          value    = w_domvalue_l
          langu    = 'E'
        IMPORTING
          dd07v_wa = w_dd07v
          rc       = w_rc
        EXCEPTIONS
          OTHERS   = 1.
      IF  w_rc = 0.
        gr_outtab8-activity_desc = w_dd07v-ddtext.
      ENDIF.

      MODIFY gt_outtab8 FROM gr_outtab8.
    ENDLOOP.
  ELSEIF w_request = 'R' OR
         w_request = 'P'.
    LOOP AT gt_outtab8 INTO gr_outtab8.
      w_domvalue_l = gr_outtab8-status.
      IF w_request = 'R'.
        w_domname = 'ZIM_ROSA_STATUS'.
      ELSE.
        w_domname = 'ZIM_PMO_STATUS'.
      ENDIF.
      CALL FUNCTION 'DD_DOMVALUE_TEXT_GET'
        EXPORTING
          domname  = w_domname
          value    = w_domvalue_l
          langu    = 'E'
        IMPORTING
          dd07v_wa = w_dd07v
          rc       = w_rc
        EXCEPTIONS
          OTHERS   = 1.
      IF  w_rc = 0.
        gr_outtab8-status_desc = w_dd07v-ddtext.
      ENDIF.
      MODIFY gt_outtab8 FROM gr_outtab8.
    ENDLOOP.
  ENDIF.

  LOOP AT gt_outtab8 INTO gr_outtab8.

    CONCATENATE zim_in_mast-pernr zim_in_mast-incident
                'EXT' w_request gr_outtab8-seq_no
                INTO w_text_key.
    PERFORM check_text      USING    w_text_key
                            CHANGING w_exists.
    IF w_exists = abap_true.
      gr_outtab8-desc_icon = icon_text_act.
    ELSE.
      gr_outtab8-desc_icon = icon_text_ina.
    ENDIF.
    gr_outtab8-email_icon = icon_mail.
    MODIFY gt_outtab8 FROM gr_outtab8.
  ENDLOOP.

  gt_outtab8_old[] = gt_outtab8[].
  CLEAR : zim_prov, zim_screen_serv_req.

ENDFORM.                    " GET_SERVICE_REQUEST

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  GET_REHAB
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM get_rehab.

  DATA: l_inc_type             TYPE zim_in_mast-inc_type,
         l_inc_status           TYPE zim_in_mast-inc_status,
         l_orgeh                TYPE pa0001-orgeh,
         l_orgeh_desc           TYPE hrp1000-stext.


  REFRESH gt_outtab9.
  SELECT * FROM zim_in_rehab
         INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE gt_outtab9
           WHERE pernr    = zim_in_mast-pernr
             AND incident = zim_in_mast-incident.

  SORT gt_outtab9 BY seq_no.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab9 INTO gr_outtab9.
    PERFORM get_objtxt  USING    gr_outtab9-orgeh
                                 'O'
                        CHANGING gr_outtab9-orgeh_desc
                                 w_short.

    CONCATENATE zim_in_mast-pernr zim_in_mast-incident
                'REHAB' gr_outtab9-seq_no
                INTO w_text_key.
    PERFORM check_text      USING    w_text_key
                            CHANGING w_exists.
    IF w_exists = abap_true.
      gr_outtab9-desc_icon = icon_text_act.
    ELSE.
      gr_outtab9-desc_icon = icon_text_ina.
    ENDIF.
    gr_outtab9-email_icon = icon_mail.
    MODIFY gt_outtab9 FROM gr_outtab9.
  ENDLOOP.

  gt_outtab9_old[] = gt_outtab9[].

ENDFORM.                    " GET_REHAB

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  GET_ERG
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM get_erg.

  DATA: l_inc_type             TYPE zim_in_mast-inc_type,
        l_inc_status           TYPE zim_in_mast-inc_status,
        l_orgeh                TYPE pa0001-orgeh,
        l_orgeh_desc           TYPE hrp1000-stext,
        l_doc_unique           TYPE zim_doc_unique.


  REFRESH gt_outtab10.
  SELECT * FROM zim_in_erg
         INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE gt_outtab10
           WHERE pernr    = zim_in_mast-pernr.

  SORT gt_outtab10 BY seq_no.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab10 INTO gr_outtab10.
    CONCATENATE zim_in_mast-pernr zim_in_mast-incident
                'ERG' gr_outtab10-seq_no
                INTO w_text_key.
    PERFORM check_text      USING    w_text_key
                            CHANGING w_exists.
    IF w_exists = abap_true.
      gr_outtab10-desc_icon = icon_text_act.
    ELSE.
      gr_outtab10-desc_icon = icon_text_ina.
    ENDIF.
    gr_outtab10-email_icon = icon_mail.
    gr_outtab10-imp_icon1  = icon_import.
    gr_outtab10-imp_icon2  = icon_import.

    CONCATENATE 'ERGC' gr_outtab10-seq_no
                INTO l_doc_unique.
    SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_in_doc_in
           WHERE pernr        = gr_outtab10-pernr
             AND incident     = gr_outtab10-incident
             AND ltr_category = '13'
             AND doc_unique   = l_doc_unique.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      gr_outtab10-disp_icon1  = icon_pdf.
      gr_outtab10-att_doc_id1 = zim_in_doc_in-att_doc_id.
    ENDIF.

    SELECT COUNT( * ) FROM zim_in_doc_in
       INTO gr_outtab10-count_1
       WHERE pernr        = gr_outtab10-pernr
         AND incident     = gr_outtab10-incident
         AND ltr_category = '13'.

    CONCATENATE 'ERGA' gr_outtab10-seq_no
                INTO l_doc_unique.
    SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_in_doc_in
           WHERE pernr        = gr_outtab10-pernr
             AND incident     = gr_outtab10-incident
             AND ltr_category = '16'
             AND doc_unique   = l_doc_unique.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      gr_outtab10-disp_icon2  = icon_pdf.
      gr_outtab10-att_doc_id2 = zim_in_doc_in-att_doc_id.
    ENDIF.

    SELECT COUNT( * ) FROM zim_in_doc_in
       INTO gr_outtab10-count_2
       WHERE pernr        = gr_outtab10-pernr
         AND incident     = gr_outtab10-incident
         AND ltr_category = '16'.

    MODIFY gt_outtab10 FROM gr_outtab10.
  ENDLOOP.

  SORT gt_outtab10 BY inspect_emp.
  CLEAR pa0001.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab10 INTO gr_outtab10.
    IF gr_outtab10-inspect_emp NE pa0001-pernr.
      SELECT SINGLE * FROM pa0001
             WHERE pernr = gr_outtab10-inspect_emp
               AND begda LE sy-datum
               AND endda GE sy-datum.
      IF sy-subrc NE 0.
        CLEAR pa0001.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
    gr_outtab10-ename = pa0001-ename.
    MODIFY gt_outtab10 FROM gr_outtab10.
  ENDLOOP.

  SORT gt_outtab10 BY incident seq_no.

  LOOP AT gt_outtab10 INTO gr_outtab10.
    PERFORM disable_erg_fields.
    MODIFY gt_outtab10 FROM gr_outtab10.
  ENDLOOP.


  gt_outtab10_old[] = gt_outtab10[].

ENDFORM.                    " GET_ERG

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  GET_ERG_2
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM get_erg_2.

  DATA: l_inc_type             TYPE zim_in_mast-inc_type,
         l_inc_status           TYPE zim_in_mast-inc_status,
         l_orgeh                TYPE pa0001-orgeh,
         l_orgeh_desc           TYPE hrp1000-stext.


  REFRESH gt_outtab10.
  IF w_ima IS INITIAL.
    SELECT * FROM zim_in_erg
           INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE gt_outtab10
             WHERE inspect_req  = 'Y'
               AND inspect_comp = '00000000'.
  ELSE.
    SELECT * FROM zim_in_erg
           INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE gt_outtab10
             WHERE inspect_req  = 'Y'
               AND inspect_emp  = w_ima
               AND inspect_comp = '00000000'.
  ENDIF.

  SORT gt_outtab10 BY pernr incident.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab10 INTO gr_outtab10.
    SELECT SINGLE * FROM pa0002
           WHERE pernr = gr_outtab10-pernr
             AND begda LE sy-datum
             AND endda GE sy-datum.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      gr_outtab10-vorna = pa0002-vorna.
      gr_outtab10-nachn = pa0002-nachn.
    ENDIF.
    CONCATENATE gr_outtab10-pernr gr_outtab10-incident
                'ERG' gr_outtab10-seq_no
                INTO w_text_key.
    PERFORM check_text      USING    w_text_key
                            CHANGING w_exists.
    IF w_exists = abap_true.
      gr_outtab10-desc_icon = icon_text_act.
    ELSE.
      gr_outtab10-desc_icon = icon_text_ina.
    ENDIF.
    SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_in_doc_in
           WHERE pernr = gr_outtab10-pernr
             AND incident = gr_outtab10-incident
             AND ltr_category = '13'.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      gr_outtab10-imp_icon1   = icon_doc.
      gr_outtab10-att_doc_id1 = zim_in_doc_in-att_doc_id.
    ENDIF.
    MODIFY gt_outtab10 FROM gr_outtab10.
  ENDLOOP.

  SORT gt_outtab10 BY inspect_emp.
  CLEAR pa0001.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab10 INTO gr_outtab10.
    IF gr_outtab10-inspect_emp NE pa0001-pernr.
      SELECT SINGLE * FROM pa0001
             WHERE pernr = gr_outtab10-inspect_emp
               AND begda LE sy-datum
               AND endda GE sy-datum.
      IF sy-subrc NE 0.
        CLEAR pa0001.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
    gr_outtab10-ename = pa0001-ename.
    MODIFY gt_outtab10 FROM gr_outtab10.
  ENDLOOP.

  SORT gt_outtab10 BY pernr incident.

  gt_outtab10_old[] = gt_outtab10[].

ENDFORM.                                                    " GET_ERG_2

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  GET_MEETING
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM get_meeting USING p_type.

  DATA: l_meet_desc(60)       TYPE c,
         l_meet_type           TYPE zim_in_mtg-meet_type.

  REFRESH gt_outtab11.
  IF p_type NE 'A'.
    SELECT * FROM zim_in_mtg
           INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE gt_outtab11
             WHERE pernr     = zim_in_mast-pernr
               AND incident  = zim_in_mast-incident
               AND meet_type = p_type.
  ELSE.
    SELECT * FROM zim_in_mtg
           INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE gt_outtab11
             WHERE pernr     = zim_in_mast-pernr
               AND incident  = zim_in_mast-incident
               AND meet_type IN g_display_m_range.
    CLEAR : l_meet_type, l_meet_desc.
    SORT gt_outtab11 BY meet_type.
    LOOP AT gt_outtab11 INTO gr_outtab11.
      IF gr_outtab11-meet_type NE l_meet_type.
        PERFORM get_domain_desc  USING 'ZIM_MEET_TYPE'
                                       gr_outtab11-meet_type
                              CHANGING l_meet_desc.
      ENDIF.
      l_meet_type = gr_outtab11-meet_type.
      gr_outtab11-meet_type_desc = l_meet_desc.
      MODIFY gt_outtab11 FROM gr_outtab11.
    ENDLOOP.
  ENDIF.

  LOOP AT gt_outtab11 INTO gr_outtab11.
    gr_outtab11-copy_icon = icon_create_copy.
    MODIFY gt_outtab11 FROM gr_outtab11.
  ENDLOOP.

  SORT gt_outtab11 BY meet_date DESCENDING.

ENDFORM.                    " GET_MEETING

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  GET_EXT_ACTIVITY
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM get_ext_activity USING p_type.

  DATA: l_ext_desc(60)       TYPE c,
         l_ext_type           TYPE zim_in_ext-ext_type,
         l_doc_unique         TYPE zim_in_doc_in-doc_unique.

  REFRESH gt_outtab36.
  IF p_type NE 'A'.
    SELECT * FROM zim_in_ext
           INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE gt_outtab36
             WHERE pernr     = zim_in_mast-pernr
               AND incident  = zim_in_mast-incident
               AND ext_type  = p_type.
  ELSE.
    SELECT * FROM zim_in_ext
           INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE gt_outtab36
             WHERE pernr     = zim_in_mast-pernr
               AND incident  = zim_in_mast-incident.
    CLEAR : l_ext_type, l_ext_desc.
    SORT gt_outtab36 BY ext_type.
    LOOP AT gt_outtab36 INTO gr_outtab36.
      IF gr_outtab36-ext_type NE l_ext_type.
        PERFORM get_domain_desc  USING 'ZIM_EXT_TYPE'
                                       gr_outtab36-ext_type
                              CHANGING l_ext_desc.
      ENDIF.
      l_ext_type = gr_outtab36-ext_type.
      gr_outtab36-ext_desc = l_ext_desc.
      MODIFY gt_outtab36 FROM gr_outtab36.
    ENDLOOP.
  ENDIF.

  LOOP AT gt_outtab36 INTO gr_outtab36.
    IF w_3400_action NE 'DISP'.
      gr_outtab36-upd_icon = icon_change.
    ELSE.
      gr_outtab36-upd_icon = icon_display.
    ENDIF.
    gr_outtab36-del_icon   = icon_delete.
    gr_outtab36-imp_icon   = icon_import.

    CONCATENATE 'EXT' gr_outtab36-seq_no
                INTO l_doc_unique.
    SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_in_doc_in
           WHERE pernr      = gr_outtab36-pernr
             AND incident   = gr_outtab36-incident
             AND doc_unique = l_doc_unique.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      gr_outtab36-doc_unique = l_doc_unique.
      gr_outtab36-doc_icon   = icon_text_act.
    ENDIF.

    MODIFY gt_outtab36 FROM gr_outtab36.
  ENDLOOP.

  SORT gt_outtab36 BY ext_date DESCENDING.

ENDFORM.                    " GET_EXT_ACTIVITY

             WHERE pernr     = zim_in_mast-pernr

  ELSE.
    SELECT * FROM zim_in_mtg
           INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE gt_outtab112
             WHERE pernr     = zim_in_mast-pernr
               AND incident  = zim_in_mast-incident.

ENDFORM.                    " GET_MEETING

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  GET_MC
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM get_mc.

  DATA: l_category         TYPE zim_in_cert-category,
         l_status           TYPE zim_in_cert-status,
         l_desc(60)         TYPE c,
         l_doc_unique       TYPE zim_in_doc_in-doc_unique.

  REFRESH gt_outtab19.
  SELECT * FROM zim_in_cert
         INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE gt_outtab19
           WHERE pernr     = zim_in_mast-pernr
             AND incident  = zim_in_mast-incident.

  SORT gt_outtab19 BY category.
  CLEAR : l_category, l_desc.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab19 INTO gr_outtab19.
    IF gr_outtab19-category NE l_category.
      l_category = gr_outtab19-category.
      PERFORM get_domain_desc  USING 'ZIM_MC_CATEGORY'
                                     l_category
                            CHANGING l_desc.
    ENDIF.
    gr_outtab19-cat_desc = l_desc.

    IF w_3100_action NE 'DISP' AND
       w_2200_action NE 'DISP' AND
       w_3600_action NE 'DISP'.
      gr_outtab19-upd_icon = icon_change.
    ELSE.
      gr_outtab19-upd_icon = icon_display.
    ENDIF.

    IF w_4001_action IS NOT INITIAL.
      gr_outtab19-upd_icon = icon_display.
    ENDIF.

    gr_outtab19-copy_icon  = icon_create_copy.

    IF gr_outtab19-status NE 'P' AND
       gr_outtab19-inactive IS INITIAL.
      gr_outtab19-gen_icon   = icon_workflow_doc_create.
    ENDIF.
    gr_outtab19-imp_icon   = icon_import.

    CONCATENATE 'MC' gr_outtab19-seq_no
                INTO l_doc_unique.
    SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_in_doc_in
           WHERE pernr      = gr_outtab19-pernr
             AND incident   = gr_outtab19-incident
             AND doc_unique = l_doc_unique.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      gr_outtab19-doc_unique = l_doc_unique.
      gr_outtab19-doc_icon   = icon_text_act.
      gr_outtab19-email_icon = icon_mail.
    ENDIF.

    MODIFY gt_outtab19 FROM gr_outtab19.
  ENDLOOP.

  SORT gt_outtab19 BY status.
  CLEAR : l_status, l_desc.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab19 INTO gr_outtab19.
    IF gr_outtab19-status NE l_status.
      l_status = gr_outtab19-status.
      PERFORM get_domain_desc  USING 'ZIM_MC_STATUS'
                                     l_status
                            CHANGING l_desc.
    ENDIF.
    gr_outtab19-status_desc = l_desc.
    gr_outtab19-cert_type   = 'M'.
    gr_outtab19-cert_code   = 'X'.
    MODIFY gt_outtab19 FROM gr_outtab19.
  ENDLOOP.


ENDFORM.                    " GET_MC

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  GET_MC_GAO
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM get_mc_gao.


  LOOP AT gt_outtab19 INTO gr_outtab19.
    PERFORM disable_mc_gao_fields.
    MODIFY gt_outtab19 FROM gr_outtab19.
  ENDLOOP.

ENDFORM.                    " GET_MC_GAO


*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  GET_CAPACITY
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM get_capacity.

  DATA: l_category         TYPE zim_in_cap-category,
         l_status           TYPE zim_in_cap-status,
         l_desc(60)         TYPE c,
         l_doc_unique       TYPE zim_in_doc_in-doc_unique.

  REFRESH gt_outtab19a.
  SELECT * FROM zim_in_cap
         INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE gt_outtab19a
           WHERE pernr     = zim_in_mast-pernr
             AND incident  = zim_in_mast-incident.

  SORT gt_outtab19a BY category.
  CLEAR : l_category, l_desc.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab19a INTO gr_outtab19.
    IF gr_outtab19-category NE l_category.
      l_category = gr_outtab19-category.
      PERFORM get_domain_desc  USING 'ZIM_CAP_CATEGORY'
                                     l_category
                            CHANGING l_desc.
    ENDIF.
    gr_outtab19-cat_desc = l_desc.
    gr_outtab19-mc_sent  = gr_outtab19-cap_sent.
    IF w_3100_action NE 'DISP' AND
       w_2200_action NE 'DISP' AND
       w_3600_action NE 'DISP'.
      gr_outtab19-upd_icon = icon_change.
    ELSE.
      gr_outtab19-upd_icon = icon_display.
    ENDIF.
    IF w_4001_action IS NOT INITIAL.
      gr_outtab19-upd_icon = icon_display.
    ENDIF.

    gr_outtab19-copy_icon  = icon_create_copy.
    IF gr_outtab19-status NE 'P' AND
       gr_outtab19-inactive IS INITIAL.
      gr_outtab19-gen_icon   = icon_workflow_doc_create.
    ENDIF.
    gr_outtab19-imp_icon   = icon_import.

    CONCATENATE 'CAP' gr_outtab19-seq_no
                INTO l_doc_unique.
    SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_in_doc_in
           WHERE pernr      = gr_outtab19-pernr
             AND incident   = gr_outtab19-incident
             AND doc_unique = l_doc_unique.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      gr_outtab19-doc_unique = l_doc_unique.
      gr_outtab19-doc_icon   = icon_text_act.
      gr_outtab19-email_icon = icon_mail.
    ENDIF.

    MODIFY gt_outtab19a FROM gr_outtab19.
  ENDLOOP.

  SORT gt_outtab19a BY status.
  CLEAR : l_status, l_desc.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab19a INTO gr_outtab19.
    IF gr_outtab19-status NE l_status.
      l_status = gr_outtab19-status.
      PERFORM get_domain_desc  USING 'ZIM_CAP_STATUS'
                                     l_status
                            CHANGING l_desc.
    ENDIF.
    gr_outtab19-status_desc = l_desc.
    gr_outtab19-cert_type   = 'C'.
    MODIFY gt_outtab19a FROM gr_outtab19.
  ENDLOOP.

  LOOP AT gt_outtab19a INTO gr_outtab19.
    APPEND gr_outtab19 TO gt_outtab19.
  ENDLOOP.


  CLEAR w_next_mc_seq_no.
  SORT gt_outtab19 BY seq_no.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab19 INTO gr_outtab19.
    w_next_mc_seq_no = gr_outtab19-seq_no.

* Retrieve the first line of the diagnosis
    CONCATENATE 'CAPDIAG' gr_outtab19-seq_no INTO w_notes_key.
    PERFORM get_notes TABLES gt_outtxt12
                             gt_outtxt12_old
                      USING  w_notes_key
                             'DISP'.
    READ TABLE gt_outtxt12 INTO gr_outtxt2 INDEX 1.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      gr_outtab19-diagnosis = gr_outtxt2.
      MODIFY gt_outtab19 FROM gr_outtab19.
    ENDIF.

  ENDLOOP.
  ADD 1 TO w_next_mc_seq_no.

  SORT gt_outtab19 BY mc_date   DESCENDING
                      date_from DESCENDING
                      date_to   DESCENDING.

ENDFORM.                    " GET_CAPACITY

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  GET_MC_BEN
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM get_mc_ben.

  DATA: l_category         TYPE zim_in_cert-category,
         l_status           TYPE zim_in_cert-status,
         l_mc_ben           TYPE zim_mc_ben_status,
         l_orgeh            TYPE pa0001-orgeh,
         l_orgeh_desc       TYPE hrp1000-stext,
         l_desc(60)         TYPE c,
         l_pernr            TYPE pa0002-pernr,
         l_incident         TYPE zim_in_cert-incident,
         l_unsworn_claim    TYPE zim_in_cm-unsworn_claim.

  REFRESH gt_outtab31.
  SELECT * FROM zim_in_cert_ben
         INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE gt_outtab31.

  IF w_gao IS NOT INITIAL.
    SORT gt_outtab31 BY pernr incident.
    CLEAR : l_pernr, l_incident.
    LOOP AT gt_outtab31 INTO gr_outtab31.
      IF gr_outtab31-pernr NE l_pernr OR
         gr_outtab31-incident NE l_incident.
        SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_in_mast
               WHERE pernr    = gr_outtab31-pernr
                 AND incident = gr_outtab31-incident.
        IF sy-subrc NE 0.
          CLEAR zim_in_mast.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.
      l_pernr    = gr_outtab31-pernr.
      l_incident = gr_outtab31-incident.
      IF zim_in_mast-gao NE w_gao.
        DELETE gt_outtab31.
        CONTINUE.
      ENDIF.
*     Only include sworn claims.
      CLEAR l_unsworn_claim.
      SELECT SINGLE unsworn_claim FROM zim_in_cm
             INTO l_unsworn_claim
             WHERE pernr    EQ gr_outtab31-pernr
               AND incident EQ gr_outtab31-incident.
      IF sy-subrc NE 0.
        zcl_hr_utilities=>is_person_sworn_unsworn(
          EXPORTING
            i_pernr   = gr_outtab31-pernr
          IMPORTING
            e_unsworn = l_unsworn_claim ).
      ENDIF.
      IF l_unsworn_claim EQ abap_true.
        DELETE gt_outtab31.
        CONTINUE.
      ENDIF.
    ENDLOOP.
  ENDIF.

  IF zim_screen-orgeh IS NOT INITIAL.
    PERFORM get_dependents_org.
  ENDIF.

  LOOP AT gt_outtab31 INTO gr_outtab31.
    PERFORM get_emp_orgeh USING    gr_outtab31-pernr
                          CHANGING gr_outtab31-orgeh.
    IF zim_screen-orgeh IS NOT INITIAL.
      IF gr_outtab31-orgeh NOT IN r_orgeh.
        DELETE gt_outtab31.
        CONTINUE.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
    MODIFY gt_outtab31 FROM gr_outtab31.
  ENDLOOP.

  SORT gt_outtab31 BY orgeh.
  CLEAR : l_orgeh, l_orgeh_desc.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab31 INTO gr_outtab31.
    IF gr_outtab31-orgeh NE l_orgeh.
      l_orgeh = gr_outtab31-orgeh.
      PERFORM get_objtxt  USING    l_orgeh
                                   'O'
                          CHANGING l_orgeh_desc
                                   w_short.
    ENDIF.
    gr_outtab31-orgeh_stext = l_orgeh_desc.
    MODIFY gt_outtab31 FROM gr_outtab31.
  ENDLOOP.

  LOOP AT gt_outtab31 INTO gr_outtab31.
    SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_in_cert
           WHERE pernr    = gr_outtab31-pernr
             AND incident = gr_outtab31-incident
             AND seq_no   = gr_outtab31-seq_no.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      MOVE-CORRESPONDING zim_in_cert TO gr_outtab31.
      gr_outtab31-cert_type = 'M'.
      MODIFY gt_outtab31 FROM gr_outtab31.
    ELSE.
      SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_in_cap
             WHERE pernr    = gr_outtab31-pernr
               AND incident = gr_outtab31-incident
               AND seq_no   = gr_outtab31-seq_no.
      IF sy-subrc = 0.
        MOVE-CORRESPONDING zim_in_cap TO gr_outtab31.
        gr_outtab31-cert_type = 'C'.
        MODIFY gt_outtab31 FROM gr_outtab31.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
  ENDLOOP.

  LOOP AT gt_outtab31 INTO gr_outtab31.
    SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_in_cm
           WHERE pernr    = gr_outtab31-pernr
             AND incident = gr_outtab31-incident.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      IF zim_in_cm-sect_40_ext = abap_true.
        DELETE gt_outtab31.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
  ENDLOOP.

  CLEAR pa0002.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab31 INTO gr_outtab31.
    IF gr_outtab31-pernr NE pa0002-pernr.
      SELECT SINGLE * FROM pa0002
             WHERE pernr    = gr_outtab31-pernr
               AND begda   LE sy-datum
               AND endda   GE sy-datum.
      IF sy-subrc NE 0.
        CLEAR pa0002.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
    gr_outtab31-nachn = pa0002-nachn.
    gr_outtab31-vorna = pa0002-vorna.

    SELECT SINGLE * FROM pa9082
           WHERE pernr = gr_outtab31-pernr
             AND casno = gr_outtab31-incident.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      SELECT SINGLE * FROM pa9076
             WHERE pernr = pa9082-pernr
               AND objps = pa9082-objps.
      IF sy-subrc = 0.
        gr_outtab31-wclmn  = pa9076-wclmn.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.

    MODIFY gt_outtab31 FROM gr_outtab31.
  ENDLOOP.


  SORT gt_outtab31 BY category.
  CLEAR : l_category, l_desc.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab31 INTO gr_outtab31
    WHERE cert_type = 'M'.
    IF gr_outtab31-category NE l_category.
      l_category = gr_outtab31-category.
      PERFORM get_domain_desc  USING 'ZIM_MC_CATEGORY'
                                     l_category
                            CHANGING l_desc.
    ENDIF.
    gr_outtab31-cat_desc = l_desc.
    gr_outtab31-del_icon = icon_delete.
    gr_outtab31-gen_icon  = icon_workflow_doc_create.
    MODIFY gt_outtab31 FROM gr_outtab31.
  ENDLOOP.

  CLEAR : l_category, l_desc.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab31 INTO gr_outtab31
    WHERE cert_type = 'C'.
    IF gr_outtab31-category NE l_category.
      l_category = gr_outtab31-category.
      PERFORM get_domain_desc  USING 'ZIM_CAP_CATEGORY'
                                     l_category
                            CHANGING l_desc.
    ENDIF.
    gr_outtab31-cat_desc = l_desc.
    gr_outtab31-del_icon = icon_delete.
    gr_outtab31-gen_icon  = icon_workflow_doc_create.
    MODIFY gt_outtab31 FROM gr_outtab31.
  ENDLOOP.

  SORT gt_outtab31 BY status.
  CLEAR : l_status, l_desc.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab31 INTO gr_outtab31
    WHERE cert_type = 'M'.
    IF gr_outtab31-status NE l_status.
      l_status = gr_outtab31-status.
      PERFORM get_domain_desc  USING 'ZIM_MC_STATUS'
                                     l_status
                            CHANGING l_desc.
    ENDIF.
    gr_outtab31-status_desc = l_desc.
    MODIFY gt_outtab31 FROM gr_outtab31.
  ENDLOOP.

  CLEAR : l_status, l_desc.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab31 INTO gr_outtab31
    WHERE cert_type = 'C'.
    IF gr_outtab31-status NE l_status.
      l_status = gr_outtab31-status.
      PERFORM get_domain_desc  USING 'ZIM_CAP_STATUS'
                                     l_status
                            CHANGING l_desc.
    ENDIF.
    gr_outtab31-status_desc = l_desc.
    MODIFY gt_outtab31 FROM gr_outtab31.
  ENDLOOP.

  SORT gt_outtab31 BY mc_ben_status.
  CLEAR : l_mc_ben, l_desc.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab31 INTO gr_outtab31.
    IF gr_outtab31-mc_ben_status NE l_mc_ben.
      l_status = gr_outtab31-mc_ben_status.
      PERFORM get_domain_desc  USING 'ZIM_MC_BEN_STATUS'
                                     l_status
                            CHANGING l_desc.
    ENDIF.
    gr_outtab31-ben_desc = l_desc.
    MODIFY gt_outtab31 FROM gr_outtab31.
  ENDLOOP.

  IF sy-tcode = 'ZWC_GAO'.

    LOOP AT gt_outtab31 INTO gr_outtab31.

      IF gr_outtab31-mc_ben_status NE 'N' AND gr_outtab31-mc_ben_status NE 'U'.
        DELETE gt_outtab31 INDEX sy-tabix.
      ENDIF.


    ENDLOOP.

  ENDIF.

  SORT gt_outtab31 BY pernr
                      date_from
                      date_to.



ENDFORM.                    " GET_MC_BEN

*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  GET_MC_BEN_EMP
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*--------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM get_mc_ben_emp.

  DATA: l_category         TYPE zim_in_cert-category,
         l_status           TYPE zim_in_cert-status,
         l_mc_ben           TYPE zim_mc_ben_status,
         l_orgeh            TYPE pa0001-orgeh,
         l_orgeh_desc       TYPE hrp1000-stext,
         l_desc(60)         TYPE c,
         l_pernr            TYPE pa0002-pernr,
         l_incident         TYPE zim_in_cert-incident.

  REFRESH gt_outtab31.
  SELECT * FROM zim_in_cert_ben
         INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE gt_outtab31
         WHERE pernr    = zim_in_mast-pernr
           AND incident = zim_in_mast-incident.

  LOOP AT gt_outtab31 INTO gr_outtab31.
    SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_in_cert
           WHERE pernr    = gr_outtab31-pernr
             AND incident = gr_outtab31-incident
             AND seq_no   = gr_outtab31-seq_no.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      MOVE-CORRESPONDING zim_in_cert TO gr_outtab31.
      gr_outtab31-cert_type = 'M'.
      MODIFY gt_outtab31 FROM gr_outtab31.
    ELSE.
      SELECT SINGLE * FROM zim_in_cap
             WHERE pernr    = gr_outtab31-pernr
               AND incident = gr_outtab31-incident
               AND seq_no   = gr_outtab31-seq_no.
      IF sy-subrc = 0.
        MOVE-CORRESPONDING zim_in_cap TO gr_outtab31.
        gr_outtab31-cert_type = 'C'.
        MODIFY gt_outtab31 FROM gr_outtab31.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
  ENDLOOP.

  SORT gt_outtab31 BY category.
  CLEAR : l_category, l_desc.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab31 INTO gr_outtab31
       WHERE cert_type = 'M'.
    IF gr_outtab31-category NE l_category.
      l_category = gr_outtab31-category.
      PERFORM get_domain_desc  USING 'ZIM_MC_CATEGORY'
                                     l_category
                            CHANGING l_desc.
    ENDIF.
    gr_outtab31-cat_desc = l_desc.
    gr_outtab31-del_icon = icon_delete.
    gr_outtab31-gen_icon = icon_workflow_doc_create.
    MODIFY gt_outtab31 FROM gr_outtab31.
  ENDLOOP.

  CLEAR : l_category, l_desc.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab31 INTO gr_outtab31
       WHERE cert_type = 'C'.
    IF gr_outtab31-category NE l_category.
      l_category = gr_outtab31-category.
      PERFORM get_domain_desc  USING 'ZIM_CAP_CATEGORY'
                                     l_category
                            CHANGING l_desc.
    ENDIF.
    gr_outtab31-cat_desc = l_desc.
    gr_outtab31-del_icon = icon_delete.
    gr_outtab31-gen_icon = icon_workflow_doc_create.
    MODIFY gt_outtab31 FROM gr_outtab31.
  ENDLOOP.

  SORT gt_outtab31 BY status.
  CLEAR : l_status, l_desc.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab31 INTO gr_outtab31
       WHERE cert_type = 'M'.
    IF gr_outtab31-status NE l_status.
      l_status = gr_outtab31-status.
      PERFORM get_domain_desc  USING 'ZIM_MC_STATUS'
                                     l_status
                            CHANGING l_desc.
    ENDIF.
    gr_outtab31-status_desc = l_desc.
    MODIFY gt_outtab31 FROM gr_outtab31.
  ENDLOOP.

  CLEAR : l_status, l_desc.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab31 INTO gr_outtab31
       WHERE cert_type = 'C'.
    IF gr_outtab31-status NE l_status.
      l_status = gr_outtab31-status.
      PERFORM get_domain_desc  USING 'ZIM_CAP_STATUS'
                                     l_status
                            CHANGING l_desc.
    ENDIF.
    gr_outtab31-status_desc = l_desc.
    MODIFY gt_outtab31 FROM gr_outtab31.
  ENDLOOP.

  SORT gt_outtab31 BY mc_ben_status.
  CLEAR : l_mc_ben, l_desc.
  LOOP AT gt_outtab31 INTO gr_outtab31.
    IF gr_outtab31-mc_ben_status NE l_mc_ben.
      l_status = gr_outtab31-mc_ben_status.
      PERFORM get_domain_desc  USING 'ZIM_MC_BEN_STATUS'
                                     l_status
                            CHANGING l_desc.
    ENDIF.
    gr_outtab31-ben_desc = l_desc.
    MODIFY gt_outtab31 FROM gr_outtab31.
  ENDLOOP.

  SORT gt_outtab31 BY date_from
                      date_to.

  IF sy-tcode = 'ZWC_GAO'.

    LOOP AT gt_outtab31 INTO gr_outtab31.

      IF gr_outtab31-mc_ben_status NE 'N' AND gr_outtab31-mc_ben_status NE 'U'.
        DELETE gt_outtab31 INDEX sy-tabix.
      ENDIF.


    ENDLOOP.

  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.                    " GET_MC_BEN_EMP
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  GET_BP_INCIDENT
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM get_bp_incident using p_pernr
                           p_incident.

*
*  SELECT SINGLE *
*    FROM zim_inc_p902
*    INTO rec_bp_incident
*   WHERE pernr    = p_pernr
*     AND incident = p_incident.
*
*
*  lv_sys_id = rec_bp_incident-what_first_aid_given.
*
*  SELECT first_aid
*   UP TO 1 ROWS
*   FROM zpbp_first_aid
*   INTO w_bp_first_aid
*  WHERE sys_id EQ lv_sys_id.
*  ENDSELECT.
*
*  lv_sys_id = rec_bp_incident-hazardsubclass.
*
*  SELECT subclass
*   UP TO 1 ROWS
*   FROM zpbp_haz_sub_cl
*   INTO w_bp_hazard
*  WHERE sys_id EQ lv_sys_id.
*  ENDSELECT.
*
*  lv_sys_id = rec_bp_incident-where_did_the_incident_happen.
*
*  SELECT doing
*   UP TO 1 ROWS
*   FROM zpbp_what_doing
*   INTO w_bp_doing
*  WHERE sys_id EQ lv_sys_id.
*  ENDSELECT.
*
*  lv_sys_id = rec_bp_incident-current_fitness.
*
*  SELECT fitness
*   UP TO 1 ROWS
*   FROM zpbp_curr_fitnes
*   INTO w_bp_fitness
*  WHERE sys_id EQ lv_sys_id.
*  ENDSELECT.
*
*  lv_sys_id = rec_bp_incident-main_exposure.
*
*  SELECT substance
*   UP TO 1 ROWS
*   FROM zpbp_haz_sub_exp
*   INTO w_bp_main_exp
*  WHERE sys_id EQ lv_sys_id.
*  ENDSELECT.
*
*  lv_sys_id = rec_bp_incident-other_exposure.
*
*  SELECT substance
*   UP TO 1 ROWS
*   FROM zpbp_haz_sub_exp
*   INTO w_bp_other_exp
*  WHERE sys_id EQ lv_sys_id.
*  ENDSELECT.
*

ENDFORM.                    " GET_BP_INCIDENT
